Method: 提出EditVid框架，包含三个关键组件：1) 稀疏因果记忆用于局部一致性；2) 基于对应性的后注意力令牌注入用于长程身份保持；3) 软潜在混合用于编辑局部性。该训练自由框架统一支持指令引导和参考引导编辑。

Result: 在FiVE基准上EditVid达到78.16的FiVE-Acc分数，远超最强训练自由基线的58.95；在IVEBench上取得有竞争力的结果。用户研究显示EditVid在与7种对比方法中整体偏好占51.8%。

Conclusion: EditVid证明了无需训练即可在统一框架中有效支持多种视频编辑范式（风格迁移、属性修改、物体插入、局部编辑、主体替换），并在质量与用户偏好上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Video editing spans diverse editing paradigms, yet achieving high-quality instruction-guided and subject-guided editing within a single unified framework remains challenging. We introduce EditVid, a training-free framework combining sparse causal memory for local coherence, correspondence-based post-attention token injection for long-range identity preservation, and soft latent blending for edit locality. The same framework supports instruction-guided and reference-guided edits, including style transfer, attribute modification, object insertion, part-level editing, and subject replacement. On FiVE, EditVid achieves 78.16 FiVE-Acc, compared with 58.95 for the strongest evaluated training-free baseline, while obtaining competitive results on IVEBench. A user study further shows a 51.8\% overall preference for EditVid over 7 competing methods.

</details>


### [98] [Puffin-World: Scaling a Unified Multimodal Model with Native 3D World States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04196)
*Kang Liao,Yihang Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Linyi Jin,Size Wu,Chunyu Lin,Yao Zhao,Fei Wang,Wei Li,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Puffin-World，一个统一的模态架构，能够在不依赖外部离线模块的情况下，同时整合物理理解、空间模拟和3D世界生成与重建。该框架联合建模物理（重力场与纬度）、几何（深度）和外观（图像）三种原生世界状态，并通过统一的Omni-Camera表示支持多样任务和灵活运动。文中还提出了跨未来帧传播物理动力学的策略，同时将外观与几何耦合在单一生成过程中，实现联合合成未来视图并重建其底层几何。此外，为扩展至复杂场景，作者构建了包含1500万视觉-语言-相机三元组和100万条轨迹的Puffin-16M数据集，并已开源代码、模型和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D世界生成与重建方法往往依赖多个外部离线模块，缺乏对物理规律和空间几何的联合建模能力，难以实现物理一致且视觉稳定的世界生成，也无法支持需要跨任务协同的闭环应用。

Method: 提出统一多模态架构Puffin-World，联合建模物理（重力场和纬度）、几何（深度）和外观（图像）三种原生世界状态；引入统一Omni-Camera表示以支持多样任务和灵活运动；提出跨未来帧传播物理动力学的策略；将外观与几何耦合在单一生成过程中，联合合成未来视图并重建其几何；构建Puffin-16M数据集用于大规模训练。

Result: Puffin-World实现了物理一致和视觉稳定的世界生成，支持模仿和自校准世界探索等交叉闭环应用；通过Puffin-16M数据集（1500万视觉-语言-相机三元组和100万轨迹）扩展到复杂场景，并已开源代码、模型和数据集。

Conclusion: Puffin-World通过统一架构将物理理解、空间模拟和3D世界生成集成于一体，消除了对外部离线模块的依赖，为多任务协同的世界生成与重建提供了新的范式，并已开源相关资源以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: We propose Puffin-World, a unified multimodal architecture that integrates physical understanding, spatial simulation, and 3D world generation and reconstruction without relying on external offline modules. To reliably construct and interact with 3D worlds, our framework jointly models three native world states: physics (gravity field and latitude), geometry (depth), and appearance (image), together with a unified Omni-Camera representation that supports diverse tasks and flexible motions. Beyond modeling these states, we introduce a strategy for propagating physical dynamics across future frames. By grounding absolute camera properties in the real world, Puffin-World enables physically consistent and visually stable world generation. We further couple appearance and geometry within a single generative process, jointly synthesizing each future view and reconstructing its underlying geometry. This unified paradigm enables interleaved closed-loop applications requiring synergy across multiple tasks, including mimic and self-calibrated world exploration. To scale Puffin-World to complex scenarios, we construct Puffin-16M, comprising 15 million vision-language-camera triplets and 1 million trajectories featuring various and challenging motions. To foster further research in this area, we released the code, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [99] [Principia: Relational Physics Tests for Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04200)
*Varun Varma Thozhiyoor,Shivam Tripathi,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Principia基准，通过成对物体的关系一致性来评估视频模型中的牛顿物理推理能力，该评估方法不依赖帧率、物体尺度或相机校准等易混淆因素。实验发现六个最先进的视频生成模型在Principia上均得分不超过0.42，而视觉-语言模型检测物理违例的准确率也普遍接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为传统视频物理推理评估依赖绝对运动测量（帧率、物体尺度、相机校准），这些参数在生成视频中常常模糊或不可用。当同一场景中两个物体遵循相同物理定律时，其运动满足可预测的关系，且这种关系与校准无关，因此可以以此构建更可靠的评估方式。

Method: 引入Principia基准，涵盖重力、弹性恢复、摩擦、转动惯量、抛体运动、动量、摆和质量-弹簧振荡等八种现象，覆盖平移、旋转、碰撞和振荡动力学，使用受控协议下录制的真实场景。同时提出与校准无关的一致性评分，直接在图像空间中量化物理违例程度。

Result: 数千次生成测试中，六个最先进的视频生成模型在Principia上均未超过0.42分，尽管它们在VBench上得分均约0.8。视觉-语言模型检测关系物理违例的能力也有限，最佳模型仅达67%准确率，大多数模型接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 现有视频生成模型和视觉-语言模型在物理关系一致性推理方面表现不佳，说明仅靠表面视觉质量指标（如VBench）不足以评估模型的物理理解能力，需要像Principia这样的关系一致性基准来更准确地衡量物理推理水平。

Abstract: Evaluating physical reasoning in video models is difficult because absolute motion measurements depend on frame rate, object scale, and camera calibration, all of which are often ambiguous or unavailable in generated video. We propose a different approach. When two objects in the same scene obey the same physical law, their motions must satisfy predictable relationships, and these relationships hold independent of calibration. We introduce Principia, a benchmark that evaluates Newtonian physics through relational consistency between paired objects. Principia spans eight phenomena - gravity, restitution, friction, rotational inertia, projectile motion, momentum, pendulum, and mass-spring oscillation - across translational, rotational, collisional, and oscillatory dynamics, using real-world scenes recorded under controlled protocols. We also introduce a calibration-independent consistency score that quantifies physical violation directly in image space. Across thousands of generations from six state-of-the-art video generators, no model exceeds 0.42 on Principia despite all scoring around 0.8 on VBench. Vision-language models are evaluated on their ability to detect relational physics violations, with the best model achieving only 67% accuracy and most performing near chance level.

</details>


### [100] [Scal3R: Learning Efficient Multi-Relative Pose Query for Scalable Online 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04201)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Yang-Che Sun,Cheng Sun,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Scal3R，一种用于在线3D重建的模型，通过将在线重建重构为多参考相对位姿查询，并使用轻量级可学习token注入到完全冻结的骨干网络中，配合在线位姿图优化与闭环检测来抑制长序列视频中的漂移累积。相比在线基线，在KITTI上平均ATE降低超过60%，仅需单GPU 8小时即可收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D重建模型在长视频上表现差，因为相对于固定首帧锚点回归位姿会导致外推远远超出训练分布，小的漂移不断累积并放大为严重的几何塌陷。

Method: 1) 将在线重建重构为多参考相对位姿查询；2) 使用仅占约1%参数的轻量级可学习token，通过非对称注意力注入到完全冻结的骨干网络中；3) 位姿相对于多个过去关键帧进行查询；4) 引入带闭环的在线位姿图优化系统以抑制长程漂移。

Result: 在KITTI上相比在线基线平均ATE降低超过60%；在Virtual KITTI、Sintel、TUM-Dynamic、ScanNet和7-Scenes上均达到最先进性能；在单GPU上8小时即可收敛。

Conclusion: 观察到的现象是：虽然全局位姿头会崩溃，但逐帧深度在失败过程中保持稳定，骨干网络的局部几何保持完整。基于这种解耦现象，通过解耦全局位姿估计与局部几何建模，多参考查询策略与位姿图优化有效解决了长视频在线重建的漂移问题。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction models perform poorly on long videos. This happens because regressing poses relative to a fixed first-frame anchor forces extrapolation far beyond the training distribution. Small drifts accumulate and amplify into significant geometric collapse. However, we observe that per-frame depth remains stable throughout this failure. The backbone's local geometry remains intact; only the global pose head breaks down. Motivated by this decoupling, we introduce Scal3R. This approach reformulates online reconstruction as multi-reference relative pose querying. We use lightweight learnable tokens, which make up about ~1% of the parameters, and inject them into a completely frozen backbone via asymmetric attention. This setup queries poses relative to multiple past keyframes. An online pose-graph optimization system with loop closure suppresses long-range drift. Scal3R reaches convergence in 8 hours on a single GPU. It reduces the average ATE by over 60% on KITTI compared to the online baseline. It also achieves state-of-the-art performance across Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, and 7-Scenes. Project page: https://linjohnss.github.io/scal3r/

</details>


### [101] [TokenMatch: 3D Mesh Correspondence Transformer with Curvature-Guided Tokenisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04202)
*Adeela Islam,Zorah Lähner,Vittorio Murino,Vladislav Golyanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenMatch，一种基于Transformer的统一3D形状对应估计模型。该模型仅在BeCoS部分到部分匹配数据集上训练，无需微调即可泛化到完整形状匹配任务。通过曲率引导的自适应网格token化，结合自注意力和交叉注意力机制学习点级和面片级对应关系，在多个标准基准上取得领先性能，且推理速度低于1秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的3D形状对应方法在部分观测和强非等距变形下仍面临挑战。基于学习的方法依赖手工设计描述子或模板表示，而基于功能映射的生成模型推理成本高、可解释性有限、对部分形状泛化能力差。作者希望解决这些局限，提出一种统一的高效、可泛化且可解释的对应估计方法。

Method: TokenMatch采用前馈Transformer架构，核心创新在于利用曲率引导将网格自适应地token化为面片，使得网络能够学习形状特有的几何描述子。模型通过自注意力机制学习面片级和点级关系，通过交叉注意力机制学习形状对之间的密集对应关系。模型仅在BeCoS数据集上训练，却能在无需重训练或微调的情况下泛化到完整形状匹配任务。

Result: TokenMatch在CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE和SHREC'19等多个标准基准上进行评估，在平均测地误差和交并比指标上，大部分情况下优于现有的部分和完整形状匹配方法，同时推理速度更快，可在亚秒级时间内完成。

Conclusion: TokenMatch证明了曲率引导的自适应token化与Transformer注意力机制的结合，能够有效统一处理部分和完整3D形状匹配问题。得益于在具有挑战性的BeCoS数据集上的训练，模型展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力，无需针对完整形状匹配进行任何微调，同时保持了高效的计算性能。

Abstract: While data-driven 3D shape correspondence estimation has recently seen substantial progress, robust matching under partial observations and strong non-isometric deformations remains challenging. Existing learning-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted descriptors or template-based representations, whereas recent generative models over functional maps suffer from high inference cost, limited interpretability, and poor generalisation to partial shapes. In response to these limitations, this paper introduces TokenMatch, a new transformer-based unified model for estimating 3D shape correspondences. Our feed-forward approach trained exclusively on BeCoS, a challenging non-isometric partial-to-partial shape-matching dataset, can generalise to matching full shapes without retraining or fine-tuning. TokenMatch uses self- and cross-attention mechanisms to efficiently learn patch-level and point-level relations as well as dense correspondences between shape pairs. Our core insight is that meshes can be adaptively tokenised into patches using shape curvature guidance, enabling effective learning of shape-specific geometric descriptors for correspondence estimation. We evaluate TokenMatch on standard benchmarks for partial and full shape matching, including CP2P, PSMAL, BeCoS, FAUST, SCAPE, and SHREC'19. Our method achieves consistently high performance, in most cases outperforming existing methods for partial and full shape matching in the mean geodesic error and intersection-over-union metrics, while also running faster at sub-second inference speeds.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Learning Visual State Tracking in Videos Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04203)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Wenshuai Zhao,Mohammad Hassan Vali,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S^3T（基于时间的自监督自蒸馏）框架，这是首个完全自包含的连续视频状态跟踪方法。通过将时间采样密度作为特权信息，让稀疏视图的学生模型匹配密集视图教师模型的下一token分布，实现无需标签、无需独立教师、无推理开销的自监督训练，在VSTAT等多种基准上显著提升状态跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续视频状态跟踪对于视频理解至关重要，但现有方法通常依赖外部标注、独立教师模型或奖励信号，限制了可扩展性。作者假设时间采样密度可作为特权信息——对同一片段更密集的采样能更准确地恢复运行状态，并希望利用这一假设构建完全自包含的训练框架，使模型无需外部信号即能自我提升状态跟踪能力。

Method: S^3T将视频时间采样密度视为特权信息。核心思路是：对同一剪辑，密集采样的视图作为教师，因其能更准确地恢复运行状态；稀疏采样的学生模型共享相同权重，通过学习匹配教师的下一token分布来进行自蒸馏。模型自生成目标，无需外部标签。方法包括三个层次：单一模型训练、模型汤（souping）集成，以及进一步结合视觉编码器适配。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S^3T使VSTAT准确率提升+1.74（单模型）、+2.38（模型汤）、+2.70（结合视觉编码器适配）。此前自进化方法对状态跟踪几乎没有改善。从无标注合成视频片段学到的能力可迁移到真实视频，在VSTAT-YouTube状态跟踪问题上提升+7.95，在MVBench Action Count上提升+4.50。

Conclusion: S^3T验证了时间采样密度作为特权信息的有效性，证明利用自蒸馏和时间稠密度差异就能在完全无标注条件下提升视频状态跟踪能力，且学到的能力具有良好的跨领域迁移性（从合成到真实视频），代表了一种可扩展、自包含的视频状态跟踪训练范式。

Abstract: We introduce S$^3$T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for continuous video state tracking. Our method treats temporal sampling density as privileged information, based on the hypothesis that a denser view of the same clip recovers the running state more accurately. This view serves as the teacher, while a sparse-view student with the same weights learns to match its next-token distribution. The model generates its own target, so training requires no labels, separate teacher, or reward signal, and adds no inference cost. On LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, S$^3$T improves VSTAT accuracy by $+1.74$ as a single model, $+2.38$ with souping, and $+2.70$ with additional vision-encoder adaptation, while prior self-evolving methods leave state tracking largely unchanged. The capability learned from unlabeled synthetic clips transfers to real videos, improving performance by $+7.95$ on VSTAT-YouTube state-tracking questions and $+4.50$ on MVBench Action Count.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的解嵌入矩阵中存在一个编码训练语料unigram分布的"无知方向"，作为模型不确定时的贝叶斯先验。通过将最终预测状态投影到此方向，可得到每个token的先验载荷因子λ，该λ与上下文信息量呈反比，并可将预测状态分解为对应温度化贝叶斯更新的两个正交分量。该方向具有因果活性，可通过调整λ引导预测朝向或背离unigram先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在信息不足时的预测机制至关重要。作者旨在揭示当模型面对极少线索时，其预测背后的几何结构基础——即unembedding矩阵中编码的训练语料统计先验，为解释模型不确定性行为提供统一的几何-概率框架。

Method: 作者在Llama、Qwen、Gemma和Pythia四个模型家族（0.4B至405B参数）中定位了解嵌入矩阵中编码unigram分布的方向"无知方向"。利用投影操作定义先验载荷因子λ，并通过正交分解将预测状态拆分为unigram先验与上下文似然两项。形式上论证该分解对应温度化贝叶斯更新的两个因子，并通过在最终预测状态上人为调整λ进行因果干预验证。

Result: "无知方向"在全部四个模型家族中普遍存在；先验载荷因子λ随上下文信息量增加而单调下降；该分解与温度化贝叶斯更新精确对应，使λ可在不同模型规模与家族间进行有意义的比较，且大规模模型在高上下文条件下先验依赖更低；因果实验表明调整λ可有效将预测引导向或远离unigram先验。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测行为可通过"无知方向"这一几何结构获得统一的概率化理解：模型在信息不足时自然回落到训练语料的统计先验，且这一依赖程度可通过一个可解释、可比、可干预的标量λ来量化和控制。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [104] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重铸"(equation recast)方法，将参数化的算子学习重新表述为学习单一规范算子，通过从控制方程中解析导出参数引起的算子变化并将其吸收为有效源项，实现在新参数区域上的零样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化PDE模型需要在广泛的输入函数和物理参数范围内进行大量数据覆盖，且模型可能在训练分布之外静默失效，缺乏可靠性保障。

Method: 方程重铸将参数化算子学习转为单一规范算子学习，通过解析方式从控制方程中导出参数诱导的算子变化并将其吸收为有效源项；利用规范表示整合稀疏异质数据集；以重铸迭代的收敛性丧失作为内部失败预警信号。

Result: 在多参数、非线性和奇异PDE设定中支持外推；在核聚变高保真托卡马克模拟中，通过规范域映射在单次联合训练的算子内统一了四种设备几何的电子温度数据。

Conclusion: 方程重铸为实现可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了一条路径，结合了方程引导迁移、数据效率与可监控的推断过程。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [105] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述全面调研了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习领域，围绕学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个核心维度展开，提出了图数据分布场景的分类法，并系统性地识别了开放挑战和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习在单点位置进行数据收集、训练和推理的方式面临可扩展性和隐私等方面的根本性限制。尽管协作学习（如联邦学习和去中心化学习）已得到广泛研究，但大多数工作聚焦于欧几里得数据，难以捕捉许多实际应用中由图表示的关系模式。图上的协作学习机会与挑战在很大程度上尚未被充分探索，亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 本文通过综述方式展开研究：首先回顾协作学习在欧几里得数据上的基础原理，沿学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个维度进行组织；然后扩展至图结构数据，提出图分布场景的分类法，刻画相关的统计异质性，并制定标准化的问题表述和算法框架；最后系统识别开放挑战与研究方向。

Result: 形成了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习完整综述，提出了图分布场景分类法，建立了标准化问题表述和算法框架，并系统总结了该新兴领域的开放挑战与未来方向。

Conclusion: 图结构数据上的协作学习是一个具有广阔前景但尚不成熟的新兴领域，本文通过全面的调研为该领域奠定了理论基础，指明了未来的研究路径。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [106] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过干预度量（RFIS和RPD）揭示RoPE Transformer中注意力头在频域上的功能组织，发现检索头与位置头之间存在一个由训练长度位置尺度界定的"全局位置带"（GPBand），并据此提出两个设计原则——位置建模应局部化、全局访问应通过位置无关的检索实现，且两者应在头粒度按层分配。基于这些原则构建了HwH混合架构（NoPE全注意力用于全局检索+线性注意力用于局部位置建模），在FA:LA比例低于1:3时仍保持语言建模和常识推理能力，显著提升检索与零样本长上下文外推性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全注意力与线性注意力的混合架构（hybrid）分配方式仍基于启发式规则，缺乏经验依据。作者希望从RoPE Transformer头部级功能组织的角度，找到一种有证据支撑的混合架构设计依据，而非盲目拼接。

Method: 提出两种干预度量：RFIS（测量每个RoPE频率对注意力分布的影响程度）和RPD（隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖）。在Qwen3系列和Llama3.1上进行测量，验证了检索头与位置头的完整分类学，边界由训练长度决定（称为GPBand）。在受控Transformer中验证了这一边界与训练长度位置尺度的关系，并据此提出两个设计原则，实例化为HwH架构：头部粒度分配NoPE全注意力做全局检索、线性注意力做局部位置建模。

Result: HwH在FA:LA比例低于1:3时依然保持强语言建模和常识推理能力，同时相比Transformer、纯LA和逐层混合基线，在检索性能上有所提升，且显著增强了零样本长上下文外推能力。消融实验验证了两个设计原则及各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 注意力的混合分配不应依赖启发式，而应基于功能上可解释的头级分类——位置建模与检索分离，且放于不同频率带宽；原则驱动的混合架构设计是构建未来基础模型的有前景方向。该分析也为零样本长度外推失败提供了潜在原因解释。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [107] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)，直接优化策略在奖励上尾部的覆盖度，即最大化超过随机奖励阈值的对数概率，使梯度更关注稀有高奖励的rollout，并可通过简单修改advantage函数兼容现有RL流程。实验表明TailRL在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化等任务中能利用稀有高奖励样本避免次优解，并在推理时随采样增加获益更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习通常优化平均奖励，但对于生成式策略，平均奖励会掩盖一个重要区别：两个策略可能具有相同的平均奖励，但产生稀有高奖励rollout的概率却截然不同。随着训练和推理阶段采样数量的增加，保留高奖励结果的概率质量变得至关重要，因此需要直接优化这种覆盖度。

Method: TailRL将连续奖励转化为一族二值成功事件（每个奖励阈值对应一个事件），并最大化超过随机选择的奖励阈值的对数概率。其梯度对稀有高奖励rollout赋予更大权重，可被解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。实现仅需对advantage函数进行简单修改，与现有强化学习管道兼容。

Result: 在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化等任务中，TailRL能够利用训练中稀有的高奖励样本避免次优解，并且产出的模型在推理时增加采样数量能获得更大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过直接优化奖励上尾部的覆盖度而非仅优化平均奖励，TailRL提供了一种简单有效的改进方案，使生成式策略更能利用稀有高奖励样本，并增强推理时的采样扩展收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [108] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）的替代模型，直接在TCAD四面体网格上预测漂移-扩散系统的三个基本物理场（静电势及电子/空穴准费米能级），结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差进行训练，实现了跨设备尺寸的泛化，并通过深度集成不确定性驱动的主动学习提升设计空间探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD有限元仿真对3D FinFET结构计算成本高昂，严重限制了多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习替代模型仅将固定设计参数映射到少数标量指标，丢弃了底层物理，且无法跨器件几何结构和族类迁移。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上以图形式操作，在每个网格节点预测静电势及电子/空穴准费米能级三个漂移-扩散基本未知量。训练目标融合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数。利用网格即图的特性实现尺寸泛化，采用深度集成的逐节点不确定度驱动主动学习循环，快速筛选候选设计并仅将最具信息量的样本送入全仿真。

Result: 在多重栅极三栅FinFET上以Sentaurus Device为基准，替代模型在每个物理场上达到亚伏特级RMSE，单设计吞吐量比全仿真高数个数量级。优势随器件尺寸增大而增强：对直接仿真难以完成的大规模多鳍阵列，推理仍能在每器件亚秒内完成，支持跨器件尺度的Pareto前沿探索。

Conclusion: 该物理信息GAT替代模型有效弥合了机器学习替代模型与全TCAD仿真之间的差距，保留了网格级物理保真度并实现了跨尺寸泛化，为超出直接TCAD扫描能力的设计空间探索提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [109] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种基于图网络的颗粒材料动力学模拟器，通过在接触边上存储交互历史（而非节点），并利用注意力消息传递和门控循环单元更新边记忆，同时使用边身份字典保持接触图变化时的记忆连续性。在2D和3D颗粒柱崩塌基准测试中，TRACE相比现有GNS和NMGNS方法将长程滚动位置误差降低31-62%，最终堆积误差降低58-89%，同时参数更少、穿透接近零，且相比MPM参考求解器分别获得12.2倍和8.9倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒材料的运动强烈依赖于颗粒间接触历史的演化和保持，而现有图模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，难以在颗粒接触不断形成、断裂和重排的过程中有效保留交互历史，导致长程模拟的物理一致性不足。

Method: TRACE的核心创新在于将交互历史直接存储在接触边上：每条边通过注意力机制的消息传递和门控循环单元维护持久记忆；引入边身份字典以在接触图动态变化时索引和保留记忆。物理结构化解码器预测颗粒间法向和切向接触力，强制满足库仑摩擦极限，并施加等大反向的内力。训练策略采用单步预训练后接自回归滚动微调。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱崩塌基准上，TRACE生成了稳定且物理一致的长期滚动模拟，较好地复现最终沉积形态和崩塌释放的动能。相比GNS和NMGNS，长程滚动位置误差降低31-62%，最终堆积误差降低58-89%，且模型参数更少，颗粒间穿透接近零。相比MPM参考求解器，2D和3D分别实现12.2倍和8.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 将接触历史直接存储在边上的设计是颗粒动力学图模拟的有效方案，它优于节点级记忆方法，在保持物理一致性的同时显著减少误差并提升计算效率，为颗粒材料的长期模拟提供了高精度、高效率的替代途径。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [110] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），从有限输入映射库中动态选择扭曲函数来修正核函数的几何失配问题，在保持GP-UCB高概率收敛保证的前提下提升样本效率，实验验证其在合成目标、超参数优化和多智能体系统设计中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-BO在昂贵黑盒优化中表现优异，但GP-UCB的收敛保证要求核函数固定。然而，当原始坐标空间与目标函数几何结构不匹配时（如对数尺度超参数或局部尖峰），固定核会严重影响样本效率。已有的输入扭曲方法能改善效率但缺乏理论保证，因此需要一个既能自适应扭曲输入几何又能保留收敛保证的框架。

Method: FLIWBO从一个有限的平滑输入映射库中按任意历史依赖规则选择扭曲映射，动态适配输入几何以加速学习。该方法在温和假设下提供高概率收敛保证，并显式给出库大小带来的√(N_ε)代价项。理论框架允许灵活的策略选择而不牺牲收敛性质。

Result: 在四个重复基准测试中（扭曲合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST超参数优化），FLIWBO-UCB在几何失配下优于原始坐标GP-UCB；能摆脱甚至击败Oracle扭曲期望改进方法的陷阱；恢复手动对数缩放的大部分收益，并在允许匹配遗憾保证的方法中领先。在20维多智能体系统设计中展示了在昂贵噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO弥合了输入扭曲提升效率与GP-UCB收敛保证之间的理论鸿沟，通过有限库的扭曲映射选择实现在不牺牲收敛保证的前提下显著提升样本效率，为几何失配场景下的黑盒优化提供了既有理论保障又具实践效能的新框架。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [111] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子海图（ENC）空间矢量数据表示为图结构，利用图神经网络（GNN）对ENC更新前后的数据对进行分类，以判断其对航行安全的影响是严重还是非严重风险，结果表明图表示方法能有效提升ENC更新分类的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ENC电子海图数据集的维护是一项挑战，尤其需要对数据对象变更按航行安全重要性进行分类。现有方法对消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示的适用性尚不明确，需要探索有效的自动化分类方法。

Method: 将新旧ENC数据集分别编码为一对图（空间对象为节点，空间与语义关系为边），将任务建模为图对分类问题，训练并评估多种GNN架构和模型配置对变更是否构成严重航行安全风险进行分类。

Result: 图表示方法提升了ENC更新的分类性能，为自动化或改进ENC维护流程提供了可扩展的方案。

Conclusion: 基于图的GNN方法能够有效改进电子海图变更分类任务，为海洋导航数据维护提供了可扩展的自动化途径。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [112] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD)，在提示词层面先验证教师模型的可靠性，再决定是使用密集的on-policy蒸馏监督还是基于验证器奖励的GRPO，从而避免错误教师带来的误导性更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统on-policy distillation (OPD)对所有提示词一视同仁地施加教师监督，没有检查教师对每个提示词的可靠性。由于reverse KL是mode-seeking的，一个自信但错误的教师会产生强烈且误导的更新。已有的分布性代理指标（如熵或师生似然一致性）只能衡量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果的正确性。

Method: TGOPD首先通过少量由验证器评分的教师探针（teacher probes）来估计每个提示词层面的教师可靠性；当可靠性检查通过时，提示词被路由到密集的OPD训练；当检查不通过时，则改用基于验证器的GRPO训练。此外，TGOPD利用异步OPD中原本空闲的教师算力进行可靠性估计，减少计算浪费。

Result: 在4B和35B规模的学生模型上，覆盖数学、代码和指令跟随任务，TGOPD在所有六个单领域设置中均优于Vanilla OPD，并在多领域训练下于两个规模均取得更高的七个基准平均得分。同时，在异步OPD中，教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%（基于4B单领域运行的实测）。

Conclusion: 在允许密集教师监督之前先在提示词层面对教师可靠性进行验证，是基于蒸馏的后训练方法中一个有效且高效的设计原则，既能提升性能，又能提高异步训练中的算力利用率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [113] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了因果基础模型（CFMs）这一新兴领域，它将基础模型范式引入因果推断领域。CFMs是通过预训练的神经网络，能够在全新数据集上利用上下文学习直接估计因果量（如平均处理效应），而无需微调或训练。文章提供了该领域的实用入门指南，涵盖了因果推断和机器学习背景知识，并附有示例代码和Jupyter笔记本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要对每个新问题定制专属流程（提出因果机制→选择估计器→训练），效率低下且缺乏泛化性。同时，机器学习领域已广泛转向基础模型范式（大规模预训练+零样本迁移），但这一范式尚未系统引入因果推断。作者希望通过引入CFMs弥合这一差距，推动因果推断走向通用化。

Method: 首先总结因果推断和机器学习的必要背景知识，然后系统阐述CFMs的核心概念与方法，包括：如何将因果量估计建模为预训练+上下文学习任务，如何设计网络架构使其能够对新数据集进行因果推断。文章采用教学性叙述方式，配合代码示例和Jupyter notebooks，提供实践导向的说明。

Result: 提供了一个覆盖CFMs领域全貌的实用教学指南，整合了从传统因果推断到基础模型范式的过渡讲解，并提供了可运行的代码和笔记本资源供读者实操学习。

Conclusion: CFMs代表了因果推断进入基础模型时代的重要方向，通过预训练和上下文学习消除逐问题定制模型的需求。这项工作为研究者提供了清晰的入门指引，有望加速CFMs领域的发展和应用普及。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [114] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench是一个基准测试框架，用于评估可解释性方法产生的内部估计器（观察者）是否足够好以指导实际干预、控制或安全任务。该框架将估计精度与动作导致的损失分开报告，并通过理论和实验证明两者缺一不可。实验表明，传统的平均精度指标（如AUROC）在电路干预、安全分诊和控制任务中可能与实际部署损失排名不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性方法越来越多地被用于指导干预（如激活操纵、电路移除、安全监控），但内部的估计器即使平均准确，也可能在选择具体动作时表现不佳。现有评估通常只关注估计精度，忽视了估计器所驱动的下游动作的实际损失，缺乏一个系统性的框架来验证估计器是否真正胜任其所服务的任务。

Method: 作者提出ObserverBench基准框架，为每个任务固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、留出案例和损失函数，并分别报告估计精度与所选动作造成的损失。通过理论分析（闭环控制中的观察者误差影响）和实验（GPT-2-small和Qwen2.5-7B上的电路干预、安全分诊、稀疏SAE读出比较等）来验证框架的有效性。

Result: 1）在电路干预任务中，成对观察者对未见效果的预测更准确，但未必能选择更好的动作；2）在安全分诊中，能完美区分违规的分数在成本不均时可能错误分配固定干预预算；3）跨多个模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it、Qwen3.5-9B APPS）AUROC与部署损失的排名可能不一致；4）稀疏SAE读出在所述Qwen面板上落后于层匹配的密集对照。

Conclusion: ObserverBench提供了固定的任务契约、可运行的基线和基于表格的提交方式，通过可解释性方法所启用的动作来评估它们。结论是评估可解释性方法不应只看估计精度，还需关注其驱动的下游动作的实际损失。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [115] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LatentDDM方法，通过在小子域上预训练神经算子，并在新场景下冻结该算子、仅训练轻量级模块来组合局部预测，从而降低神经算子在新几何、尺寸或工况下的部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在物理模拟中是快速可微的替代模型，但当域几何、尺寸或工况偏离训练分布时精度会下降。监督式适配虽可恢复精度，但即使是小规模目标集也需要昂贵的高保真模拟，部署成本高。因此需要协同设计预训练与迁移策略来降低部署成本。

Method: LatentDDM首先在小子域上预训练神经算子以预测局部物理场；对新的设定，冻结预训练算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合这些局部预测，实现跨域迁移与自适应。

Result: 在两个互补问题上评估：稳态达西流和大俯仰角气动翼型的非定常不可压缩流动。相比在全域上处理的能力匹配模型，LatentDDM在更大的达西域上使用16个目标模拟进行适配后误差降低36-56%；同时在快速俯仰翼型流动的20步场滚动预测中，无论是零样本还是少样本校准后都有改进。

Conclusion: 协同设计的局部预训练与组合层面迁移是物理基础模型的一个有前景的设计原则。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [116] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream是一个用于端侧设备LLM推理的流式推测与修正（speculate-and-refine）框架，通过利用部分GPU结果逐步优化计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，实现了GPU执行与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，在移动和嵌入式平台上相比现有系统显著降低了内存占用并提升了token生成吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧LLM推理由于模型权重远超可用DRAM而面临挑战。现有系统利用激活稀疏性并通过SSD或闪存卸载权重，但面临一个根本性的系统权衡：准确的稀疏执行决策需要最新的上下文，而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要早期预测。这导致现有设计要么串行化执行，要么产生冗余的权重获取、额外计算和较大缓存开销。

Method: LeanStream提出了一种流式推测与修正（speculate-and-refine）框架。其核心思想是利用部分GPU结果来渐进式地（progressively）精炼和调整计算、加载及缓存保留的优先级，从而在不牺牲稀疏执行准确性的前提下，实现GPU执行与存储I/O之间的细粒度重叠。在移动和嵌入式平台上均有实现。

Result: 与之前的端侧LLM推理系统相比，在达到先前工作最佳吞吐量的情况下，LeanStream将内存使用量减少了4.8倍到7.5倍；同时进一步将token生成吞吐量提升了1.6倍到2.1倍。

Conclusion: LeanStream通过推测与修正的流式机制有效解决了激活稀疏性利用中"执行决策准确性"与"计算-I/O重叠效率"之间的根本性权衡，证明了在资源受限的端侧设备上实现高效LLM推理的可行性。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [117] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估矩阵值连续思维链模型中秩（rank）作为潜在思维结构观测量的有效性。通过四种训练机制和多层读出头实验，发现秩-投影消融曲线始终平坦（波动在0.6个百分点以内），表明矩阵潜在表示的秩并未编码多重并行推理路径。进一步通过MRI探针和负对照实验证明，秩–k消融本身混淆了"秩无关性"与"位置无关性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续思维链模型将推理压缩到潜在token中，而矩阵值变体引入秩作为单样本结构可观测量。如果矩阵潜变量通过叠加携带并行推理路径，秩应能追踪这些路径。关键科学问题在于：秩是否真正承载了可独立观察和利用的推理结构信息？回答这一问题对理解此类模型内部推理机制至关重要。

Method: 研究采用了系统性消融与多重对照的实验设计：(1) 在matrix-CODI模型的四种训练机制（ProsQA三种+GSM8K-Aug一种）上进行秩-k投影消融；(2) 设计四种不同的读出头（双线性重参数化、双线性+GELU、SVD增强MLP、Z Z^T二次读出头）排除flatten-then-project读出机制导致的秩失明；(3) 使用线性探针对比原始预训练隐藏状态；(4) 在vanilla GPT-2 SFT上设置负对照实验（三个种子，n=500），比较同一干预范式下的行为。

Result: 所有秩-k投影消融曲线在0.6个百分点内保持平坦；三种子复制准确率81.0±2.0pp而有效秩在{4,12,13}间变化，表明损失函数不奖励特定秩。四种非线性读出头均无法揭示秩相关性（Spearman p值为0.63,0.14,0.82,0.46）。线性探针AUC为0.673，低于原始预训练隐藏状态的0.846。vanilla GPT-2 SFT负对照也再现平凡曲线（范围0.20pp），说明该消融范式本身不足以区分秩盲性与位置无关性。

Conclusion: 矩阵CODI模型中的秩未编码可观察的多重推理路径结构。所有秩消融结果均平坦，无论读出头是否非线性；负对照实验进一步指出：秩-k消融作为方法学工具存在根本局限，其混淆了秩盲性与位置无关性，不能单独用于论证矩阵潜变量中的结构编码。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [118] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用深度学习光流替代传统窗口相关跟踪方法，用于静止卫星大气运动矢量（AMV）的立体风反演，并通过知识蒸馏实现单卫星全盘风场生成，提升了水汽波段的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV反演依赖窗口交叉相关和NWP背景温度的圆形依赖，导致高度不准确、计算成本高且覆盖率稀疏；立体风方法虽消除了NWP依赖并提高精度，但计算量大且覆盖有限，且需要多卫星重叠观测。

Method: 用深度光流替代立体匹配中的窗口跟踪；通过微调平衡自监督几何残差损失和有监督探空重建损失；将立体教师模型蒸馏为单卫星学生模型；学生模型模拟教师的卡方和高度不确定性用于质量控制。

Result: 三样本协方差验证表明，立体风在水汽波段（6.2、6.9和7.3 μm）性能优于业务AMV，但在长波红外（11.2 μm）波段存在退化。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习光流和知识蒸馏的方法能够高效改进立体AMV反演，单卫星学生模型可生成全球全盘风场，并在水汽波段超越业务水平，但仍需改进长波红外波段的表现。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [119] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究调查了现代深度学习中的缩放定律是否适用于精算费率厘定任务。使用真实车险组合数据，在多个模型家族上进行数据规模扩展实验，发现所有模型都随数据量增加而改善，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM在数据缩放上表现远优于纯监督表格Transformer和标准MLP基线。Transformer的参数量扩展效果有限，除非引入额外归纳偏置（如TabM式适配或自监督）。研究为按数据规模选择模型提供了定量指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中已广泛验证"损失随数据量、参数量、算力增加呈幂律改善"的缩放定律，但该规律是否适用于精算费率厘定场景尚不清楚。精算数据具有表格化、异质性、高噪声等特征，且传统GLM仍是强基线，因此需要系统探究缩放定律在此类实际任务中的适用性，为模型选择提供定量依据。

Method: 使用真实车险组合数据集，在不同模型家族（GLM、MLP、表格Transformer、TabM、带自监督的Transformer变体等）上，以递增的训练数据比例和多种随机种子进行训练，采用泊松偏差（Poisson deviance）作为泊松计数预测的似然基损失函数进行样本外评估，通过比较各模型家族的数据缩放指数和参数缩放行为来系统分析缩放规律。

Result: 所有模型家族均随数据量增加而有所提升，但缩放指数存在显著差异：TabM表现出显著更强的数据缩放能力，优于纯监督表格Transformer和标准MLP基线。Transformer变体在参数扩展上表现疲弱，除非引入额外归纳偏置（TabM式适配或自监督机制）。单纯增大Transformer规模带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 在精算表格任务上，有效缩放取决于架构设计和损失函数目标的设计，仅仅增加Transformer规模收效甚微。TabM类方法更具数据效率优势。研究结果为依据数据规模选择精算模型提供了定量指导，表明缩放定律虽在精算场景中部分成立，但其特性高度依赖特定架构与目标函数设计。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [120] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种算法解决神经场从稀疏观测中快速高质量重建的难题：NTK-KIP通过蒸馏支撑集实现NTK修复；MetaQuill通过元学习共享初始化实现小偏移快速适应；MetaQuill-KIP融合两者，获得高PSNR重建和语义合理的修复，仅需轻量级实例适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场（NFs）将连续坐标映射到颜色等信号，但从稀疏观测中进行快速高质量重建仍然困难。经典NTK回归有闭式解但本质是线性的，无法积累可复用的任务先验，需要弥合解析核与实用少样本重建之间的差距。

Method: 提出三种算法：(1) NTK-KIP：学习蒸馏支撑集坐标（及可选标签），使有限NTK能从少量观测数据修复大块缺失区域，获得紧凑非线性表示；(2) MetaQuill：元学习INR的共享初始化，新场景仅更新小的任务特定权重偏移，实现真正的特征学习和可复用先验；(3) MetaQuill-KIP：融合两者，先用KIP风格非线性热启动播种任务，再在元学习初始化基础上精炼小偏移。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测条件下实现了高PSNR重建和语义合理的修复，且仅需轻量级逐实例适配，相比之下扩散式基线依赖大型预训练生成先验和昂贵的逐图像调优。

Conclusion: NTK驱动的神经场可以被赋予非线性和元学习能力，缩小了解析核方法与实践少样本重建之间的差距。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [121] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究MoE（混合专家）+LoRA多领域微调中"路由分离能否防止负迁移"的假设，发现即使专家路由近乎不相交，领域间仍会发生干扰，并提出SpawnLoRA方法通过动态添加门控子适配器来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常假设MoE中的token级路由能够自然地将不同领域的更新分离到不同专家中，从而避免负迁移。作者希望检验这一假设在MoE+LoRA组合下的有效性。

Method: 1) 使用配对Python代码与生物医学文本、数学推理数据进行实验，检验MoE+LoRA的路由分离假设；2) 提出Jaccard路由重叠和适配器梯度余弦相似度两种诊断指标，分别衡量专家共享程度与更新兼容性；3) 提出SpawnLoRA方法，在检测到适配器级冲突时，于MoE专家内部动态添加带门控的子适配器，同时保持路由器固定。

Result: 实验发现：生物医学数据加入后代码困惑度显著上升，说明路由分离不足以防止负迁移；诊断指标表明干扰主要来自在共享低秩适配器子空间内近正交的领域梯度竞争；在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B上，SpawnLoRA相比标准LoRA和秩自适应LoRA更有效降低负迁移。

Conclusion: 专家内部的机构性分离带来的收益超越了单纯路由分离或秩扩展，负迁移问题需要结合适配器子空间层面的隔离来解决。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [122] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿大语言模型在批量优化场景中的表现，涵盖连续和离散两类设置。研究发现，LLM在数值测试函数的零样本批量优化上与经典方法竞争力相当但表现脆弱；而在语义丰富的离散空间中，LLM体现出显著优势，这与预训练数据的结构相似性密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM凭借大规模预训练已被视为优化的有吸引力的先验，但现代推理型LLM在批量优化设置中的有效性尚未得到充分探索，因此需要系统性地填补这一研究空白。

Method: 研究者对当前一代前沿LLM作为批量优化器的性能进行了实证评估，覆盖连续数值测试函数和离散语义空间两类优化设置，并与经典非LLM优化方法进行对比。

Result: LLM在数值测试函数上作为零样本批量优化器表现具有竞争力但结果脆弱；然而在语义丰富的离散空间中，LLM的批量优化行为表现出显著更优的效果。

Conclusion: LLM的优化能力高度依赖于搜索空间与预训练数据的结构相似性——在结构与语言/语义预训练数据相近的离散空间中效果最佳，而在纯数值优化上优势有限且鲁棒性不足。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [123] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了合成数据生成中通过因果图边切割实现公平性的机制是否具有通用性。作者将三种公平性定义从DECAF框架移植到九种来自三个不同家族（边际分布、GAN和扩散模型）的生成器上，并在不同隐私保护级别下进行了2520组匹配实验。结果显示该机制在所有生成器中都有效，且新提出的因果扩散骨干网络在公平性和保真度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DECAF此前在单一非私有GAN上证明了三种公平性定义可以转化为生成器因果图上的三种边切割操作，但该机制是否适用于其他类型的生成器、是否本质上是因果分解本身而非DECAF框架的专有特性，这一问题尚未得到验证。

Method: 将三种公平性定义（DECAF中的三个边切割集合）移植到九种生成器上，这些生成器来自三个无关家族：边际分布方法、GAN和扩散模型，每种都包含差分隐私变体。在Adult和COMPAS数据集上进行了2520组匹配对实验，跨越三个正式隐私保护级别，并引入了一种新的因果扩散骨干网络。

Result: 该机制在所有生成器家族中均可迁移有效；新提出的因果扩散骨干在公平性上表现最佳，保真度接近边际分布的基准水平。应用边切割对保真度影响很小，仅使下游分类器AUC平均下降约0.07到0.15，且加入隐私保证不会降低数据公平性。

Conclusion: 因果图边切割实现公平性的机制是通用且普适的，并非DECAF框架所独有，而是因果分解本身固有的特性。其在实际应用中成本较低，且可与差分隐私兼容而不牺牲公平性。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [124] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BioGlyph方法，将网络拓扑结构编译为可解释的"结构角色语言"，使大语言模型（LLM）能够更好地推理网络结构问题。在20个跨5个领域的网络上，该方法相比基线方法在系统准确率上最高提升26个百分点，并在酵母蛋白互作网络中揭示了生物学组织规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言领域表现出色，但当网络以边列表、句子或测量表格形式呈现时，难以理解其结构含义（如中心性、桥接社区、删除后的变化等）。现有表示方法缺乏对网络拓扑结构的显式、可解释编码，限制了LLM对结构化系统的推理能力。

Method: BioGlyph通过两阶段流程工作：首先利用图划分和结构测量识别网络中各类结构角色（如枢纽节点、社区核心、跨社区连接器）；然后通过固定规则将这些角色翻译为通用的可解释词汇表，每个元素由其结构角色、支持证据和语义后果共同描述。该方法不改变网络本身，也不微调LLM，仅改变输入表示形式。

Result: 在20个网络（覆盖5个领域）上，BioGlyph显著提升了开源LLM回答结构推理问题的能力，相比基于边的、数值的和学习得到的表示，系统准确率最高提升26个百分点。消融实验表明增益来源于以语义可解释的术语显式编码结构角色。增益在密集、社区结构明显的网络中更突出，在能从文本直接推断拓扑的稀疏网络中减弱。在芽殖酵母蛋白互作网络中，BioGlyph揭示了跨社区连接器富集于必需基因而外围蛋白则耗竭的生物学规律。

Conclusion: BioGlyph提供了一种兼具可解释性和迁移性的网络表示方法，既能让大语言模型更好地进行结构推理，也能帮助科学家理解网络的组织原理，弥合了网络拓扑分析与LLM推理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged. Across twenty networks spanning five domains, BioGlyph substantially improves open LLMs' ability to answer structural reasoning questions, outperforming edge-based, numerical and learned representations by up to 26 percentage points in system accuracy. Ablations show that the gain comes from explicitly encoding structural roles in semantically interpretable terms. The gain is more prominent in dense, community-structured networks and diminishes in sparse networks whose topology is more readily inferred from text. In a budding-yeast protein-interaction network, BioGlyph exposes biological organization: cross-community connectors are enriched for essential genes, whereas peripheral proteins are depleted. BioGlyph thus provides an interpretable representation for both language models and scientists to reason about network structure.

</details>


### [125] [The 2026 PNPL Competition: Word Classification and Efficient Cross-Subject Generalisation in LibriBrain100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03231)
*Francesco Mantegna,Gereon Elvers,Dulhan Jayalath,Gilad Landau,Tasha Kim,Miran Özdogan,Luisa Kurth,Teyun Kwon,SungJun Cho,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,Anna Greer,Pratik Somaiya,Christian Herff,Yorguin Mantilla Ramos,Hamza Abdelhedi,Karim Jerbi,Greg Farquhar,Brendan Shillingford,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 2026年PNPL竞赛基于扩展的LibriBrain100数据集（新增32名受试者，每人约40分钟数据），设立两个赛道：Deep赛道聚焦于大规模同受试者词分类以追求最佳性能，Broad赛道聚焦于跨受试者泛化，逐步将微调数据从约40分钟降到约10分钟，推动非侵入性脑机接口向临床可行方向迈进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年PNPL竞赛虽然在语音检测和音素分类任务上取得了显著成绩，但其成功依赖单个受试者约50小时的大量数据。而实际的脑机接口需要新用户仅凭几分钟数据即可使用，因此需要解决跨受试者泛化这一关键挑战。

Method: 构建LibriBrain100扩展数据集，在原有单受试者约50小时数据基础上，新增32名受试者（每人约40分钟）并提供更多同受试者数据（约80小时）。竞赛设计两个互补赛道：Deep赛道（同受试者词分类，追求最佳性能）和Broad赛道（跨受试者泛化，逐步减少受试者特定微调数据量）。

Result: 2025年竞赛获胜方案在语音检测和音素分类任务上分别达到95.6%和73.6%的F1-macro分数。2026年竞赛则以此为基础，将任务推进到词分类，并针对跨受试者泛化设置了不同数据量的微调条件。

Conclusion: 通过将微调数据降至约10分钟这一临床可行范围，该竞赛旨在推动非侵入性脑机接口取得实质性进展，最终实现为严重瘫痪患者恢复沟通能力的目标。

Abstract: The ambition of the 2025 PNPL competition (Landau et al., 2025) was to launch a multi-year curriculum for non-invasive speech decoding. Designed to progress from foundational tasks toward the linguistic complexity required for a practical brain-computer interface (BCI), it set the stage with speech detection and phoneme classification tasks. Winning submissions reached F1-macro scores of 95.6% and 73.6% on the respective tasks (Elvers et al., 2026), highly significant advances. This success was built on the LibriBrain dataset (Özdogan et al., 2025), the largest within-subject MEG dataset recorded at the time with ${\sim}50$ hours of data for one subject. However, while within-subject scale drives strong decoding performance, a practical BCI must generalise to new users from minutes of data, not hours.
  The 2026 PNPL competition responds to this challenge with LibriBrain100 (Mantegna et al., 2026), an extended LibriBrain dataset with 32 additional subjects (${\sim}40$ minutes each) plus even more within-subject data (${\sim}80$ hours). Advancing the curriculum of tasks to focus on word classification, two complementary tracks are presented in this competition: the Deep track targets within-subject word classification at scale, aiming at the best possible performance; the Broad track targets cross-subject generalisation, progressively reducing the amount of subject-specific fine-tuning data from ${\sim}40$ to ${\sim}20$ to ${\sim}10$ minutes, a duration that falls within a clinically feasible range and brings us a step closer to a non-invasive BCI capable of restoring communication to people living with profound paralysis.

</details>


### [126] [B2B Customer Conversion Prediction: A Document Representation, Graph Theory, and CatBoost Driven Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03239)
*Tianqi Wang,Sheikh Shams Azam,Wan Eih Huang,Anton Wiranata,Christopher G. Brinton,Jan P. Allebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了B2B一次性销售场景下的客户转化预测问题，提出了一种基于多键聚合的B2B客户数据整合算法，采用新颖架构对含拼写错误和变体的公司名称进行聚类，定义并生成特征后使用CatBoost模型进行客户转化预测，最终达到91%的预测准确率，并结合预测结果讨论个性化营销活动推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在B2B一次性销售情境中，购买周期可能长达数月甚至数年。在漫长的过程中，精准识别高购买潜力的客户并推荐个性化营销活动对有效营销至关重要，因此需要解决客户数据聚合、特征生成和转化预测等问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于多键将个体联系人聚合到B2B客户级别的算法；针对公司名称等非标准化键提出一种新颖的聚类架构，能够处理拼写错误和拼写变体等不规则情况；定义并生成一系列特征后，应用CatBoost模型进行客户转化预测。

Result: 该框架实现了91%的预测准确率。

Conclusion: 基于预测结果和模型分析，探讨了个性化营销活动推荐策略，以促进客户转化。

Abstract: In the one-time selling B2B context, the buying cycle may last months or even years. During the long process, targeting customers that have a high potential to make purchases and recommending personalized campaigns accordingly are important for effective marketing. For this goal, we study the following problems, B2B customer data aggregation, customer feature generation, and prediction of whether a B2B customer would show interest in making a purchase (i.e., prediction of conversion into sales funnel). We propose an algorithm to aggregate individual contacts to the B2B customer level based on multiple keys. For non-standardized keys such as company names, we propose a novel architecture to cluster them in a domain encompassing irregularities such as spelling mistakes and spelling variants. We then define and generate a set of features and apply the CatBoost model for customer conversion prediction. Our framework achieves 91\% prediction accuracy. Based on the prediction results and analysis of the model, we then discuss personalized campaign recommendations to foster conversion.

</details>


### [127] [FlowBalance: Verifier-Grounded Self-Improvement from On-Policy Reasoning Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03241)
*Zixun Huang,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBalance是一种基于验证器校准的自改进推理方法，通过将策略自身的token级对数概率增益与验证器导出的组优势校准，构建归一化的完整响应分布。负面轨迹的引导信号被反转，无偏好时禁用，并通过轨迹平衡拟合目标分布。在数学推理任务上，FlowBalance在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上均优于FlowRL，同时提升训练速度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型从自身同策略经验中改进存在脆弱性：终端验证器监督可靠但稀疏，而密集的同模型引导可能强化虚假自信或将学习过度集中在单一解模式上。需要一种既能利用验证器可靠监督，又避免自我引导误导的方法。

Method: FlowBalance构建完整响应上的归一化分布。对每条同策略轨迹，使用冻结的训练时策略视图结合特权上下文生成token级对数概率增益，聚合为轨迹级自引导分数。该分数与验证器导出的组优势校准：正优势轨迹保留引导，负优势轨迹反转引导，无偏好组禁用引导。能量函数指数式重加权参考策略，通过概形轨迹平衡以每组一次对数配分估计拟合归一化目标，无需单独的token级模仿损失。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，FlowBalance在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上均优于FlowRL的平均性能，同时提升训练速度和稳定性，避免直接OPSD的响应长度坍缩，并在受控AIME24诊断中展现更高的正确策略多样性。

Conclusion: FlowBalance通过结果校准的自引导与轨迹平衡实现稳定的推理模型自我改进，理论分析确立了组内对比保持、最小变化反向KL刻画、验证器对目标奖励的单调控制，以及对被拒响应假阳性自引导的精确修正。实验结果验证了其在性能、效率和鲁棒性上的综合优势。

Abstract: A reasoning model can improve from its own on-policy experience, but this inner loop is fragile: terminal verifiers provide reliable yet sparse supervision, while dense same-model guidance can reinforce false confidence or overconcentrate learning on a narrow solution mode. We introduce FlowBalance, a verifier-grounded self-improvement method that learns a normalized distribution over complete responses. For each on-policy trajectory, a frozen training-time view of the same policy uses privileged context to produce token-level log-probability gains, which are aggregated into a trajectory-level self-guidance score. FlowBalance calibrates this score with the verifier-derived group advantage: guidance is retained on positive-advantage trajectories, reversed on negative-advantage trajectories, and disabled when the rollout group provides no outcome preference. The resulting energy exponentially reweights a reference policy, and profiled trajectory balance fits the normalized target with one log-partition estimate per rollout group. This realizes outcome-calibrated self-guidance via trajectory balance, without a separate token-level imitation loss. Our analysis establishes within-group contrast preservation, a minimum-change reverse-KL characterization, monotonic verifier control of target reward, and an exact correction against false-positive self-guidance on rejected responses. On mathematical reasoning, FlowBalance improves average performance over FlowRL on both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, while also improving training speed and stability, avoiding direct OPSD's response-length collapse, and exhibiting higher correct-strategy diversity in a controlled AIME24 diagnostic.

</details>


### [128] [Selective Hypergraph Refinement for Frozen Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03265)
*Zimo Si*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性超图精化（SHR）方法，用于对已训练并冻结的图聚类模型进行后处理：在不更新模型参数、节点表示或原始图结构的前提下，利用属性超图补充高阶关系，仅对具有充分证据支持的节点更新聚类分配。实验表明该方法在15个骨干-数据集组合中13个取得正向宏增益，平均增益为0.066个百分点，且仅有约0.2%的硬分配发生变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图聚类方法大多通过优化模型参数和节点表示来提升聚类性能，但对已经训练好且冻结的模型，缺乏有效的进一步改进手段。作者希望探索一个尚未被充分研究的问题：在模型完全冻结、不使用标签的情况下，能否通过后处理进一步精化已有聚类结果。

Method: SHR方法的核心步骤包括：(1) 利用属性超图补充普通图无法表达的高阶关系；(2) 从超图生成候选残差方向；(3) 使用图结构、节点属性和匹配零模型（matched-null）证据评估候选方向的可靠性；(4) 只更新具有充分支持证据的节点，否则保留原有聚类分配。文中还分析了节点是否改变聚类的条件——由原生分配间隔与精化方向强度共同决定。

Result: 在受控通用评测套件中，15个骨干-数据集组合中13个获得正的平均宏增益，1个无变化，1个为负。宏增益均值为0.066个百分点（95%自助法置信区间[0.030, 0.107]），平均仅0.209%的硬分配发生变化。更广泛的15种组合原生接口评测中，宏增益为0.137个百分点，平均变化率为0.375%。

Conclusion: 冻结的图聚类输出在训练后仍保留有限但可测量的精化空间。该效果在骨干-数据集对之间具有异质性，且更广泛的覆盖也会增加负迁移的风险暴露。SHR为冻结模型的聚类后处理提供了一种可行且高效的改进路径。

Abstract: Existing graph-clustering methods typically improve clustering performance by optimizing model parameters and node representations. Effective means of further improving the clustering results of an already trained and frozen model, however, remain limited. We study post-processing for frozen graph clustering. After checkpoint fixation, the procedure uses no labels and updates neither model parameters, node representations, nor the original graph structure. Instead, it exploits an attribute hypergraph to supplement higher-order relations that ordinary graphs cannot readily express, thereby refining existing cluster assignments. Because global hypergraph refinement can yield both performance gains and erroneous updates, we propose Selective Hypergraph Refinement (SHR). The method generates candidate residual directions from the hypergraph and evaluates their reliability using graph structure, node attributes, and matched-null evidence. It updates only nodes with sufficient support and otherwise retains their original assignments. Further analysis shows that whether a node changes cluster is jointly governed by its native assignment gap and the directional strength of the refinement. In a controlled common-suite evaluation, 13 of 15 backbone-dataset cells had a positive mean macro gain, one produced exact no-action, and one was negative. The cell-equal macro gain was 0.066 pp (95% bootstrap CI, [0.030, 0.107] pp), while only 0.209% of hard assignments changed on average. A broader 15-combination native-interface evaluation yielded a macro gain of 0.137 pp at a mean change ratio of 0.375%. These results indicate that frozen clustering outputs retain a limited but measurable refinement space after training. The effect is heterogeneous across backbone-dataset pairs, and broader coverage also increases exposure to negative transfer.

</details>


### [129] [Latent Energy Action Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03294)
*Phu Pham,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEAP是一种新的潜在世界模型规划方法，将整个动作序列视为可微变量进行优化，结合终端潜在目标匹配与终端窗口状态能量，在不动冻结LeWM模型的情况下，将任务成功率从LeWM+CEM的77.5%提升至94.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型在高维观测下进行模型预测控制时，优化单一学习到的潜在目标容易产生解码器预测的终端描述符与目标描述符不匹配的动作序列，导致规划性能受限。

Method: LEAP将完整动作时域作为可微变量，通过冻结的LeWM进行优化。其核心包括：(1) 耦合终端潜在目标匹配与终端窗口状态能量（低能量要求预测的终端潜在表示与目标潜在一致，且解码器预测的终端描述符与目标描述符一致）；(2) 使用冻结的目标条件提议初始化搜索；(3) 通过拟牛顿求解器在自回归展开中精炼动作；(4) 优化后投影确保动作在允许范围内。

Result: 在四个控制域中，使用官方发布的LeWM检查点，LEAP将平均成功率从LeWM+CEM的77.5%提升至94.8%，提升了17.3个百分点，且完全保留冻结的LeWM表示与预测器。

Conclusion: LEAP通过在规划阶段引入能量耦合机制和可微优化，证明即使不重新训练世界模型，也能显著提升潜在世界模型的规划性能，为潜在模型预测控制提供了更可靠的规划范式。

Abstract: Latent world models support efficient model predictive control from high-dimensional observations, yet optimizing a single learned latent objective can favor action sequences whose decoder-predicted terminal descriptor does not match the goal descriptor. We introduce Latent Energy Action Planning (LEAP), which treats the complete action horizon as a differentiable variable and optimizes it through a frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM). LEAP couples terminal latent goal matching with a terminal-window state energy. Low energy requires the predicted terminal latent to agree with the goal latent and the decoder-predicted terminal descriptor to agree with the goal descriptor. A frozen goal-conditioned proposal initializes the search, a quasi-Newton solver refines actions through the autoregressive rollout, and post-optimization projection enforces the admissible action range. Across four control domains using the officially released LeWM checkpoints, the complete LEAP planning system raises mean success from 77.5% for LeWM planned with the cross-entropy method (LeWM+CEM) to 94.8% under a matched protocol, a 17.3-percentage-point improvement, while retaining the frozen LeWM representation and predictor.

</details>


### [130] [Geometry-Aware Graph Construction via Adaptive Spectral Bandwidth Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03306)
*Ecem Bozkurt,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核的图方法中高斯带宽σ的自适应选择准则，通过将核的谱复杂度与底层流形的内在复杂度相匹配（即匹配有效秩与由最小生成树估计的局部内在维度），在CIFAR-100上对六个SSL编码器进行评估，证明该方法在留一法分类和标签传播精度上优于固定带宽及其他自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核化图方法（如谱聚类、扩散映射、稀疏核回归图）的性能高度依赖于高斯带宽σ的选择。σ过小会高估局部复杂度，将每个样本视为独立方向；σ过大则导致核坍塌多个方向，条件数发散，丧失几何判别信息。现有方法缺乏一种能将核的谱复杂度与底层流形内在复杂度对齐的原则性带宽选择方案。

Method: 提出一个逐节点的带宽准则：通过将核的有效秩与基于最小生成树估计的局部内在维度进行联合匹配，并将搜索锚定在流形一致的log-log尺度区域，从而实现核的谱复杂度与底层流形内在复杂度的协调一致。

Result: 在CIFAR-100数据集上，对六个自监督学习（SSL）编码器生成的嵌入进行评估，自适应带宽方法在留一法（LOO）分类和标签传播（LP）精度上一致优于固定带宽方法及其他竞争性的自适应方法。

Conclusion: 通过将核的谱复杂度与流形的内在复杂度对齐来选择带宽，可以更好地保留几何判别信息，从而在谱聚类、扩散映射等核化图方法中取得更优的分类与传播性能。

Abstract: Kernelized graph methods - spectral clustering, diffusion maps, and sparse kernel -regression graphs - that use Gaussian kernels depend on the choice of Gaussian bandwidth sigma, which governs the spectral character of the local kernel operator. When sigma is too small, the kernel overestimates local complexity and treats each sample as an independent direction; when sigma is too large, the kernel collapses multiple directions together, the condition number diverges, and all geometric discrimination is lost. We propose a choice of scale to make the spectral complexity of the kernel consistent with the intrinsic complexity of the underlying manifold. We propose a per-node bandwidth criterion that operationalizes this principle by jointly matching the kernel's effective rank to the local intrinsic dimension estimated via minimum spanning tree, anchoring the search in the manifold-consistent log-log scaling regime. We evaluate SSL embeddings from six encoders on CIFAR-100, showing that adaptive bandwidth consistently improves leave-one-out (LOO) classification and label propagation (LP) accuracy over fixed-bandwidth methods and competing adaptive methods.

</details>


### [131] [Risk and Anomaly Identification for Distribution Network Optimal Operation Based on Reinforcement Learning and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03308)
*Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度强化学习框架，通过分布强化学习与贝叶斯深度强化学习的结合，实现二阶不确定性量化，将总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性和认知不确定性，分别用于刻画配电网固有运行风险和分布外异常检测，从而实现配电网的可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代配电网在普遍不确定性下需要及时识别运行风险和异常事件。实际运行中，运营商必须识别随机但分布内条件固有的风险，以及对应于分布外行为（如异常负荷模式、极端天气或网络物理攻击）的异常。现有方法难以联合处理这两类问题。

Method: 提出一个显式不确定性感知的深度强化学习框架，将分布深度强化学习与贝叶斯深度强化学习相结合，实现二阶不确定性量化方案，将总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性（aleatoric）和认知不确定性（epistemic）两部分。认知不确定性估计用于驱动训练期间的探索和部署期间的分布外检测及回退控制；偶然不确定性估计用于刻画固有运行风险。

Result: 仿真结果展示了所提出的DRL智能体的性能以及不确定性量化的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将不确定性分解用于不同目的：偶然不确定性表征固有运行风险，认知不确定性驱动探索和分布外异常检测，从而提升配电网最优运行的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Reliable operation of modern distribution networks requires timely identification of operational risks and anomalous events under pervasive uncertainty.
