In practice, operators must identify risks that are inherent in stochastic yet in-distribution conditions, and anomalies that correspond to out-of-distribution behaviors such as unusual load patterns, extreme weather or cyber-physical attacks. This paper addresses this joint risk and anomaly identification problem for optimal distribution network operation and proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework that is explicitly uncertainty aware. We integrate distributional and Bayesian deep reinforcement learning to realize a second- order uncertainty quantification scheme that decomposes total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, which are respectively used to characterize inherent risk and out-of- distribution anomalies. The resulting epistemic estimates drive both exploration during training and out-of-distribution detec- tion with fallback control during deployment, whereas aleatoric estimates are used to characterize intrinsic operational risk. Simulation results demonstrate the performance of our DRL agent and the effectiveness of the uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [132] [DE-Venus: A Data-Efficient RLVR Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03324)
*Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Kai Tang,Zhengqing Zang,Xing Zheng,Haobo Wang,Yingfan Ma,Bowen Song,Bo Han,Bo An,Lei Feng,Weiqiang Wang,Junbo Zhao,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DE-Venus是一个面向数据高效RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）的统一框架，将监督视为跨越数据准备和策略优化的演化状态，通过三个模块（主动数据选择、弱监督构建、训练时监督精炼）组织整个生命周期，支持七种代表性方法和数据选择流水线，在保留verl分布式执行契约的同时，仅用10%标签或13%相关数据即可保持或提升模型质量，并减少63%-75%的收敛步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升大语言模型推理能力，但其实际扩展受限于昂贵的on-policy rollout和获得可靠目标的高成本。现有方法分别处理样本选择、不完整监督或噪声标签问题，缺乏统一框架，且常将监督逻辑与分布式训练纠缠在一起，阻碍了受控比较和复用。

Method: DE-Venus将监督视为一个跨数据准备与策略优化的演化状态，组织为三个模块：主动数据选择（分配训练和标注预算）、弱监督构建（从未标注示例中派生学习信号）、训练时监督精炼（过滤或纠正不可靠监督）。通过将方法特定的决策表达为离线数据集转换或对目标、奖励、批次和优势的在线变换，兼容七种代表性方法和数据选择流水线，同时保持verl的分布式执行契约。

Result: 在公开基准和三个业务场景中，独立配置仅使用10%的标签或低至13%的相关数据即可保持或改善模型质量；选定的业务配置还将观测到的收敛步骤减少63%-75%。

Conclusion: DE-Venus在不牺牲可扩展RL执行的前提下，显著降低了标注成本和训练开销，为数据高效RLVR提供了一种统一、解耦且可复用的框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves large language model reasoning, but its practical scaling is constrained by expensive on-policy rollouts and the cost of obtaining reliable targets at scale. Existing methods address sample selection, incomplete supervision, or noisy labels separately, often entangling supervision logic with distributed training and hindering controlled comparison and reuse. We present DE-Venus, a unified framework for data-efficient RLVR that treats supervision as evolving state across data preparation and policy optimization. It organizes this lifecycle into three modules: Active Data Selection allocates training and annotation budgets; Weak Supervision Construction derives learning signals from unlabeled examples; and Training-Time Supervision Refinement filters or corrects unreliable supervision. DE-Venus supports seven representative methods and a data-selection pipeline by expressing method-specific decisions as offline dataset transitions or online transformations of targets, rewards, batches, and advantages while preserving verl's distributed execution contracts. Across public benchmarks and three business scenarios, separate configurations preserve or improve model quality with only 10% of labels or as little as 13% of relevant data; selected business configurations also reduce observed convergence steps by 63%--75%. DE-Venus thus reduces annotation and training costs without sacrificing scalable RL execution.

</details>


### [133] [A Large Open Multi-Energy Corpus of Soil Compaction Tests, with Machine-Learning Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03337)
*Sompote Youwai,Chana Phutthananon,Warat Kongkitkul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个包含2,854个实验室击实试验的大规模数据集，涵盖162个来源组和四种Proctor能量水平。研究发现相当比例的已发表压实数据在物理上不可能（零气隙条件筛选移除11.8%记录）。提出了从分类套件和压实标准估算最大干密度和最优含水量的预测模型，包括表格基础模型和符号回归，并通过相位关系约束确保预测不超物理极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的击实试验相关性研究受限于样本量小、通常来自单一实验室且使用单一压实能量，并且很少公开发布。工程填充体设计需要最大干密度和最优含水量，每次测定都需要完整的Proctor试验，因此需要构建一个更大规模、多来源、多能量水平的开放数据集，以建立更可靠的预测模型。

Method: 从六个公开来源收集2,854个实验室击实试验记录，跨162个来源组和四种Proctor能量水平。每条记录都严格审计至其来源命名的Proctor方法，不推断能量。使用零气隙条件进行数据筛选，剔除物理上不可能的记录。然后使用表格基础模型和符号回归方法，从分类套件和压实标准预测最大干密度和最优含水量，并通过相位关系耦合预测结果。

Result: 零气隙筛选移除了11.8%的调和记录（含实测比重记录中移除5.7%）。最优饱和度基线为0.815，变异系数11%。表格基础模型在随机折下达到密度R²=0.824、含水量R²=0.784；按来源组折叠时为0.727/0.696；扣留整个来源时为0.520/0.614。压实能量在边际上可忽略但条件性上很关键：修改Proctor记录预测密度时，含能量R²=0.740，不含能量则为-0.651。

Conclusion: 相当比例已发表的压实数据在物理上不可能，说明现有数据质量存在问题。推荐的预测模型用于筛选（如试验计划设计）而非验收标准。最优饱和度0.815是基线而非常数，压实能量的影响在特定条件下至关重要。符号回归得到的封闭形式通过相位关系耦合，确保预测对不会超过零气隙线。

Abstract: Every engineered fill is specified by a maximum dry density and an optimum moisture content. Each determination needs a full Proctor test. Published correlations rest on one to four hundred specimens, usually from one laboratory at one compactive energy, and are seldom released. This paper releases a corpus without those limits. It holds 2,854 laboratory compaction tests from six public sources, across 162 provenance groups and four Proctor energy levels, with fines from 1.5 to 100%. Every record is audited to the Proctor method its source names, and no energy is inferred. Screening on the zero-air-voids condition removed 11.8% of harmonised records, and 5.7% of those with a measured specific gravity. A material share of published compaction data is physically impossible. The optimum degree of saturation over the corpus is 0.815 at a coefficient of variation of 11%. That is a baseline, not a constant. Both parameters are then estimated from one classification suite and the compaction standard. A tabular foundation model reaches R2 0.824 for density and 0.784 for water content under random folds. It reaches 0.727 and 0.696 with folds drawn around provenance, and 0.520 and 0.614 with a whole source held out. Compactive energy is negligible marginally yet decisive conditionally. Density on the 66 modified-Proctor records is predicted at R2 0.740 with it and -0.651 without. Symbolic regression yields closed forms coupled through a phase relation. No predicted pair can then exceed the zero-air-voids line. The predictions are for screening, not acceptance.

</details>


### [134] [Gradients Know What Outcomes Don't: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning with Gradient-Aligned Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03342)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Fang Niu,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Jie Wu,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gradient-Aligned Reward (GAR)，利用策略自身的梯度空间构建稠密奖励信号：通过对输出投影层进行截断反向传播提取紧凑梯度向量，再与专家锚定梯度计算余弦相似度作为奖励。该方法在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上优于GRPO等基线，且在竞赛级数学基准上表现出色，并零样本泛化到GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro，额外开销低于9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR的二元结果奖励无法区分正确的推理轨迹；稠密奖励替代方案要么忽略训练语料中已有的专家解，要么需要昂贵的离线标注，亟需一种利用已有专家数据且无需额外标注的高效稠密奖励方法。

Method: GAR方法：在策略自身的梯度空间中操作，通过对输出投影层进行截断反向传播，为每个rollout提取紧凑梯度向量，然后计算该向量与专家锚定梯度的余弦相似度作为稠密、推理感知的奖励信号。作者还从理论上证明该余弦相似度可分解为预测误差因子与激活模式因子的乘积。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，GAR在竞赛级数学基准上持续优于GRPO及其他基线，并能迁移到GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro（无需领域特定数据）。额外墙钟开销低于9%。

Conclusion: GAR展示了利用策略梯度空间构建稠密奖励的可行性，既无需额外标注又充分利用了已有的专家知识，是一种低成本高效益的RLVR改进方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models, yet its binary outcome reward cannot distinguish among correct trajectories. Existing dense reward alternatives, from surface heuristics to process reward models, either ignore the expert solutions already present in training corpora or require expensive offline annotation. We propose Gradient-Aligned Reward (GAR), which operates in the policy's own gradient space: truncated backpropagation through the output projection layer extracts a compact gradient vector for each rollout, and cosine similarity with an expert-anchor gradient yields a dense, reasoning-aware reward with less than 9% wall-clock overhead. We prove that this cosine admits a multiplicative decomposition into prediction-error and activation-pattern factors, providing a concrete characterization of what the alignment signal measures. On Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, GAR consistently improves over GRPO and other baselines on competition-level math benchmarks and transfers to GPQA Diamond and MMLU-Pro without domain-specific data. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LQgdwind/GAR.

</details>


### [135] [From Zero to Hero: An Open LLM Ecosystem for Armenian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03350)
*Erik Arakelyan,Khatun Avetisyan,Meri Davtyan,Heghine Grigoryan,Nane Khachatryan,Hayk Shahsuvaryan,Henrik Sergoyan,Vahan Martirosyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建并发布了两个亚美尼亚语数据集（ArmWeb新闻语料和ArmSTEM平行STEM数据集），通过持续预训练Gemma-4-E4B得到arm-gemma-e4b模型，该模型在现有开放亚美尼亚语模型中表现最优，且首次提供了完整的训练数据与配方；消融实验显示纯新闻持续预训练虽提升流畅性但损害知识，加入少量翻译STEM数据可逆转此损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚美尼亚语作为形态丰富且低资源的语言，其预训练数据稀缺，且目前没有开放亚美尼亚语大语言模型公开可复现的数据与配方，这一空白阻碍了该语言NLP研究的发展。

Method: （1）精心构建并验证ArmWeb（437万篇亚美尼亚语新闻文档）与ArmSTEM（37.3万道英亚平行数学与科学题及逐步解答，经LLM判断与人工评估双重验证）两个数据集；（2）基于Gemma-4-E4B进行持续预训练，并通过消融实验比较纯新闻与混合STEM数据的训练效果。

Result: 所训练的arm-gemma-e4b模型在所有现有开放亚美尼亚语模型及未适配的基础模型上均取得最优性能；消融实验发现新闻预训练提升流畅性但侵蚀知识（现有亚美尼亚语模型中也观察到该现象），少量经验证的翻译STEM数据可逆转知识损失；同时发现最大公共亚美尼亚语语料库与网页评估集存在严重重叠，包括FineWeb-2中训练/测试的自重叠。

Conclusion: 本研究首次发布了具备完整训练数据和配方的开放亚美尼亚语LLM，验证了新闻与STEM数据混合的训练策略有效性，并揭示了公共语料库与评估集重叠的严重问题，为低资源语言LLM的开发提供了可复现的范式。

Abstract: Pretraining data for Armenian, a morphologically rich and low-resource language, is scarce, and no open Armenian LLM has been released with the data and recipe needed to reproduce it. To address this gap, we curate and release two datasets. ArmWeb is an extensively validated corpus of 4.37M Armenian news documents. ArmSTEM is a parallel English-Armenian collection of 373K math and science problems with step-by-step solutions, translated into Armenian and verified through both answer-preserving LLM judgment and human evaluation. Continued pretraining of Gemma-4-E4B on these datasets yields arm-gemma-e4b, which outperforms every existing open Armenian model as well as its unadapted base, and is the first open Armenian LLM with complete training data and recipe. Our ablations show that news-only continued pretraining improves fluency while eroding knowledge, a pattern we also observe in existing Armenian models, and that a small share of verified translated STEM data reverses the loss. We further find that the largest public Armenian corpora overlap web-derived evaluation panels heavily, including a train/test self-overlap inside FineWeb-2. We openly release all data, models, and code.

</details>


### [136] [Time Without Timesteps: Simulating Coupled Dynamical Systems via Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03358)
*Liyu Zerihun,Mark Shinyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的动力系统数值仿真范式：将传统逐步时间推进（stepwise rollout）替换为基于轨迹级自洽约束的定点迭代求解。作者为每个子系统训练神经代理模型（从完整驱动轨迹和初始条件直接映射到完整输出轨迹），并通过波形松弛（waveform relaxation）的思想组装耦合系统，使仿真变成一个在完整轨迹上求解定点问题的过程。在耦合van der Pol振子和Hodgkin-Huxley神经元网络上验证：求解迭代仅需4-10次Newton迭代（对比参考积分器1500步），梯度计算转化为线性系统并用GMRES求解，内存与求解深度无关。此外，作者发现由学习算子Jacobian的谱半径这一标量可提前预测耦合求解是否收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力系统数值仿真是按时间因果逐步推进的，每一步状态依赖前一步，这导致仿真深度（时间步数）与计算/梯度开销线性相关，尤其在耦合系统中，深度带来的递归梯度计算既耗时又占用大量内存。作者希望探索一种非逐步推进的替代方案，将仿真组织为完整轨迹上的定点求解问题，从而打破时间递归的瓶颈，实现更高效且内存友好的仿真与梯度计算。

Method: 1) 对每个子系统类型训练神经代理（surrogate），直接学习从完整驱动轨迹加初始条件到完整输出轨迹的映射，而非逐步映射。2) 借鉴经典波形松弛方法，将耦合系统组装为轨迹自洽约束，使仿真变为完整轨迹上的定点问题。3) 定点问题通过Newton迭代求解（4-10次迭代即可收敛）。4) 梯度不再沿时间递归，而是转化为线性系统，用GMRES求解，内存与求解深度解耦。5) 利用学习算子Jacobian的谱半径作为标量判据，预测耦合求解的收敛性。

Result: 在耦合van der Pol振荡器和Hodgkin-Huxley神经元网络上，定点迭代方案仅需4-10次Newton迭代即可收敛，而参考积分器需要1500步。梯度通过GMRES求解线性系统获得，内存开销与求解器深度无关。作者还发现Jacobian的谱半径可提前预测耦合求解是否收敛：越过收敛边界后，展开的反向传播发散，Neumann伴随（adjoint）方法失败，而隐式梯度仍保持0.04%的精度。论文也报告了方法的成功场景及代理误差导致性能退化的情况。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将耦合动力系统仿真重构为轨迹级定点问题在计算效率和内存效率上的显著优势，并揭示了一个重要的可预测性：通过一个标量（Jacobian谱半径）即可预判定点求解的收敛性。这为大规模耦合系统仿真及基于梯度的优化（如参数学习、控制）提供了新的范式。但方法性能受代理模型精度制约，未来需进一步提升代理精度或研究误差鲁棒策略。

Abstract: Numerical simulation of dynamical systems is usually organized as a causal march through time: each state is computed from the previous one. We explore a different formulation for coupled systems. For each subsystem type we train a neural surrogate mapping a full driving trajectory and initial condition directly to a full output trajectory; following classical waveform relaxation, coupled systems are assembled by enforcing self-consistency among these trajectories: simulation becomes a fixed-point problem over complete trajectories rather than a stepwise rollout. On coupled van der Pol oscillators and Hodgkin-Huxley neuron networks, sequential depth becomes the number of solver iterations: 4-10 Newton iterations where the reference integrator takes 1500 steps. The gradient likewise loses its time recursion: it becomes a linear system solved by GMRES at memory independent of solver depth. A single scalar measured from the learned operator, the spectral radius of its Jacobian, predicts in advance where the coupled solve will converge; past that boundary, unrolled backpropagation diverges and a Neumann adjoint fails, while the implicit gradient remains correct to 0.04%. We report where the approach succeeds and where surrogate error degrades it.

</details>


### [137] [SimpleDesign: A Joint Model for Protein Sequence and Structure Codesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03377)
*Jiarui Lu,Yuyang Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu,Navdeep Jaitly,Joshua M. Susskind,Miguel Ángel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SimpleDesign，一种直接在数据空间训练的端到端多模态蛋白质设计模型，通过Mixture-of-Transformer架构结合离散交叉熵（序列）和回归目标（结构），在超过200万序列-结构对上训练，绕过了传统两阶段潜在空间生成范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蛋白质共设计模型通常需要多阶段训练：先训练自编码器将数据token化为潜在表示，再在潜在空间上训练生成模型。作者假设这种多阶段训练对于获得性能良好的共设计模型并非必要，因此探索直接在数据空间进行单阶段端到端训练的可能性。

Method: SimpleDesign采用单阶段端到端目标函数，包含序列的离散交叉熵损失和结构的回归损失。架构上设计了一种Mixture-of-Transformer结构，允许模态特异性处理（序列与结构分别处理），同时保持跨模态的全局自注意力。模型在超过200万序列-结构对上训练。

Result: SimpleDesign在蛋白质共设计和无条件序列/结构生成基准测试中均取得了强性能表现。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，多阶段潜在空间训练并非蛋白质共设计模型的必要条件，直接的数据空间单阶段训练策略同样能获得性能优异的模型，这为蛋白质设计模型的训练范式提供了更简洁有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Proteins are fundamental to biological processes, with their function determined by the complex interplay between the amino acid sequence and the three-dimensional structure. Developing generative models capable of understanding this intrinsically multi-modal relationship is crucial for fields like drug discovery and protein engineering. Existing models often rely on a multi-stage training process where autoencoders that tokenize data into latent representations are trained in a first stage. Secondly, a generative model is trained on the latent representation of the autoencoder(s), i.e., generative modeling in a latent space. We hypothesize that this multi-stage training is not necessary to obtain performant co-design models and thus present SimpleDesign, an effective multi-modal protein design model trained directly in the data space. SimpleDesign leverages a single-stage end-to-end objective that combines discrete cross-entropy for sequences and a regression objective for structures. In order to effectively model the difference in sequence and structure modalities, we develop a Mixture-of-Transformer architecture that allows modality-specific processing while keeping global self-attention over both modalities. We train SimpleDesign on over 2M sequence-structure pairs achieving strong performance across co-design and unconditional sequence/structure generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [RecurTrace: Adaptive Latent Reasoning with Loop-Time Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03379)
*Yuxiang Wang,Kunyu Feng,Yingda Shen,Haoning Xu,Junyu Wang,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecurTrace通过两项改进增强语言模型的潜在循环推理：一是引入Loop Memory Attention，让循环层沿循环时间轴关注自身先前迭代的状态，使模型能回溯早期计算；二是引入halting head机制，通过oracle监督动态决定循环次数，避免固定循环数的浪费或不足。实验表明RecurTrace在MathQA上以平均2.0次循环达到56.9%准确率，且在不同规模的生成任务中均优于同预算的微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明重复中间层块可以增加语言模型的有效推理深度而无需增加参数，但这种潜在循环有两个限制：每次迭代只能看到上一次输出，无法直接访问更早的计算；同时固定循环次数对简单输入浪费深度，对困难输入计算不足。

Method: RecurTrace包含两大核心组件：Loop Memory Attention允许每个循环层沿循环时间轴关注其自身在先前迭代中的状态，使模型能重新审视早期计算而非仅依赖最新状态；halting head读取循环状态以预测是否继续循环，其监督信号来自一个能识别何时继续加深深度可降低损失的oracle。

Result: 在相同的循环骨干网络控制对比下，RecurTrace在MathQA上以平均2.0次循环达到56.9%准确率，比最优固定循环深度高2.2个点（计算量匹配）。ACT和PonderNet退化为1次循环，CALM仅54.1%但需5.6次循环，LoopUS-Conf为55.3%（3.2次循环），TaH-Mismatch为55.7%（2.1次循环）。在0.6B至8B规模的生成任务中，RecurTrace优于同预算微调基线0.6至3.4个百分点，且增益随模型规模增大而增加。

Conclusion: 利用循环自身轨迹的Loop Memory Attention和自适应halting机制能显著提升潜在循环推理的性能与效率，且该优势随模型规模扩大而增强。

Abstract: Repeating a small block of middle layers increases a language model's effective inference depth without adding parameters or generating extra tokens, and recent work shows that this latent recurrence improves reasoning. However, two design choices limit these gains. Each iteration sees only the previous output and cannot directly access earlier computations. Moreover, a fixed loop count wastes depth on easy inputs while leaving hard ones with too little computation. We introduce RecurTrace, which addresses both limitations using the loop's own trajectory. Specifically, Loop Memory Attention lets each looped layer attend to its own states from previous iterations along the loop-time axis, so the model can revisit earlier computations instead of relying on the latest state alone. A halting head then reads the loop state and predicts whether to continue, with supervision from an oracle that identifies when additional depth still reduces loss. In a controlled MathQA comparison on the same looped backbone, RecurTrace achieves 56.9% accuracy with an average of 2.0 loops, exceeding the best fixed loop depth by 2.2 points at matched compute. By comparison, ACT and PonderNet collapse to one loop, and CALM reaches only 54.1% with 5.6 loops, while the stronger LoopUS-Conf and TaH-Mismatch baselines reach 55.3% at 3.2 loops and 55.7% at 2.1 loops. Finally, RecurTrace improves generation accuracy over same-budget fine-tuned baselines at 0.6B, 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, with the gain growing with model size from 0.6 to 3.4 points.

</details>


### [139] [TIGPO: Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03383)
*Jinwei Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间实例图策略优化（TIGPO），通过跨策略更新维护持久化转移图，并引入探索槽与重访槽的预算分配机制，在同一任务上跨时间配对当前与历史轨迹组以稳定相对优势估计，在ALFWorld和WebShop上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的策略优化方法在每个策略更新周期内独立构建图，丢弃了早期策略发现的状态转移信息，且优势估计受限于小规模、批次局部的轨迹组，导致长程LLM智能体的信用分配不够准确。

Method: TIGPO维护每个任务的持久化转移图，使不同策略版本发现的合法转移共同决定当前轨迹的信用；在固定预算内分配探索槽（普通任务采样）和重访槽（延迟重试先前探索过的任务）；重访时将当前轨迹组与对应的早期探索组配对构建跨时间参考，历史转移与分数仅作为结构性且解耦的统计参考，不参与策略损失回放。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上的实验表明，TIGPO在性能上持续优于先前的基于组和基于图的策略优化方法。

Conclusion: 跨时间维护转移图并结合同任务跨时期配对参考可以有效改善长程LLM智能体的信用分配和策略优化，尤其在小组轨迹场景下更加稳定。

Abstract: Graph-based policy optimization improves credit assignment for long-horizon LLM agents by organizing rollout trajectories into state-transition graphs. However, existing methods construct graphs independently within each policy update, discarding transitions discovered by earlier policies and limiting advantage estimation to small, batch-local rollout groups. We propose \emph{Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization} (TIGPO), which extends graph-based credit assignment across policy updates. TIGPO maintains a persistent transition graph for each task, allowing valid transitions discovered by different policy versions to jointly determine credit for current rollouts. To actively reconnect current exploration with historical experience, TIGPO allocates a fixed rollout budget between Exploration slots for ordinary task sampling and Revisit slots for delayed reattempts of previously explored tasks. For each revisit, TIGPO pairs the current rollout group with its corresponding earlier Exploration group to construct a cross-temporal reference. The enlarged reference is designed to stabilize relative advantage estimation under small rollout groups, while comparison on the same task directly captures policy improvement across training stages. Historical transitions and scores serve only as structural and detached statistical references and are never replayed in the policy loss. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that TIGPO consistently outperforms prior group-based and graph-based policy optimization methods.

</details>


### [140] [Inferred Generative-Process Diversity Predicts Correlated Failure Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03422)
*Ross Tieman,Evan Markou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用生成过程多样性（通过归一化压缩距离衡量）来评估多语言模型系统的多样性，相比传统的语义相似性，能更好地预测模型对间的相关失败，从而为多模型系统的安全设计提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样性对集体系统韧性至关重要，但评估多语言模型系统时，语义相似性只能捕捉输出含义的差异，无法反映模型间生成过程的根本差异及其相关失败模式，需要更本质的多样性度量。

Method: 基于算法信息论，使用原始模型输出之间的归一化压缩距离（置换对照残差化）来推断生成过程多样性。对38个语言模型在10个不重叠基准族上进行跨任务分析，并与语义相似性和模型对能力进行比较。

Result: 该方法识别出语义相似性遗漏的群体结构；跨基准偏秩相关为-0.216（95%区间[-0.309,-0.122]），在所有10个基准上均为负值，表明生成过程多样性与模型对相关失败减少相关，且该关联不能归因于语义相似性或能力。

Conclusion: 推断的生成过程多样性为多模型系统的多样性与安全性研究提供了新颖且实用的度量方法，能补充语义相似性的不足，对安全相关场景中多模型系统的构建与评估具有重要价值。

Abstract: Diversity is a widely observed factor in the resilient function of collective systems, yet the type of diversity that matters depends on the properties and failure modes of the system. This distinction is important for systems composed of multiple language models. Different models may be treated as independent components even when their behaviour and failures remain strongly correlated. Assessments of language-model populations using semantic similarity demonstrate limited semantic diversity, but this captures only differences in the meaning of observed outputs. We argue that a more fundamental notion of model diversity is generative-process diversity, the differences between processes capable of generating the observed outputs. Drawing from Algorithmic Information Theory, we use Normalised Compression Distance between raw model outputs, residualised against a permutation control, as a measure of inferred generative-process diversity. Across 38 language models, this measure identifies population structure missed by semantic similarity and predicts cross-task variation in chance-corrected correlated failure among model pairs across ten disjoint benchmark families, beyond semantic similarity and model-pair capability. The cross-benchmark partial rank association is $-0.216$ with a 95% interval of $[-0.309,-0.122]$, and the estimate is negative on all ten benchmarks. These results indicate that increased generative-process diversity is associated with reduced correlated failure in model pairs that is not attributable to semantic similarity or capability. Inferred generative-process diversity offers a novel and practical approach for investigating diversity of multi-model systems in safety-relevant contexts.

</details>


### [141] [TraveL: Transformer-based Multi-view Path Distributional Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03427)
*Fang He,Tao-yang Fu,Wang-chien Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的多视角分布表示学习框架TraveL，用于道路网络中的路径表示学习。与传统方法仅利用共现关系学习路径向量不同，TraveL将路径与出行起始时间编码为分布表示，以捕捉多样的出行者行为及路段区域间的相关性，并通过K-S检验辅助训练。在合成和真实数据集上，该方法在出行时间分布估计、路径相似性预测和目的地预测等任务上均优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径表示学习方法仅利用路段与路径间的共现关系来学习向量形式的路径表示，未能探索出行者行为的多样性和路段间的区域相关性，导致路径表示信息不充分。

Method: 提出TraveL框架：1）基于Transformer将路径与出行起始时间编码为分布表示，可用于解码出行者行为的可能样本；2）设计区域注意力机制编码路段间的区域相关性；3）利用Kolmogorov-Smirnov (K-S)检验比较采样出行者行为与真实数据之间的差异来辅助训练。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上优于最先进方法：Mean K-S距离（出行时间分布估计）提升14.7%，MAE（路径相似性预测）提升16.7%，MAE（目的地预测）提升3.97%。

Conclusion: 实验验证了分布表示学习和区域相关性编码对路径相关应用的显著价值，TraveL框架在多个路径预测任务上均取得一致且明显的性能提升。

Abstract: Path representation learning (PRL) for road networks has received increasing research attention, due to various path-related applications. Existing works on PRL typically exploit the co-occurrence relationship among road segments and paths to learn a vector as the path representation, without exploring the varied traveler behaviors and the regional correlation on the path. In this work, we propose to learn distributional representations, which provide valuable information for use in path-related applications, by capturing the varied traveler behaviors as well as the various dependencies within regions of road segments. We propose a novel Transformer-based Multi-view Distributional Representation Learning (TraveL) framework to encode a path along with a travel starting time to a distributional representation, which can be used to decode possible samples of on-path traveler behavior. Moreover, by analyzing the regional correlation which reveals various road segment relationships, we propose a regional attention to encode these correlations in a path. Also, we explore the idea of Kolmogorov-Smirnov (K-S) test to compare the sampled traveler behavior against the collected ground truth to facilitate training. Experimental results show that the proposed TraveL model outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real-world datasets, by 14.7% in Mean K-S distance for travel time distribution estimation, 16.7% in Mean Absolute Error (MAE) for path similarity prediction, and 3.97% in MAE for destination prediction.

</details>


### [142] [It's the Problem, Not the Path: Budget and Difficulty Confounds in LLM Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03436)
*Yigit Utku Bulut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了现有关于大语言模型推理轨迹中"突破时刻"和"早期可读命运"的研究结论，通过两种严格的对照实验（重启动控制的截断探针和预注册的难度控制测试）表明：在178个问题-模型单元中仅1个真正受前缀限制；早期内部信号在控制问题难度后几乎没有可检测的结果信息，先前报道的高池化AUROC主要反映问题难度而非尝试内信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM推理轨迹的研究广泛解读其中包含"突破"时刻或早期即可预见结局，但这些解读所依赖的测量缺少两个层面的反事实对照：一是在声称层面缺少"从头重新计算"的对照；二是在结果信息层面缺少问题难度基线的控制。作者旨在通过提供这两类缺失的对照来检验这些解读的可靠性。

Method: 本文采用两种方法：(1) 重启动控制的截断探针：将"附加锚点的继续求解率"与"从头重启动曲线"在同等的生成token预算下进行比较，应用于89个MATH问题×2个小开放模型的178个单元；用重启剂量-反应区分计算受限模型与能力受限模型。(2) 预注册的难度控制测试：检验早期窗口内部信号是否携带超出问题难度基线的结果信息；另用两种无生成的语料分析作为对照——痕迹盲的难度代理探针和紧密重建的先前公开发表的早期窗口探针。

Result: 178个单元中仅1个真正受前缀限制；在匹配预算可行处，延续自己的前缀优于重启（9/9），主要体现为计算压缩而非扩展可达性。控制难度后，早期内部信号无超出基线的可检测结果信息；痕迹盲难度代理在192K DeepSeek-R1上达到AUROC 0.873（落在已发表的探针范围内）；重建的早期窗口探针在池化水平达0.849，但在问题内部与随机无异（t=4时为0.496）。

Conclusion: 高池化探针AUROC本身不能证明尝试内的信息性；必须加入仅含问题的基线或进行问题内评估。先前关于推理轨迹中存在"早期可读命运"的结论可能被问题难度混淆所污染，需要在更严格的对照下重新审视。

Abstract: Reasoning traces of large language models are widely read as containing "breakthrough" moments and early-legible fates. Both readings rest on measurements missing a counterfactual control at the level of the claim; we supply both controls. First, a restart-controlled truncation probe separates when a solution fits the continuation budget from when a prefix carries value that fresh computation cannot buy, comparing per-anchor continuation solve rates against from-scratch restart curves at matched total generated-token budget. Applied to 178 problem-model cells (89 MATH problems x two small open models, an outcome-blind but difficulty-targeted cohort), exactly 1 of 178 cells survives as prefix-limited; restart dose-response separates a compute-starved model from a capability-limited one; and wherever the matched budget lies inside the restart grid, continuing the model's own prefix beats restarting (9 of 9) -- predominantly compute compression rather than expanded reachability. Second, a pre-registered, difficulty-controlled test finds no detectable outcome information in early-window internal signals beyond a problem-difficulty baseline, and two generation-free analyses of public corpora show why this control is needed: a trace-blind difficulty proxy reaches AUROC 0.873 on 192K DeepSeek-R1 generations -- inside the published probe range -- and a closely matched reconstruction of the closest published early-window positive recovers a comparable pooled result (0.849) while within problem it is statistically indistinguishable from chance at all ten anchors (0.496 at t=4); a post-hoc within-targeted probe finds only a small average residual, concentrated in three low-failure problems. High pooled probe AUROCs cannot by themselves establish within-attempt information; a question-only baseline or within-problem evaluation is required.

</details>


### [143] [Guide, Not Bind: Why Defeasible Priors Fail in Augmented Lagrangian Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03442)
*Sairam Sundararaman,Sara Girdhar,Manit Narasimha Murthy,Samrudh N,Bhaskarjyoti Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了可微因果发现方法中编码专家先验的"引导而非约束"（guide, not bind）设计，指出基于增广拉格朗日（ALM）惩罚的禁忌边约束存在两个独立且精确刻画的问题：顺序惩罚递增机制会在大幅提升前抑制被错误禁止的真实边，且相关性匹配目标无法区分真实边与其反向边（二者成本完全相同），而协方差匹配则可实现可证明的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微因果发现方法越来越依赖将专家先验编码为禁忌边约束，通过ALM惩罚强制执行，其假设是数据自适应的松弛机制会折扣并最终覆盖与数据矛盾的错误规则。然而这一设计假设缺乏严格验证，作者旨在揭示这一设计为何以及如何失败。

Method: 作者提出两个精确刻画的问题：(1) 通过三个必要条件和DADU松弛规则的反例证明顺序惩罚递增ALM会抑制被错误禁止的真实边（在4到32节点图上进行3,072次训练实验验证，错误先验在87-97%的情况下抑制真实边）；(2) 通过闭式证明（引理）表明相关性匹配目标将真实边和反向边绑定到相同的成本2r^2（Lemma 1），并证明协方差匹配能以至少w_0^4的可证明间隔分离两个方向（Lemma 2）。

Result: 实验确认在DADU下，单个错误先验在87-97%的试验中抑制了真实边；理论上证明相关性归一化丢弃了使模型可识别的方差信息，导致方向不可区分，而协方差匹配提供了可证明的方向分离保证。

Conclusion: "引导而非约束"设计存在根本性缺陷：即使修复自适应松弛机制，标准相关性匹配目标本身也会因丢弃方差信息而使方向不可识别。修复这两点只能部分恢复该设计的有效性，需要更根本性的设计变革。

Abstract: Differentiable causal discovery methods increasingly encode expert priors as forbidden-edge constraints enforced by an Augmented Lagrangian (ALM) penalty, on the assumption that a data-adaptive relaxation mechanism will discount and eventually override a rule the data consistently contradicts. We show this design, which we call \emph{guide, not bind}, fails for two independent, precisely characterized reasons, and that directly repairing both restores it only partially. First, sequential penalty-ramping ALM suppresses a wrongly-forbidden true edge before any counterfactual check can detect it: we give three necessary conditions any adaptive relaxation must satisfy to avoid this (Proposition~\ref{prop:conditions}), prove that DADU---the natural relaxation rule this paper introduces as the object of study---violates all three (Corollary~\ref{cor:dadu_failure}), and confirm the failure across 3{,}072 training runs spanning graphs from 4 to 32 nodes, where a single wrong prior suppresses a true edge in 87--97\% of trials under DADU. Second, and independent of any fix to the mechanism, we prove in closed form that the standard correlation-matching objective ties a true edge and its reverse to an identical cost of exactly $2r^2$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:tie}), not because the underlying equal-variance model is unidentifiable, but because normalizing to correlation discards exactly the variance information that would make it identifiable; covariance matching instead separates the two directions by a provable margin of at least $w_0^4$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:separation}).

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Straightness: Non-Crossing Flow Matching via Quantile AlignTree Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03443)
*Junyi Lin,Mengyu Li,Jingxuan Hu,Kejun He,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAT-FM（Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching），一种基于分位数对齐树结构的高效耦合策略，用于构建高斯先验与目标数据分布之间的结构化耦合，以提升Flow Matching的生成性能并降低耦合构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching的性能高度依赖于源分布与目标分布之间耦合的质量。独立耦合常常导致路径交叉和局部速度模糊问题，而基于最优传输（OT）的耦合虽然质量较高但构建成本昂贵。因此需要一种既高效又能提供高质量路径的耦合策略。

Method: QAT-FM通过分位数对齐的树结构在高斯先验与目标数据分布之间构建分层耦合。耦合构建时间为O(Nd log N)，每对样本的源采样复杂度为O(d)，适用于大规模高维生成任务。理论上证明了QAT耦合满足边际一致性、诱导非交叉的线性插值路径、并在中间时刻一致性地改善路径分离度。此外，方法可自然扩展到条件生成场景。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，QAT-FM在保持有竞争力的生成性能的同时，显著降低了耦合构建成本。

Conclusion: QAT-FM提供了一种兼具效率与质量的结构化耦合方案，在理论和实验层面均验证了其有效性，为大规模高维Flow Matching生成任务提供了一种实用的耦合构建方法。

Abstract: The performance of Flow Matching largely depends on the quality of the coupling between the source and target distributions. However, independent coupling often leads to path crossings and local velocity ambiguity, while OT-based couplings typically incur high construction costs. To address this challenge, we propose Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching (QAT-FM), an efficient structured coupling strategy that constructs a hierarchical coupling between a Gaussian prior and the target data distribution via a quantile-aligned tree structure. QAT-FM constructs the coupling in $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log N)$ time and supports per-pair source sampling with $\mathcal{O}(d)$ complexity, enabling scalable training for large-scale high-dimensional generative tasks. Theoretically, we prove that the QAT coupling satisfies marginal consistency, induces non-crossing linear interpolation paths, and consistently improves path separation at intermediate times compared with independent coupling, thereby alleviating local velocity ambiguity. QAT-FM further extends naturally to conditional generation, enabling structured conditional coupling while preserving global Gaussian alignment. Experiments across diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that QAT-FM achieves competitive generative performance while substantially reducing coupling construction cost.

</details>


### [145] [A Two-Stage Forecasting System for CPU Workload Prediction in Private Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03457)
*Ashir Javeed,Anton Borg,Håkan Grahn,Lars Lundberg,Dhyey Patel,Sogand Shirinbab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段级联集成预测模型，先将客户服务请求（TPS）作为中间变量进行预测，再基于TPS预测结果估计未来的CPU工作负载，以显式建模客户需求与资源消耗之间的依赖关系。采用XGBoost级联架构配合扩展窗口自适应在线重训练来处理概念漂移，在包含十个应用的私有云真实数据集上验证，大多数应用SMAPE低于7%，最佳应用MAE为0.7372、RMSE为1.1866、SMAPE为3.57%、R²为0.9185，在60步预测范围内误差累积可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的云资源预测方法大多直接从历史资源轨迹预测未来CPU负载，忽略了客户服务需求与后续资源消耗之间的因果关系，导致预测精度和可解释性不足。

Method: 提出两阶段级联预测框架：第一阶段预测客户服务请求（TPS），第二阶段基于TPS预测结果估计CPU工作负载；两个组件均采用XGBoost模型，并在级联架构中结合扩展窗口策略的自适应在线重训练以应对云负载演化中的概念漂移。

Result: 在私有云环境中十个应用的真实轨迹上进行评估，大多数应用的SMAPE低于7%；最佳应用取得MAE=0.7372、RMSE=1.1866、SMAPE=3.57%、R²=0.9185。水平漂移分析确认了60步预测范围内具有稳定的递归预测行为且误差累积可控。与传统直接CPU预测方法相比，该模型在预测鲁棒性、计算效率和可解释性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 两阶段集成预测模型通过显式建模TPS到CPU负载的依赖链路，有效提升了云环境资源预测的准确性和稳健性，兼具计算效率和可解释性，适用于主动资源管理和智能自动伸缩等云计算场景。

Abstract: Accurate cloud resource forecasting is essential for proactive resource provisioning, maintaining Quality of Service (QoS), and reducing operational costs in dynamic cloud environments. The existing forecasting approaches predominantly estimate future CPU workload directly from historical resource traces, which often overlook the relationship between customer service demand and subsequent resource consumption. This study proposes a two-stage integrated forecasting model that explicitly models this dependency by first forecasting customer service requests, expressed as Transactions Per Second (TPS), and subsequently estimating future CPU workload from the TPS forecast. Both the forecasting component and resource prediction component employed the XGBoost model within a cascaded learning architecture, complemented by adaptive online retraining using an expanding-window strategy to address concept drift in continuously evolving cloud workloads. The proposed work was evaluated using real-world traces collected from a private cloud environment comprising ten applications. Experimental results demonstrate robust forecasting performance by achieving Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE) below $7\%$ for most applications, with the best-performing application achieving an MAE of $0.7372$, RMSE of $1.1866$, SMAPE of $3.57\%$, and an R2 of $0.9185$. Horizon-wise drift analysis confirmed stable recursive forecasting behavior with controlled error accumulation across a 60-step prediction horizon. Compared with the conventional direct CPU forecasting method, the proposed two-stage integrated model gives improved forecasting robustness, computational efficiency, and interpretability, making it well-suited for proactive resource management and intelligent auto-scaling in cloud computing environments.

</details>


### [146] [Mind the Gap: Robustness Risks in PII Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03464)
*Adeel Zafar,Slawomir Nowaczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代PII检测系统在标准基准上表现优异，但在真实部署中的分布偏移下存在严重鲁棒性失败。通过构建跨越七类自然分布偏移的压力测试基准，评估了三种主流架构（基于编码器的NER、基于规则的混合检测和生成式LLM抽取），发现三者存在互补的失败模式，并提出了带有QA反馈循环的混合检测流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PII检测系统的标准基准评估掩盖了部署中遇到的现实分布偏移（如噪声、非结构化、非正式输入）所导致的鲁棒性缺陷，而这些漏检直接构成隐私和安全风险。

Method: 构建包含七类自然分布偏移的压力测试基准，评估三种广泛部署的架构族：基于编码器的NER（SpaCy）、基于规则的混合检测（Presidio）、生成式LLM抽取（Qwen2.5-3B）；基于其互补失败模式提出混合检测流水线并引入QA驱动的反馈循环机制。

Result: 三种系统在分布外输入上均显著退化但失败模式互补：编码器模型主要失败于未见表面形式和边界检测，基于规则系统失败于非标准格式，LLM表现出实体类型混淆和生成不稳定。表明聚合基准分数掩盖了部署关键弱点，没有任何单一架构在所有PII类别上始终可靠。

Conclusion: 研究强调OOD评估对PII系统的重要性，提出混合检测流水线配合QA反馈循环实现迭代风险缓解，并发布基准以支持PII系统的分布偏移感知评估。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection is a foundational component of data protection infrastructure where missed entities constitute direct privacy and security risks. Although modern PII systems report strong performance on standard benchmarks, we show that these evaluations mask substantial robustness failures under realistic distribution shifts encountered in deployment. Rather than comparing state-of-the-art accuracy, we study how different PII detection paradigms fail under noisy, unstructured, and informal inputs. We construct a stress test benchmark spanning seven categories of natural distribution shift and evaluate representative systems from three widely deployed architectural families: encoder-based NER (SpaCy), rule-based hybrid detection (Presidio), and generative LLM extraction (Qwen2.5-3B).
  All three exhibit significant degradation on out-of-distribution inputs, but with distinct and complementary failure modes. Encoder models primarily fail on unseen surface forms and boundary detection, rule-based systems fail on non-standard formats, and LLMs exhibit entity-type confusion and generation instability. These results show that aggregate benchmark scores obscure deployment-critical weaknesses and that no single architecture is uniformly reliable across PII categories. Motivated by these findings, we propose a hybrid detection pipeline with a QA-driven feedback loop for iterative risk mitigation, and release our benchmark to support OOD-aware evaluation of PII systems.

</details>


### [147] [Spectral characteristics of autoencoder parameters as a vector representation of data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03495)
*Maria Nikitina,Anton Bishuk,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自编码器模型参数与训练数据统计特性之间的关系，提出将模型参数视为对应数据样本的稠密向量表示。通过理论分析和实验验证，基于模型参数矩阵的谱特征（奇异值）构建的向量表示能够在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上高精度地区分在不同数据子集上训练的模型，表明自编码器参数可以被视为样本表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自编码器模型参数是否能够作为训练数据样本的向量表示，从而在不使用原始样本的情况下实现模型与数据之间的信息传递和区分。

Method: 通过理论分析证明自编码器参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值之间存在关联；基于此，利用参数矩阵的谱特征构建向量表示，并在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于自编码器参数谱特征构建的向量表示能够高精度地区分在不同数据子集上训练的模型，无需复杂的向量生成算法或原始样本。

Conclusion: 训练完成的自编码器参数可以视为训练数据样本的有效表示，这为数据空间与参数空间之间的信息传递提供了新的理论依据和实际方法。

Abstract: This paper examines the relationship between the parameters of autoencoder models and the statistical properties of the data on which they are trained. Autoencoders are defined as models with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained to reconstruct input data through a compressed latent representation. It is proposed that the model parameters can be viewed as a dense vector representation of the corresponding sample. To test this hypothesis, a theoretical and experimental study is conducted in which a vector representation is formed based on the spectral characteristics of the autoencoder parameter matrices. Theoretical analysis shows that the singular values of the model parameter matrices are related to the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix of the training data, ensuring the transfer of information between the data space and the parameter space. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and FashionMNIST datasets confirm that the resulting vector representations allow for a high degree of accuracy in distinguishing between models trained on different data subsets, without resorting to complex vector generation algorithms or using the original samples. These results suggest that the parameters of trained autoencoders can be viewed as sample representations.

</details>


### [148] [Restricted Eigenvalues Beyond Gaussian Width: Threshold Occupancy under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03504)
*Shi Fu,Huibo Xu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统否定了COLT 2015开放问题中关于重尾设计下限制特征值（RE）界的猜想，证明均匀小球条件不足以保证与亚高斯测量相同的样本复杂度规律，关键障碍是"同时阈值占用"现象。作者给出了显式的反例构造，并建立了尖锐的最坏情形样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banerjee等人（2015）在COLT提出的开放问题：在仅满足均匀小球条件的重尾设计中，RE界是否遵循与各向同性亚高斯测量相同的样本复杂度定律（1+w(A)^2）。作者希望验证或否定这一猜想。

Method: 通过构造显式的常宽度多面体下降锥给出反例；利用阈值编码将有限值域空间嵌入任意窄球冠并提升为完整的多面体下降锥截面；分析固定阈值VC维数下的最坏情形样本复杂度；对比高斯测量与重尾设计的路径行为差异。

Result: 该猜想在完整的无维度、任意集合形式下失败。常宽度多面体下降锥在高达一半环境维度的样本量下每条样本路径上经验RE为零。每个有限值域空间可精确阈值编码到任意窄球冠中，并可提升为完整的多面体下降锥截面。对固定阈值VC维数d，β↓0时最坏样本复杂度为Θ(β^{-1}[d log(1/β)+log(1/δ)])。高斯测量成功只需O(1+log(1/δ))样本，而各向同性重尾设计在n≲√(p/log p)时路径性失败。高斯平滑后仍保持任意差的RE。

Conclusion: 均匀小球条件不足以复现亚高斯情形下的RE样本复杂度定律。重尾设计与高斯测量之间存在不可弥合的分离，即便在精确各向同性和有限矩条件下依然存在。提出基于仿射维数和包围半径平方的分布无关回退界，在该族上是尖锐的。

Abstract: Restricted eigenvalue (RE) bounds govern stable recovery by norm-regularized estimators. For isotropic sub-Gaussian measurements, the benchmark sample size is $1+w(A)^2$, where $w(A)$ is the Gaussian width of the normalized descent cone. The COLT 2015 open-problem note (Banerjee et al., 2015) asked whether the same law follows for heavy-tailed designs from a uniform small-ball condition alone. We give an explicit and systematic negative answer to the general question as formulated there: the proposed law fails in its full dimension-free, arbitrary-set form, and the missing obstruction is simultaneous threshold occupancy. A constant-width polyhedral descent cone with fixed small-ball constants has zero empirical RE on every sample path up to half the ambient dimension. More generally, every finite range space admits exact threshold encoding in an arbitrarily narrow spherical cap and a lift to a full polyhedral descent-cone section. For every fixed threshold VC dimension $d$, as $β\downarrow0$, the sharp worst-case sample complexity is $Θ(β^{-1}[d\log(1/β)+\log(1/δ)])$. The separation persists under exact isotropy and all finite moments: on the same constant-width cone, Gaussian measurements succeed with $O(1+\log(1/δ))$ samples, whereas an isotropic heavy-tailed design fails pathwise for $n\lesssim\sqrt{p/\log p}$. Gaussian smoothing yields an everywhere-positive $C^\infty$ density while retaining arbitrarily poor RE. Under isotropy, a distribution-free fallback governed by affine dimension times squared enclosing radius is sharp on this family.

</details>


### [149] [An Adversarial Zero-Shot Learning Approach for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate IoT Traffic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03505)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对抗学习和对比损失的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，采用序列VAE架构实现IoT网络的零样本域自适应，无需标签数据和原始特征迁移，在六个跨域数据集和44个迁移场景中验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT网络中的异常检测面临设备多样性、缺乏标注数据以及不同环境间领域差异等独特挑战，现有方法的跨域能力受限于数据分布不匹配和隐私约束。

Method: 提出一个基于序列VAE的框架，联合优化领域不变潜在表征和语义结构化的嵌入空间；引入编解码器适配层用于对齐跨域特征分布；提出基于目的地的分段策略来建模IoT流量的真实通信结构。

Result: 在六个不同领域（工业、企业、通用、智能家居、军事自动化）的数据集上、44个迁移场景中进行了全面评估，在多个跨域设置中表现出强零样本泛化能力，并在真实、异质、隐私受限的IoT条件下与对比域适应基线性能相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该框架在无标签数据和隐私受限的IoT场景中实现了有效的零样本跨域异常检测，展示了对抗学习和对比损失在建模领域不变表征方面的潜力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to the diversity of devices, lack of labeled data, and domain variability across environments. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection that leverages adversarial learning and contrastive loss within a sequence-based Variational Autoencoder (VAE) architecture. Our method enables zero-shot domain adaptation by jointly optimizing domain-invariant latent representations and semantically structured embedding spaces, without requiring labeled data or raw feature transfer. To address the heterogeneity of IoT deployments, we introduce encoder and decoder adaptor layers that align feature distributions across domains while preserving contextual semantics. Additionally, we propose a destination-based segmentation strategy to better model real-world communication structures in IoT traffic. Our framework is comprehensively evaluated on six distinct datasets spanning industrial, enterprise, general-purpose, smart home, and military automation domains across 44 transfer scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization in several cross-domain settings and competitive performance against a contrastive domain-adaptation baseline under realistic, heterogeneous, and privacy-constrained IoT conditions.

</details>


### [150] [LongCounsel-8: A Benchmark Suite for Longitudinal Depression Tracking from Multi-Session Counseling Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03507)
*Jiayi Li,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LongCounsel-8基准套件，包含三个独立生成的数据集，共7,749个五会话咨询轨迹，用于纵向抑郁症追踪研究。通过基于档案的模拟、实证状态构建和间接行为实现三个策略生成数据，并通过实验揭示了三个关键发现：单次会话评分误差低并不保证趋势识别准确、现有方法在恶化轨迹上可靠性较低、额外的会话历史可能反而降低趋势预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向抑郁症追踪需要同时估计当前症状严重程度及其跨会话的变化趋势，但现有资源要么缺少多会话对话的抑郁标签，要么只有带标签的单会话访谈。构建此类基准面临三个挑战：保持纵向一致性和多样性、将症状进展扎根于实证模式、在不暴露目标标签的情况下自然表达受控抑郁状态。作者希望填补多会话纵向抑郁症评估基准缺失的空白。

Method: 作者构建了LongCounsel-8基准套件，包含三个独立生成的数据集，总计7,749个五会话咨询轨迹。数据生成结合了三个核心策略：(1) 基于真实世界客户档案和咨询模式进行档案驱动的模拟；(2) 基于实证数据构建受控的抑郁状态和症状构成；(3) 采用间接行为实现方式，在会话中自然表达抑郁状态而不直接暴露目标标签。通过模拟的自我报告来验证生成的状态与受控状态的一致性。

Result: 实验结果显示：生成的模拟自报告能紧密恢复受控状态，验证了标签保真度。对现有抑郁症追踪方法的测试揭示了三个关键发现：(1) 较低的单会话评分误差并不能保证趋势（改善或恶化）识别的准确性；(2) 现有方法在恶化轨迹上始终表现得更不可靠；(3) 添加更多会话历史可能反而降低趋势预测的准确性。

Conclusion: LongCounsel-8为抑郁症评估从静态单会话预测转向可靠的纵向心理健康变化追踪提供了基础。该研究揭示了现有方法在纵向趋势预测方面的局限性，尤其是在恶化轨迹上的不足，为未来开发更可靠的纵向抑郁症追踪方法指明了方向。

Abstract: Tracking depression from multi-session counseling dialogues requires estimating both current symptom severity and how it changes across sessions. Yet progress on this task is constrained by the scarcity of longitudinal counseling data with standardized session-level depression labels. Existing resources typically provide either multi-session conversations without depression labels or labeled interviews in a single session. Building such a benchmark poses three challenges: maintaining longitudinal consistency and diversity, grounding symptom progression in empirical patterns, and expressing controlled depression states naturally without exposing target labels. To address these challenges, we introduce LongCounsel-8, a benchmark suite of three independently generated datasets totaling 7,749 five-session counseling trajectories, grounded in real-world client profiles, depression trajectories, symptom compositions, and counseling patterns. We combine profile-grounded simulation, empirically informed state construction, and indirect behavioral realization to address these challenges. Across the benchmark, simulated self-reports closely recover the controlled states, supporting label fidelity. Experiments on existing depression tracking methods reveal three key findings: (1) lower single-session score error does not guarantee accurate identification of trend, i.e., improvement or worsening; (2) existing methods are consistently less reliable on worsening trajectories; and (3) additional session history may reduce the accuracy of trend prediction. Together, these findings establish LongCounsel-8 as a foundation for advancing depression assessment from static, single-session prediction toward reliable longitudinal tracking of mental-health change.

</details>


### [151] [LeanGRPO: Eliminating Redundant Recomputation in Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03528)
*Sijie Wang,Zhiqiang Tan,Xinrui Yang,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LeanGRPO，通过重构数据并行布局并引入两种免重计算的训练调度策略（LeanGRPO-Retain和LeanGRPO-Reweight），消除了扩散强化学习rollout阶段与更新阶段之间的冗余前向重计算，在FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO框架下结合FLUX.1-dev和Wan模型实现了最高1.83倍的端到端加速，同时保持原始优化目标不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散强化学习方法（如DanceGRPO和FlowGRPO）在rollout后需要重新计算选定时间步并启用梯度跟踪进行更新。在on-policy训练下使用相同后端时，这种重计算在数学上是冗余的，但直接复用前向骨干网络会带来rollout阶段的大量内存开销。因此需要一种既能消除冗余计算又能控制内存开销的方案。

Method: LeanGRPO重构了数据并行布局，并提出两种免重计算的训练调度：(1) LeanGRPO-Retain：在rollout阶段就启用梯度跟踪，直接复用计算图和保存的激活值用于update阶段的反向传播，无需重计算；(2) LeanGRPO-Reweight：在rollout阶段启用梯度，但立即用临时优势值对每个选定步骤进行反向传播并延迟梯度同步，待轨迹完成后用真实优势值修正临时梯度。两种调度分别适配不同模型规模和输入尺寸。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Wan上使用FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO基准测试，LeanGRPO实现了最高1.83倍的端到端加速，同时保持了原始优化目标。

Conclusion: LeanGRPO通过设计合理的数据并行布局与两种互补的免重计算调度方案，在保持扩散RL优化目标不变的前提下显著提升了训练效率，为大规模图像/视频生成模型的后训练提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Diffusion reinforcement learning (RL) has recently achieved significant success in post-training image and video generative models. However, most diffusion RL methods, including DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, recompute selected timesteps with gradient tracking after rollout. Under on-policy training with the same backend for rollout and update, this recomputation is mathematically redundant. Intuitively, the rollout and policy update steps can reuse the same feed-forward backbone to avoid redundant computation, but doing so can incur a large memory overhead during rollout. To address the issue, we present LeanGRPO by restructuring the data-parallel layout and introducing two recompute-free training schedules for trajectory-logprob diffusion RL: (1) LeanGRPO-Retain enables gradient tracking during rollout and directly reuses the resulting computation graphs and saved activations for backward during update, requiring no recomputation; and (2) LeanGRPO-Reweight also enables gradients during rollout, but immediately backpropagates each selected step using a provisional advantage and delays gradient synchronization, then corrects the provisional gradients with the true advantage after the trajectory is completed. These schedules target different model scales and input sizes. Across FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO with FLUX.1-dev and Wan, LeanGRPO achieves up to 1.83x end-to-end speedup while preserving the original optimization objective.

</details>


### [152] [Coupled Scaling: A Representational Accessibility Framework for Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03533)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"耦合缩放"(Coupled Scaling)框架，认为神经网络的缩放行为不仅取决于数据几何或数据-模型谱，还取决于任务结构与架构-优化系统可达几何之间的耦合关系。通过一个可解的模式截断模型，推导出残差指数位于覆盖率和尾部衰减率所界定范围内，并给出了固定核下的训练时间指数推导等具体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经缩放定律理论要么从数据几何出发，要么从给定的数据-模型谱出发，无法解释为何相同数据上训练的系统会因架构或优化方式的改变而产生不同的缩放行为。需要一个新的框架将"任务结构"与"架构-优化系统可达的几何"联系起来。

Method: 引入任务条件化的耦合缩放框架；在可解的模式截断模型中，损失分解为架构支持外的目标能量与未解决的有支持尾部两部分；对任意优先级顺序，残差介于最优N尾与最大已完成高值前缀之后尾部之间；推导出当两种对数速率分别为γ和ρ时残差指数在[ργ,γ]区间内，在受限off-prefix增益下指数等于ργ(b-1)；通过固定核特化，利用任务加权谱测度的近零尾导出训练时间指数。

Result: 建立了耦合缩放的理论框架，给出了有界范围内的残差指数公式(a=ργ或a=ρ(b-1))，将架构支持与有限预算获取分离，提出两个测试：静态任务相关几何应在共同预算下追踪损失，多尺度几何应追踪耦合特定的指数排序（包括跨对比任务的逆转现象）。文章还审计了已发布的涌现轨迹，提出直接因子检验所需的控制条件。

Conclusion: 耦合缩放框架表明，数据表面的统计规律不足以决定缩放行为，需同时考虑架构-优化系统可达的几何与任务结构的匹配关系。该框架为理论分析神经缩放提供了新的任务条件化视角，并指出了实验验证所需的关键控制条件。

Abstract: Existing theories derive neural scaling from data geometry or a specified data-model spectrum, but systems trained on the same data can scale differently when architecture or optimization changes the representations they can efficiently reach. We introduce Coupled Scaling, a task-conditioned framework in which finite-budget scaling depends on the relation between task structure and the geometry accessible to an architecture-optimization system. In a solvable mode-truncation model, loss separates into target energy outside architectural support and an unresolved supported tail. For an arbitrary priority order, the residual lies between the best-N supported tail and the tail beyond the largest completed high-value prefix. If the cumulative-tail and coverage log-rates are $γ_{A,T}$ and $ρ_{A,O,T}$, the residual exponent lies in $[ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T},γ_{A,T}]$. Under bounded off-prefix gain, the completed prefix is rate-determining and $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T}$; for $a_{A,T,j}\asymp j^{-b_{A,T}}$, this gives $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}(b_{A,T}-1)$. A fixed-kernel specialization derives the training-time exponent from the near-zero tail of a task-weighted spectral measure defined independently of the loss fit. The framework separates architectural support from finite-budget acquisition and motivates two tests: static task-relevant geometry should track loss at a common budget, while multiscale geometry should track coupling-specific exponent ordering, including reversal across contrasting tasks. An audit of released emergence trajectories identifies the controls needed for a direct factorial test that measures geometry separately from the scaling fit.

</details>


### [153] [WeatherNext 3: Increasing resolution and performance of global weather models with raw observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03582)
*Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Dominic Masters,Andrew El-Kadi,Samier Merchant,Guy Shalev,Ilan Price,Fred Zyda,Remi Lam,Sasha Shysheya,Matthew Willson,Stratis Markou,Shreya Agrawal,Suhani Vora,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Sunny Mak,Tom R. Andersson,Megan Bela,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Ferran Alet,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherNext 3是一个新型AI天气预测模型，通过每小时更新、0.1度分辨率、融合卫星与站点观测数据，克服了现有AI天气模型在时空分辨率和数据初始化方面的局限，在中期概率预报技能上建立了新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的AI天气模型存在两个关键缺陷：一是其预测的时空分辨率低于最好的基于物理的模型；二是它们仅用分析数据初始化和训练，无法直接利用观测数据，且会继承分析数据的偏差。这限制了AI天气模型的性能和应用范围。

Method: WeatherNext 3通过三项核心改进解决问题：(1)每小时生成新预报（传统全球模型为6小时），利用低延迟地球静止卫星数据；(2)时空分辨率与物理模型对齐，采用小时级时间步和0.1度空间分辨率（含太阳辐射和云量）；(3)超越传统分析变量，学习预测卫星衍生的降水估计、热带气旋和站点观测，利用稀疏站点数据建模，在任意地点和时间生成2米温度和露点预测，并融合当地地理特征。

Result: WeatherNext 3建立了中期概率预报技能的新最先进水平。其2米温度和露点预测在任意地点和时间均显著优于竞争性全球模型，即使在未见站点上评估也是如此。

Conclusion: WeatherNext 3使操作级AI天气预报不再仅仅模拟传统上分离的数据同化、预报和后处理阶段，而是整合这些能力，进一步推动了全球天气预测的性能和细粒度边界。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI weather models have shown impressive medium-range forecast skill and computational efficiency, but suffer two key shortcomings: their forecasts have lower spatial and temporal resolution than the best physics-based models and they are exclusively initialized with and trained on analysis data. As a result, they cannot directly make use of observations, and any biases in the analysis are inherited by the forecast. WeatherNext 3 addresses these shortcomings and establishes a new state-of-the-art for probabilistic medium-range forecasting skill. First, WeatherNext 3 generates new forecasts every hour (rather than every 6 hours like traditional global models) by ingesting low-latency geostationary satellite data. Second, WeatherNext 3's temporal and spatial resolution are on par with physics-based global models, with hourly time steps and 0.1 degree resolution for single-level variables, including solar radiation and cloud cover. Third, WeatherNext 3 moves beyond traditional analysis variables by learning to predict satellite-derived precipitation estimates, as well as tropical cyclone and station observations. Modelling sparse station data allows WeatherNext 3 to make 2m temperature and dewpoint predictions at any location and time, conditioned on local geographical features, with substantially lower error than competing global models, even when evaluated against unseen stations. Together, WeatherNext 3's capabilities move operational AI-based weather forecasting beyond emulating the traditionally distinct stages of data assimilation, forecasting and post-processing, which helps to further push the frontier of performance and granularity for global weather prediction.

</details>


### [154] [Neural-Network Maxent: a general extension with learned nonlinearity, applied to time-series for Desert Locust distribution modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03603)
*Alessandro Grassi,Edoardo Kimani Bellotto,Wassim El Azami,Sabrina Outmani,Maximilien Houel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN Maxent，一种将Maxent框架中的固定特征字典替换为GRU神经网络的扩展方法，用于沙漠蝗虫栖息地适宜性建模。该方法使用50天环境时序数据（ERA5 Land、MODIS、Sentinel 3）并保持7天预测间隔，相比标准Maxent在ROC AUC（0.862 vs 0.792）和F1（0.671 vs 0.590）上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Maxent依赖人工选择的线性特征组合，无法捕捉生态监测中常见的时间序列协变量（如降水、土壤湿度、植被指数）的非线性时序关系。标准实现将时序数据展平为独立特征，丢弃了关键的序列结构信息。

Method: RNN Maxent将Maxent框架中的固定特征字典替换为GRU神经网络，通过反向传播进行端到端训练。该方法保留了Maxent的存在数据统计基础、背景归一化和概率校准，仅在非线性特征学习方面由数据驱动而非预先固定。实验使用50天环境时序数据，并设置7天间隔实现预测行为。

Result: 相比标准Maxent，RNN Maxent在多项指标上表现更优：ROC AUC从0.792提升至0.862（标准差0.036 vs 0.056），F1从0.590提升至0.671（标准差0.056 vs 0.056）。

Conclusion: RNN Maxent通过在Maxent框架中引入可学习的非线性时序特征提取能力，有效改善了沙漠蝗虫栖息地适宜性预测的准确性，验证了深度学习架构与经典生态位模型的结合潜力。

Abstract: Species Distribution Modelling (SDM) is essential for understanding how environmental conditions shape biodiversity, particularly for destructive pests such as the Desert Locust (Schistocerca gregaria), whose breeding dynamics are tightly coupled to rapidly evolving environmental conditions. Maxent has become the dominant method for presence-only data, but its reliance on a linear combination of hand chosen feature transforms limits its ability to capture the nonlinear, temporal relationships common in ecological monitoring, where covariates such as precipitation, soil moisture, and vegetation indices evolve meaningfully over time. Standard implementations flatten time-series covariates into independent features, discarding sequential structure that carries critical signal. We introduce RNN Maxent, an extension of the Maxent framework that replaces the fixed feature dictionary with a neural network, specifically a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), trained end to end via backpropagation. The approach preserves Maxent's presence only statistical foundations, background normalization, and probability calibration, differing only in that the nonlinearity is learned from data rather than fixed in advance. We apply RNN Maxent to map suitable habitat for the Desert Locust using 50 day environmental time series derived from ERA5 Land, MODIS, and Sentinel 3, maintaining a 7 day gap between covariates and presence records to yield forecasting behavior. Compared against standard Maxent, RNN Maxent improves performance across metrics (ROC AUC 0.862 std 0.036 vs. 0.792; F1 0.671 std 0.056 vs. 0.590).

</details>


### [155] [On the Interaction Between Model Compression and Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03604)
*Francesco Corti,Dong Wang,Young D. Kwon,Cecilia Mascolo,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了结构化压缩对深度神经网络在分布偏移下测试时自适应（TTA）能力的影响。研究发现即使压缩模型在监督自适应下保持高精度，其TTA性能仍随压缩程度增加而显著下降，根源在于表征多样性降低和结构约束限制了可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在实际部署中需要兼顾高效性与适应性，模型压缩和测试时自适应（TTA）各自已被广泛研究，但两者之间的交互机制尚未被充分理解，缺乏系统性的分析。

Method: 使用ResNet-18和ViT-Base作为基础模型，在CIFAR-10-C和ImageNet-C基准上，将多种压缩方法与标准TTA技术相结合进行评估。引入了一个诊断框架，分别考察表征表达能力和自适应子空间兼容性两个维度。

Result: 实验结果揭示了一致性的性能差距：尽管压缩模型在监督自适应下仍能保持高精度，但随着压缩程度的增加，其TTA性能显著下降。造成这一现象的原因是压缩导致的表征多样性降低以及结构性约束限制了模型的可恢复能力，且这些效应强烈依赖于所采用的压缩方法。

Conclusion: 模型压缩与TTA之间存在非平凡的交互作用，压缩方法的选择对模型的适应能力有显著影响。未来需要设计能够保留模型适应性的压缩策略，而不仅仅是追求精度与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed in the wild must be both efficient and adaptable, requiring model compression and test-time adaptation (TTA). While both are well studied in isolation, their interaction remains poorly understood. We systematically analyze how structured compression affects a model's ability to adapt under distribution shift. Using ResNet-18 and ViT-Base on CIFAR-10-C and ImageNet-C, we evaluate multiple compression methods combined with standard TTA techniques. We introduce a diagnostic framework that examines representational expressivity and adaptation subspace compatibility. Our results reveal a consistent gap: although compressed models retain high accuracy under supervised adaptation, their TTA performance degrades significantly with increasing compression. We show that this stems from reduced representational diversity and structural constraints that limit recoverability. These effects strongly depend on the compression method, highlighting the need to design compression strategies that preserve adaptability.

</details>


### [156] [Local Updates, Global Learning (LUGL): Playing Games with non-incremental Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03660)
*David Milec,Spyridon Samothrakis,Michael Fairbank,Dennis J. N. J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LUGL框架，将数据收集与模型拟合解耦，使梯度提升树（如LightGBM）等非增量学习器能够在强化学习的自我对弈训练中发挥作用。该框架在四个完美信息游戏和五个不完美信息游戏中表现出与DQN和DeepCFR相当或更优的性能，挑战了神经网络在游戏智能体中的主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在强化学习中占据主导地位，主要因为其增量学习能力适合在线、非平稳的自我对弈训练。然而，梯度提升树在表格数据的监督学习中通常优于神经网络，而游戏状态本质上具有表格结构（离散动作、分类卡牌身份、结构化棋盘位置），因此树模型理论上应适合游戏学习，但目前缺乏让树模型在RL中有效工作的框架。

Method: LUGL框架通过两个交替阶段工作：1) 局部更新阶段——智能体进行自我对弈游戏，在有限表中累积表格更新（Q值、V值、策略或遗憾值）；2) 全局学习阶段——用积累的表格训练函数逼近器，使其泛化到未见过的状态，然后重置表格。该框架使非增量学习器能够规避因分布漂移而无法在RL中工作的问题。

Result: 在四个完美信息游戏（井字棋、四子棋、奥赛罗棋、六边形棋）和五个不完美信息游戏（Kuhn扑克、Leduc Hold'em、Liar's Dice、Goofspiel、Flop5 Hold'em）中，基于LightGBM的智能体表现与DQN和DeepCFR相当或更优，在所有测试基准上均展现出竞争力。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明社区对神经网络在游戏智能体中的强烈偏好可能缺乏依据，基于LightGBM的智能体在所有测试基准上均达到竞争性或更优的性能，表明树模型在表格结构的游戏环境中具有显著的潜力。

Abstract: The dominance of Neural Networks (NNs) in RL is partially due to their incremental learning capability, which naturally suits the online, non-stationary nature of self-play training. However, gradient-boosted trees like LightGBM are widely recognised as the state of the art for tabular data in supervised learning, often outperforming NNs in accuracy and efficiency. Game states are inherently tabular---discrete actions, categorical card identities, structured board positions---which makes them an ideal candidate for tree-based methods. We introduce LUGL (Local Updates, Global Learning), a framework that decouples data collection from model fitting, enabling non-incremental learners such as GBTs to operate in RL settings where they would otherwise fail due to distributional shift. LUGL alternates between a local updates phase, where the agent plays self-play games and accumulates tabular updates (Q-values, V-values, policies, or regret values) in a finite table, and a global learning phase, where the table is used to train a function approximator that generalises to unseen states before the table is reset. We test our approach in four standard perfect-information games (Tic-tac-toe, Connect-4, Othello, and Hex) and five imperfect-information games (Kuhn's poker, Leduc Hold'em, Liar's Dice, Goofspiel, and Flop5 Hold'em), and show that our results are competitive with or superior to DQN and DeepCFR. Our experiments demonstrate that the community's strong bias towards NNs in game-playing may be unwarranted, since LightGBM-based agents achieve competitive or superior performance across all tested benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [Extracting Forgotten Prompts from Targeted Unlearned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03662)
*Au Ashley Hoi-Ting,Meghdad Kurmanji,William F. Shen,Nicholas D. Lane,Ligang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个新的机器遗忘漏洞：现有的遗忘攻击假设对手已知被遗忘的提示词，只需恢复其答案；但本文提出的TAS攻击表明，攻击者仅凭保留数据和模型的黑盒访问权限，就能提取被遗忘的提示词本身。TAS在有限查询预算下先用模板和实体池识别被遗忘的实体，再实例化模板重建被遗忘的提示词，在三个数据集、三个LLM和三种遗忘方法上实现了100%的实体恢复准确率和高达95%的提示词重建率，同时比朴素探测少用99.7%的查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘方法（如NPO、DPO、LUNAR）通过拒绝对齐来压制被遗忘数据，但已有研究表明拒绝式响应会留下遗忘痕迹，且现有攻击能恢复部分被遗忘知识。然而，所有现有攻击均假设对手已知被遗忘的提示词，只需恢复答案。本文挑战了这一假设，首次证明被遗忘的提示词本身也可以被提取，揭示了一个新的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出的TAS（Targeted Active Search）攻击分为两个阶段：1）通过构建规范模板和实体池，在有限查询预算下利用最具信息量的模板-实体组合主动查询模型，识别被遗忘的实体；2）用识别出的实体实例化提示模板，探测未学习模型，重建被遗忘的提示词。该方法利用保留数据进行引导，并结合主动学习策略优化查询效率。

Result: 在三种遗忘方法（NPO、DPO、LUNAR）、三个数据集和三个LLM上的实验表明：TAS能以100%的准确率恢复被遗忘的实体，重建多达95%的被遗忘提示词，同时比朴素探测方法减少高达99.7%的查询次数。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了机器遗忘中的一个新漏洞——被遗忘的提示词本身可被提取，而非仅其答案。这一发现表明当前的遗忘方法在隐私保护上仍存在根本性缺陷，需要开发更强的防御机制来防止提示词和实体的泄露，而不仅仅是防止答案恢复。

Abstract: Recent unlearning methods (e.g. NPO, DPO, LUNAR) make use of refusal alignment to suppress forgotten data. However, it has been shown that refusal responses might leave traces of unlearning, and recent attacks have been able to successfully recover some of the unlearned knowledge. In this paper, we uncover a new vulnerability. Existing attacks typically assume that the forgotten prompts are already known to the adversary and focus on recovering their answers. However, we show that the forgotten prompts themselves can be extracted by using the retained data and black-box access to the model. Our attack, Targeted Active Search (TAS), first identifies the forgotten entities by constructing canonical templates and entity pool, and selectively querying the model using the most informative template-entity pair under a limited query budget. Once the entities are identified, TAS instantiates prompt templates with those entities to probe the unlearned model and reconstruct the forgotten prompts. Experiments across three unlearning methods with three datasets and three LLMs shows that TAS recovers the forgotten entity with $100\%$ accuracy and reconstructs up to $95\%$ of forgotten prompts, all while using up to $99.7\%$ fewer queries than naive probing.

</details>


### [158] [Out-of-Distribution Generalisation with Sequence Models in Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03667)
*Oussama Hidaoui,Omer Ebead,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Siddarth Singh,Juan Claude Formanek,Felix Chalumeau,Omayma Mahjoub,Sasha Abramowitz,Ruan John de Kock,Wiem Khlifi,Louay Ben Nessir,Simon Verster Du Toit,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Asim Awad Osman,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Refiloe Shabe,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离线多智能体强化学习中零样本任务泛化问题，通过扩展序列建模架构来应对多任务观测/动作空间和可变智能体数量，发现任务多样性（而非数据集规模）是实现稳健零样本迁移的主导因素，在四个环境中平均性能提升3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MARL中泛化到未见过的任务仍是一个基础性挑战，以往研究缺乏对任务多样性、数据集规模和网络容量等缩放规律的系统性分析和实证研究。

Method: 扩展离线序列建模架构以处理多任务观测和动作空间以及跨任务的可变智能体数量，并在四个具有挑战性的环境（Connector、RWARE、SMAX、LBF）中进行大规模实验，系统研究任务多样性、数据集大小和网络容量的缩放行为。

Result: 任务多样性是决定零样本迁移鲁棒性的主导因素；多任务方法在保留测试任务上平均比单任务模型提升3.2倍，并持续优于强行为克隆基线。

Conclusion: 开发可泛化的MARL智能体应优先考虑包含不同智能体数量的训练分布多样性，为离线MARL的有效扩展提供了路线图。

Abstract: Generalising to unseen tasks remains a fundamental challenge in offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). In this work, we present a principled analysis of zero-shot task generalisation in the offline setting and conduct an extensive empirical investigation into the scaling behaviour governing task diversity, dataset size, and network capacity. To facilitate this study, we extend offline sequence modelling architectures to handle multi-task observation and action spaces alongside variable agent counts across tasks. Our primary finding is that scaling task diversity---rather than sheer dataset size is the dominant factor in achieving robust zero-shot transfer. Through large-scale experiments across four challenging environments (Connector, RWARE, SMAX, and LBF), we demonstrate that our multi-task approach achieves a mean improvement of 3.2x on held-out test tasks compared to single-task models and consistently outperforms strong behaviour cloning baselines. These results suggest that the development of generalisable MARL agents should prioritise the diversity of the training distribution with varying numbers of agents, providing a roadmap for scaling offline MARL effectively.

</details>


### [159] [Resolution-Aware Experimental Design under Partial Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03686)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"分辨率感知实验设计"(RAED)的新方法，该方法选择能够产生最小期望非空结构候选集（在控制错误排除的前提下）的实验，以应对部分可识别性下持续存在的干扰不确定性。作者证明了交叉干扰混淆分离定理，表明在某些情况下，传统信息准则偏好的实验可能具有任意更差的有效结构分辨率。RAED保持了复合Blackwell比较下的期望排序，并通过学习式评分实现，具备有限样本校准和尾部保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的实验设计通常以期望信息量最大化为目标，但在部分可识别（partial identifiability）情况下，持续的干扰不确定性会导致相同的观测数据携带不同的结构含义，此时传统信息增益准则可能无法有效地消除结构不确定性，需要一种能直接针对结构分辨率进行优化的实验设计准则。

Method: 提出RAED准则，选择最小期望非空结构候选集且满足错误排除控制的实验；证明交叉干扰混淆分离定理；开发基于学习的评分实现，具有有限样本干扰平均和正尾部校准；刻画稀有尾部样本复杂度障碍。

Result: 在约束传感下，两个地下水流基准实验中RAED与传统期望信息增益(EIG)的实验选择存在真实分歧；河流冲积基准中尾部保护改变了所选物理实验并将硬区域错误排除转化为显式模糊；甲烷氧化基准中，预先设定的5%错误排除容差实现了尾部敏感干扰风险的有限样本总体保证，三个结构族具有95%联合置信度。

Conclusion: 实验可以与全潜在信息增益、平均分类和干扰边缘化信息度量的偏好相悖，却具有任意更差的有效结构分辨率，因此实验设计应直接针对结构分辨率进行优化，RAED提供了一种可操作且具有理论保证的实现方案。

Abstract: Experimental design is commonly framed as choosing the experiment expected to provide the most information. Under partial identifiability however, persistent nuisance uncertainty can make the same observation carry different structural meanings. We introduce Resolution-Aware Experimental Design (RAED), which selects an experiment by the smallest expected nonempty structural candidate set achievable subject to false-exclusion control. We prove an exact cross-nuisance aliasing separation: an experiment can be preferred by structural and full-latent information gain, average classification, and nuisance-marginalized informativeness while having arbitrarily poorer valid structural resolution. RAED nevertheless preserves the expected ordering under a genuine composite Blackwell comparison. To make this criterion operational, we develop a learned score-based implementation with finite-sample nuisance-average and positive-tail calibration, and characterize a rare-tail sample-complexity obstruction. Under constrained sensing, two subsurface-flow benchmarks exhibit genuine RAED--expected-information-gain (EIG) experiment-selection disagreements, with the clearest and largest held-out resolution differences in WCA. In a fluvial benchmark, tail protection changes the selected physical experiment and replaces hard-region false exclusions primarily with explicit ambiguity. In a mechanistic methane-oxidation benchmark, a prospectively specified 5\% false-exclusion tolerance also yields a nontrivial finite-sample population guarantee for tail-sensitive nuisance risk, with 95\% joint confidence across all three structural families.

</details>


### [160] [Federated Causal Discovery via Regression-Directed Cumulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03705)
*Pablo Torrijos,Fabio Stella,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedRCD系列联邦因果发现算法，基于高阶累积量张量解决LiNGAM模型在联邦环境下的因果发现问题，克服了现有FedISHC在近对称噪声下失效的局限，并揭示了累积量方法在实际中实际上按DAG有向路径上的方差阶梯排序而非总体不对称性排序的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiNGAM因果模型在联邦环境中的应用面临挑战：数据稀缺且因GDPR等法规不宜集中化，而标准集中式估计器DirectLiNGAM无法直接联邦化。现有基于高阶累积量的联邦方法FedISHC在近对称噪声条件下失效，需要新的方法克服这一局限。

Method: 引入FedRCD系列算法，利用高阶累积量张量仅依赖变量联合分布且在独立样本组间可精确相加的特性，实现单轮通信即可完成水平、垂直和混合分区的联邦学习。包含三个变体：两个是集中式高阶累积量（HC）和HC-LiNGAM算法的精确联邦对应物，以及支持任意粒度精确反学习的单轮变体。

Result: 数值实验表明，在真实部署的典型样本量下，整个基于累积量的联邦方法族实际上不是按总体不对称性排序变量，而是按DAG沿有向路径诱导的方差阶梯（即累积量版本的varsortability）排序。边际标准化会使所有累积量方法崩溃为近随机排序，而尺度不变的DirectLiNGAM不受影响但无法在该协议下联邦化。

Conclusion: FedRCD家族为联邦LiNGAM因果发现提供了可行的新方案，支持高效的精确反学习，但同时揭示了累积量联邦方法在有限样本下存在与varsortability类似的排序偏差问题，这对其实际应用提出了重要警示。

Abstract: In this paper we study linear non-Gaussian acyclic models (LiNGAM) when used in federated environments. These causal models allow one to go beyond Markov equivalence. However, in many domains data are scarce, and increasing the sample size by centralising data from different clients is not advisable due to regulations such as the GDPR. The federated environment offers an attractive option to balance privacy and causal discovery accuracy. Unfortunately, the standard centralised estimator in the LiNGAM setting, i.e., DirectLiNGAM, cannot be straightforwardly federated. Higher-order cumulant tensors offer a way around this obstacle: they depend only on the joint distribution of the variables involved and add exactly across independent sample groups, so a single communication round suffices in horizontal, vertical, and hybrid partitions.
  However, FedISHC, i.e., the current federated method along these lines, breaks down under near-symmetric noise. To overcome the above limitation, we introduce the FedRCD family of causal discovery algorithms, and investigate three variants that trade off communication rounds against algebraic noise; two of them are exact federated counterparts of the centralised high-order cumulant (HC) and HC-LiNGAM algorithms, and the single-round variants further effectively support exact unlearning at any granularity, from a single observation to a whole client. Numerical experiments show that at sample sizes typical of real deployments, the entire cumulant-based federated family does not actually rank variables by the population asymmetry that the scores encode at zero. It ranks them by a variance ladder induced by the DAG along its directed paths, the cumulant counterpart of varsortability. Marginal standardisation collapses every cumulant method to near-random ordering, while scale-invariant DirectLiNGAM, not federable under this protocol, is unaffected.

</details>


### [161] [Projected Riemannian Gradient Descent for the Bures-Wasserstein Barycenter: Dimension-Independent Linear Convergence at Unit Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03762)
*A. Afham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了Bures-Wasserstein质心计算中单位步长Riemannian梯度下降（RGD）收敛保证的维度依赖困境，提出投影RGD算法，在单位步长下实现维度无关的线性收敛，速率为(1-κ^{-3/2})，优于现有小步长保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bures-Wasserstein质心计算在机器学习、最优传输和量子信息中广泛应用。实践中使用的单位步长RGD收敛很快，但现有理论分析存在两难：单位步长的保证带有最坏情况下的维度指数依赖，而维度无关的保证则需要小步长，牺牲了实际速度。

Method: 提出Projected RGD算法，其核心是新颖的投影引理：将正定矩阵的特征值裁剪到区间[α, β]是到集合{S: αI ≤ S ≤ βI}的闭式、非扩张（1-Lipschitz）BW度量投影。该投影是"免费"的，因为它复用了下一步迭代必须进行的特征分解。分析还覆盖了Brahmachari等人的不变矩阵投影问题，将其不动点算法识别为全测地子流形上的单位步长RGD。

Result: Projected RGD实现了维度无关的线性收敛，速率为(1-κ^{-3/2})，其中κ为集合的条件数。该速率在小步长最优保证上实现了多项式改进（迭代复杂度从κ^{5/2}提升到κ^{3/2}），且投影和未投影迭代每步成本相同。

Conclusion: 通过投影而非改进单位步长RGD的保证，解析了现有理论的二元对立。投影引理本身是一个独立的贡献，展示了该分析框架可扩展到其他相关设置。关键洞察是：投影步骤不增加计算开销，因为特征分解在下一步中无论如何都需要。

Abstract: The computation of the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) barycenter of an ensemble of positive definite matrices arises throughout machine learning, optimal transport, and quantum information. Riemannian gradient descent (RGD) at unit step size -- the fixed-point iteration used in practice -- converges rapidly, yet existing analyses present a dichotomy: unit-step guarantees carry worst-case exponential dependence on the dimension, while dimension-independent guarantees require small step sizes that forfeit the empirical speed. We resolve this dichotomy, not by improving the guarantees for unit-step RGD, but by proposing a Projected RGD algorithm that achieves dimension-independent linear convergence at unit step size. The achieved rate, $(1 - κ^{-3/2})$, where $κ$ is the condition number of the ensemble, also polynomially improves on the best small-step guarantee ($κ^{3/2}$ versus $κ^{5/2}$ iteration complexity). The crux is a novel Projection Lemma: clipping the eigenvalues of a positive matrix to an interval $[α, β]$ is the closed-form, non-expansive (1-Lipschitz) BW-metric projection onto the set $\{S : αI \leq S \leq βI\}$ -- a statement which, unlike its known one-sided counterpart, does not follow from convexity. The projection is moreover free: it reuses an eigendecomposition the next iteration must perform in any case, so the projected and unprojected iterations cost the same per step. The same analysis covers the invariant matrix projection problem of Brahmachari et al. (2025), whose fixed-point algorithm we identify as unit-step RGD on a totally geodesic submanifold, thereby extending the dimension-independent guarantee to that setting verbatim.

</details>


### [162] [From Nowcasting to Forecasting: Adapting a Reanalysis-Trained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03763)
*Mikko Partio,Leila Hieta,Ossi Laine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCast v2，一种用于12小时云量预报的机器学习模型，采用观测初始条件，首先在再分析数据上训练学习云演化动态，然后通过条件流匹配生成方法将模型适配到卫星观测云场。该模型相比前代CloudCast v1将平均绝对误差降低了10%，并在3-6小时后超越前代的分数技能评分，证明观测初始化机器学习预报可超越传统的1-3小时临近预报范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期云量预报在最初几小时能保持观测到的云的位置，但随着云场通过生成、消散和变形演化，其技能下降。更长的预报时效需要考虑大气演化，但业务数值天气预报(NWP)在初始化时可能无法准确表示卫星观测到的云状态。因此需要一种既能利用观测初始条件、又能学习云演化动力学的方法来扩展云量预报的时效范围。

Method: CloudCast v2采用两阶段训练策略：首先在哥白尼欧洲区域再分析(Ridal2024)数据上训练，以学习云演化动力学；然后使用条件流匹配(Lipman2023)，一种将噪声转化为云量预报的生成方法，以观测到的初始云场和NWP输入为条件，将模型适配到卫星衍生的云场。

Result: CloudCast v2在1-12小时范围内相比前代CloudCast v1将平均绝对误差降低了10%。在分数技能评分（一种基于邻域的空间一致性度量）上，CloudCast v2根据云量类别在约3-6小时后超越CloudCast v1。

Conclusion: 结果表明，观测初始化驱动的机器学习云量预报能够将预报时效扩展到传统的1-3小时临近预报范围之外，同时保留来自卫星云场的空间细节，为云量预报提供了有效的长时效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate cloud-cover forecasts are important for temperature prediction, radiation forecasting, and solar-power operations. Short-range forecasting methods can preserve observed cloud placement during the first forecast hours, but their skill decreases when cloud fields evolve through formation, dissipation and deformation. Longer lead times require accounting for atmospheric evolution, but operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts may not accurately represent the satellite-observed cloud state at initialization. We develop CloudCast v2, a machine-learning model for 12-hour cloud-cover forecasting from observation-based initial conditions. The model is first trained on the Copernicus European Regional Reanalysis (Ridal2024) to learn cloud-evolution dynamics, and is then adapted to satellite-derived cloud fields using conditional flow matching (Lipman2023), a generative method that transforms noise into cloud-cover forecasts conditioned on the observed initial cloud fields and NWP inputs. CloudCast v2 reduces mean absolute error by 10% relative to its predecessor, CloudCast v1 (Partio2025), over the 1-12 h range. It also overtakes CloudCast v1 in fractions skill score, a neighborhood-based measure of spatial agreement, after approximately 3-6 h, depending on the cloudiness category. These results show that observation-initialized machine-learning forecasts can extend beyond the usual 1-3-hour nowcasting range while retaining spatial detail from satellite cloud fields.

</details>


### [163] [OBER+: Continuity-Aware Reporting and Traceable Continuous Improvement in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03770)
*Elakkiya Rajasekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OBER+，一个扩展开发的学习成果达成平台，通过五个关联阶段将"测得的学习差距"转化为"评估后的纠正措施"，并以规则化计算方式支持基于成果的教育中的决策制定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于成果的教育（OBE）机构通常例行计算学习成果达成度，但课程分析综述显示缺乏证据表明这些计算结果如何用于指导实际决策。现有平台仅计算达成度，却不连接测量结果与后续行动，存在从数据到决策的空白。

Method: OBER+平台包含五个关联阶段：（1）跨课程授课累积成果达成数据；（2）发出学习差距及持续性差距的信号；（3）按监管者已使用的截止值对差距进行分级；（4）将决策记录到带有证据注释的实践目录中；（5）记录变更并量化差距随后的变动。另外设计了一条规则来比较成果陈述的连续版本，确保成果改动时达成度不跨越变更点被误读为连续序列。

Result: 在两条真实课程的实时记录上应用该规则产生了三项结果：一是核心课程的每项成果在连续授课间均被实质性重新定义，内容在不同成果编号间移动，若简单解读会产生25点的虚假崩塌；二是按文档化规则重新计算平台数据，发现十项中有六项偏差超出舍入误差范围，模式指向一个已上报给机构的缺陷；三是十五对陈述对比中五对逐字符相同，剩余十对中有六对中相同编号的成果与早期不同编号的成果最为相似，此结果依赖相似度排序，无需阈值或标签。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为基于成果的报告提供了一种计算设计，表述为任何成果达成平台都可实施的规则，并用真实机构记录中的证据展示了这些规则能够揭示什么。

Abstract: Institutions practising outcome-based education compute learning outcome attainment routinely, while reviews of curriculum analytics report an absence of evidence on how that computation informs decisions. This paper presents OBER+, an extension of a deployed institutional attainment platform that computes the step from a measured shortfall to an evaluated corrective action. Five connected stages accumulate attainment across deliveries of a course, signal a shortfall and a persistent shortfall, grade it on cutoffs the regulator already uses, record the decision against a catalogue of practices annotated with their evidence, log the change, and quantify the subsequent movement in the shortfall. A further rule compares successive statements of an outcome, so attainment is never read as a series across a point at which the outcome changed. Applying the rules to the live record of two real courses produced three results.
