Every outcome of a core course was substantively redefined between consecutive deliveries, with subject matter moving between outcome numbers, so a naive reading would have reported a twenty-five point collapse between quantities that do not refer to the same learning. Recomputing the platform's figures from its documented rule showed six of ten differing by more than rounding explains, in a pattern that identified a defect since reported to the institution. Across fifteen statement pairs from three transitions, five were identical character for character, and among the ten that were not, the outcome carrying a given number was nearest to a differently numbered earlier outcome in six, a result resting on an ordering of similarities and requiring no threshold and no labelling. The contribution is a computational design for outcome-based reporting, stated as rules any attainment platform can implement, with evidence of what they make visible in a live institutional record.

</details>


### [164] [Landmark-Based Discrimination of Injury-Associated Athlete-Sessions from Minute-Resolution Multimodal Football Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03790)
*Evangelos Chatzidimitriou,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定地标、每个运动员-会话一个表征的建模框架，解决运动员监测数据中分钟级数据与整体会话级损伤标签之间的粒度不匹配问题。基于2020 SoccerMon数据（3,743个运动员-会话），在多个时间地标上评估不同表征的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动员监测数据以分钟级记录，而损伤标签仅存在于会话级别（整场会话是否相关），两者的粒度不匹配导致难以直接将分钟级数据与损伤结果关联。现有方法会给每一分钟都赋予相同的会话级标签，这隐含了在每时每刻损伤状态已知的错误假设，因为会话内部的损伤发生时间实际上是未知的。

Method: 提出固定地标（fixed-landmark）方法：在每个地标时间点（如10、20、30分钟）构建每个运动员-会话的单一表征，使用截至该时间点的观测数据。目标保持在会话级别，避免不合理的分钟级监督。评估了会前（pre-session）、累积（cumulative）、动态（dynamic）和组合（combined）四类表征，并采用运动员不相交验证、运动员聚类自助不确定性、共同队列敏感性分析等多种稳健性检验，对比Logistic Regression、Random Forest和XGBoost三种基准模型。

Result: 基于2020 SoccerMon数据，包含48名精英女子足球运动员的3,743个运动员-会话，其中22个损伤相关会话来自5名运动员。主要CUM+DYN Logistic Regression模型在各时间地标上的ROC-AUC为0.367-0.607，PR-AUC为0.0080-0.0150，不确定性区间宽。包含会前信息（PRE）的表征在多个地标上点估计更高，但结果仍具较大不确定性。

Conclusion: 该固定地标框架有效地解决了数据粒度不匹配问题，为运动员监测中的会话级损伤建模提供了更严谨的途径。然而，由于损伤事件数量有限（仅22个损伤相关会话），模型判别性能整体有限且不确定性较大，表明在该小样本、类别不平衡场景下进行损伤预测仍具有显著挑战。

Abstract: Athlete monitoring data may be recorded minute by minute throughout a match or training session, while injury information may only indicate whether the entire session was injury-associated.
  This creates a modelling problem: assigning the same session-level label to every minute would imply that injury status is known at each exact time, even though within-session injury onset is unknown.
  Our novelty is a fixed-landmark, one-representation-per-athlete-session formulation that directly addresses this mismatch. Instead of labelling every minute, we construct one representation per athlete-session at each landmark using information observed up to that point. This keeps the target at the session level and avoids unsupported minute-level injury supervision.
  A landmark is a fixed time point within the same session, such as 10, 20, or 30 minutes. At each landmark, we assess whether the whole session is injury-associated or non-injury-associated and examine how discrimination changes as more within-session information becomes available.
  Using 2020 SoccerMon data, we analyse 3,743 athlete-sessions from 48 elite women's football athletes, including 22 injury-associated sessions from five athletes. We evaluate pre-session, cumulative, dynamic, and combined representations with athlete-disjoint validation, athlete-cluster bootstrap uncertainty, common-cohort sensitivity analysis, alternative negative-athlete fold allocations, equal-athlete weighting, and Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and XGBoost benchmarks.
  Primary CUM+DYN Logistic Regression yields ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607 and PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150 across landmarks, with wide uncertainty. PRE-containing representations show higher point estimates at several landmarks but remain uncertain.

</details>


### [165] [From Ordered Bernoulli Levels to Critical-Line Geometry: Integer Quantization, Bernoulli Residual Phase, and Prime-Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03801)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有序伯努利词核f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k)及其逆整数水平集生成的几何结构。二进制水平2^(-n)唯一锚定p=1/2，经互补保持的复延拓得到共轭对称的垂直几何。通过二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)的整数量化，将临界线零点纵坐标gamma_k诱导的水平精确分解为整数部分与周期第一伯努利残差，再通过圆化映射将gamma_k^2 mod 1作为残差相位变量。唯一分解将整数壳层分解为素数发生器坐标，并用复指数将同一构造提升到狄利克雷原子，连接狄利克雷级数与欧拉乘积。文中明确声明不声称证明黎曼猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在从组合概率核（伯努利词核）出发，探索其水平集几何与临界线零点结构之间的深层数学联系，试图在不依赖ζ函数先验输入的情况下建立对称几何框架，并为理解zeta函数零点的模1分布提供一个新的组合视角。

Method: (1) 从有序伯努利词核f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k)出发，分析二进制水平2^(-n)唯一选定的实数锚点p=1/2；(2) 通过互补保持的复数延拓得到共轭对称对z=1/2+iu和1-z=1/2-iu，构建垂直几何；(3) 引入二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2并建立精确整数量化；(4) 对临界线零点纵坐标gamma_k的水平L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2做最近整数分解L_k=N_k+delta_k；(5) 通过圆化映射Z_k=exp(2πi delta_k)分离gamma_k^2 mod 1作为残差相位；(6) 用唯一分解处理整数壳层为素数发生器坐标；(7) 用复指数s将构造提升至狄利克雷原子m^(-s)。

Result: 研究建立了伯努利词核水平集与临界线零点之间的精确分解关系：水平L_k可分解为最近整数加周期第一伯努利残差；圆化映射成功隔离gamma_k^2的模1相位；通过唯一分解得到整数壳层的素数生成器表示；狄利克雷原子通过复指数与同一构造连接。作者明确区分了精确恒等式、经典zeta连接、数值验证和条件性Weyl检验，并声明未声称证明黎曼猜想。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个从纯组合伯努利核出发的几何框架，在不引入ζ函数先验输入的情况下自然生成共轭对称结构，并通过整数量化与残差分解将零点的模1分布问题转化为可计算的几何条件。作者审慎地将开放性问题（如条件性Weyl检验）与已建立的精确结果分离，明确不主张对黎曼猜想的证明，该工作更可能被视为一种新的方法论工具而非最终定论。

Abstract: We study the ordered Bernoulli-word kernel f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) and the geometry generated by its inverse-integer level sets. The binary level 2^(-n) selects p=1/2 as the unique real split-independent anchor. Under complement-preserving complex continuation, the pair becomes z=1/2+iu and 1-z=1/2-iu, producing a conjugation-symmetric vertical geometry before any zeta-function input is introduced. The quadratic coordinate Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2 has a sharp minimum at the central point and admits an exact integer quantization. For critical-line zero ordinates gamma_k, the induced levels L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 are decomposed exactly as L_k=N_k+delta_k, where N_k is the nearest integer and delta_k is a periodic first-Bernoulli residual. Circularization gives Z_k=exp(2 pi i delta_k), isolating gamma_k^2 mod 1 as the residual phase variable. Unique factorization resolves the integer shells into prime-generator coordinates, while a distinct complex exponent s lifts the same construction to the Dirichlet atoms m^(-s), linking the Dirichlet-series and Euler-product assemblies. Exact identities, classical zeta connections, numerical controls, and open conditional Weyl tests are kept explicitly separate. No proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [166] [Free Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03807)
*John Langford,Nathan Godey,Giovanni Monea,Yoav Artzi,Harry Dong,Ying Fan,Gustavo de Rosa,Zheng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"自由暂停令牌"（free pause token）方法，通过并行预测流在权重共享骨干网络上提供额外计算以形成每个next-token预测，而非在序列中增加额外令牌。该方法在1B参数模型上提升了2-3个百分点的next-token预测准确率，且推理时几乎不增加上下文长度、KV缓存和延迟，唯一的主要成本在训练阶段（约1.14倍训练计算开销），实现了在等计算、等参数、等令牌条件下的标准transformer改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的暂停/思考令牌（pause/thinking token）虽然能为语言模型提供额外计算以改善预测质量，但会引入序列长度增加、KV缓存增长和推理延迟等额外成本。作者希望设计一种既能提供类似"思考"能力、又不会在推理时产生这些开销的方法，从而在不改变推理效率的前提下提升模型性能。

Method: 该方法引入了"自由暂停令牌"，它在权重共享的骨干网络上采用并行预测流来携带额外的计算，而不是作为序列中的额外令牌。该"暂停"依附于已有位置，因此在推理时不会增加上下文长度、KV缓存或明显延迟。训练时通过优化预训练流程将额外计算开销降至最低（低至1.14倍），同时保留大部分收益。

Result: 在1B参数模型上，该方法将next-token预测准确率提升了2-3个百分点。推理阶段几乎无额外延迟和存储开销，训练阶段的计算开销可控制在约1.14倍以内且大部分收益得以保留。

Conclusion: 该方法在等FLOPs、等参数规模和等令牌数量的条件下，相较于标准next-token训练的transformer实现了显著改进，是一种"免费"获得模型思考能力的高效方案。

Abstract: A free pause token gives a language model extra compute to form each next-token prediction (as a pause, or thinking, token does) but carries that compute in a parallel prediction stream over a weight-shared backbone rather than as an extra token in the sequence. It improves next-token prediction by 2-3 centinats in practice on a 1B parameter model. Because the pause rides an existing position instead of adding one, it is free to use: at inference it adds no context length, no KV cache, and essentially no latency with the growth in inference flops typically irrelevant as it is not the active bottleneck on throughput. The only primary cost is in training, where additional training compute versus an optimized pretraining pipeline is reduced to as low as x1.14 while preserving most of the benefits. The result is an isoflop, isoparameter, and isotoken improvement over standard next token trained transformers.

</details>


### [167] [A Peer-Relative Representation Learning Framework for Energy Inefficiency Identification in Mobile Network Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03809)
*Eliud Nyakweba Koto,Jaco du Toit,Adham Stoltz,Johan du Preez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的元胞相似性对比方法，通过在嵌入空间中引入基于能量的排斥机制，将有异常高能耗的站点与其相似邻居分离，从而在不依赖真实标签的情况下识别移动网络中能效低下的站点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络运营商的能源消耗是最大的运营支出之一，但站点级能效问题（如故障的冷却控制器、空闲无线电设备和寄生辅助负载）常因缺乏真实标注和历史测量数据中已嵌入低效模式而难以被检测。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于能量的最小失真嵌入（MDE）公式，在标准MDE目标中扩展了基于能量的排斥机制。该方法假设结构和运营特征相似的站点应具有可比的能耗，通过使能耗异常的站点在嵌入空间中偏离其局部邻域，同时保留结构相似性并编码能耗偏差，进而通过元胞相对比较生成异常评分。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法优于传统异常检测基线，并为大规模移动网络能效优化提供了稳健的基础。

Conclusion: 该方法为移动网络运营商提供了实用的站点优先级排序机制，使其能够将工程资源聚焦于最可能产生节能效果的站点，从而推动更有效的能效管理。

Abstract: Energy consumption is one of the largest operational expenditure items for mobile network operators, yet site-level energy inefficiencies such as faulty cooling controllers, idle radio equipment, and parasitic auxiliary loads often remain undetected because no ground-truth inefficiency labels exist and historical measurements may already contain embedded inefficiencies. This study proposes an unsupervised peer-relative approach based on the premise that sites with similar structural and operational characteristics should exhibit comparable energy consumption. To capture these relationships, a novel energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding (MDE) formulation is introduced that extends the standard MDE objective with an energy-based repulsion mechanism. This encourages sites with anomalously high energy consumption relative to comparable peers to become displaced from their local neighbourhoods in the embedding space. The resulting low-dimensional representation simultaneously preserves structural similarity and encodes energy-related deviations, enabling the identification of potentially inefficient sites through peer-relative comparison. The derived anomaly scores provide a practical mechanism for prioritising field investigations, allowing mobile network operators to focus engineering resources on sites most likely to yield energy savings. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional anomaly detection baselines and provides a robust foundation for large-scale energy-efficiency optimisation in mobile networks.

</details>


### [168] [Witnesses Explain Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03826)
*Lamine Diop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WAND，一种无需事后解释的可解释无监督表格异常检测器。WAND围绕单位球面上的方向组织计算，通过投影逃逸亚高斯极值基线来评分，标志点的"见证方向"本身就是特征归因解释，无需额外成本。在47个ADBench数据集上与16个无监督基线相比，WAND在ROC-AUC相当的情况下取得最优平均Friedman排名，其原生解释比SHAP/LIME及ECOD更准确和忠实，且查询成本仅为后者的零头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督异常检测器只给出分数但不解释哪些特征导致了异常，事后用SHAP或LIME补充解释需要对检测器进行成千上万次重新查询且只能近似逼近，计算开销大且解释不准确。

Method: WAND将计算组织在单位球面方向上，对每个点按其投影如何逃逸亚高斯极值基线进行评分。标出异常点的见证方向作为特征空间中的向量，天然构成逐特征归因解释；评分函数可微，解释也可通过梯度获得。时间复杂度关于样本规模为线性，并有探测效率界保证每个异常点都能获得见证方向和解释。

Result: 在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND在ROC-AUC与16个无监督基线持平的情况下取得最佳平均Friedman排名；其原生解释比事后SHAP/LIME和ECOD更准确、更忠实，且查询成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: WAND证明了解释性可以在不牺牲检测精度的情况下内置于检测器设计之中，是一种实用且可解释的异常检测解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection scores each point of an unlabelled, contaminated sample in a single pass, and increasingly must also explain why a point is flagged. Yet the dominant detectors give a score with no account of which features drive it, and explanations are bolted on post-hoc with SHAP or LIME, which re-query the detector thousands of times per point and only approximate it. We introduce WAND, an unsupervised tabular anomaly detector that is explainable by design. WAND organises its computation around directions on the unit sphere, scoring each point by how far its projection escapes a sub-Gaussian extreme-value baseline. The originality of our approach is that the witness directions that flag a point, being vectors in feature space, are its explanation, a per-feature attribution obtained at no cost over scoring and, since the score is differentiable, recoverable by gradients. Scoring is linear in the sample size, and a probe-efficiency bound guarantees every anomaly a witness, hence an explanation. Across 47 ADBench datasets WAND attains the best mean Friedman rank at ROC-AUC parity with 16 unsupervised baselines, so the gain is interpretability at no accuracy cost; its native explanations are more accurate and faithful than post-hoc SHAP/LIME and ECOD at a fraction of the query cost. WAND is thus a practical, interpretable solution for explainable anomaly detection.

</details>


### [169] [Multi-step Proximal Policy Improvement in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03842)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多步近端策略改进（MPI）方法，将离线强化学习的actor更新建模为概率流形上的梯度流离散化，通过多重定心近端步骤的组合实现可控的策略超越数据集支持范围的改进，并在D4RL基准上提升了多种强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临两难：策略更新既要贴近数据集支持的动作以保持价值估计可靠，又需要超越行为分布以获得有意义的性能提升。现有方法缺乏一个统一的几何视角来理解离线actor更新如何在这两个竞争目标之间取得平衡。

Method: 将策略建模为带度量几何的概率流形，把广泛的离线actor目标解释为单一近端策略改进步骤（SPI），即批评器定义能量诱导的流形梯度流的隐式离散化。在此基础上提出多步近端策略改进（MPI），通过组合顺序的定心近端步骤实现受控的策略改进，同时保持每步的近端约束，支持多种策略几何并适用于确定性和对角高斯策略。

Result: 在D4RL基准实验中，少量MPI精炼步骤在多个任务上显著提升了TD3+BC、ReBRAC和IQL等强离线基线方法的表现。进一步诊断实验区分了定心精炼与固定目标更新调度的差异，并刻画了批评器误差下的局限性。

Conclusion: MPI作为一种即插即用的精炼机制，能够在不引入复杂训练过程的前提下，通过几何流形上受控的多步近端更新来增强现有离线强化学习算法的策略改进能力，为离线策略优化提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) must reconcile two competing requirements: policy updates should stay near dataset-supported actions to keep value estimates reliable, yet meaningful gains often require moving beyond the behavior distribution. We develop a geometric view of offline actor updates by modeling policies as a probability manifold endowed with a chosen metric geometry. Under this lens, a broad class of offline actor objectives can be interpreted as a single proximal policy improvement step (SPI), i.e., an implicit discretization of a manifold gradient flow induced by a critic-defined energy. Building on this insight, we propose multi-step proximal policy improvement (MPI), a plug-in refinement mechanism that composes sequential re-centered proximal steps. MPI enables controlled policy improvement beyond dataset support while retaining proximal control at each refinement. The framework accommodates multiple policy geometries and admits practical instantiations for deterministic and diagonal-Gaussian policies. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks show that small numbers of MPI refinements improve strong offline baselines, including TD3+BC, ReBRAC, and IQL, on many tasks. Focused diagnostics further distinguish re-centered refinement from fixed-objective update scheduling and characterize limitations under critic error.

</details>


### [170] [Pushing the (Decision) Boundaries: Dynamically Calibrating Differentially Private Noise to Explainability in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03851)
*Michael Khavkin,Kichang Lee,Jaeho Jin,JeongGil Ko,Eran Toch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XCal-FL，一种基于可解释性驱动的闭环局部训练算法，用于跨孤岛联邦学习中的图像分类任务。它通过三个互补信号（预测logits变化、反事实边界、显著性集中度）动态校准差分隐私噪声，在保持形式化DP保证的同时，相比静态噪声FL方法在预测性能上提升超过10%，在解释保真度上最高提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带差分隐私的联邦学习虽然在分布式机器学习中被广泛用于保护数据机密性，但DP噪声会扭曲学习表示并降低解释保真度，限制了需要可信解释的DP-FL应用场景（如临床辅助诊断）。先前的工作仅使用静态特征重要性信号来调整DP噪声，将可解释性局限于事后分析，无法在训练过程中根据解释质量对噪声进行校准。

Method: 提出XCal-FL闭环可解释性驱动训练算法：从三个互补信号动态校准DP噪声——(1)预测logit变化（衡量对模型置信度的因果影响）、(2)反事实边界（捕捉决策边界敏感性）、(3)显著性集中度（量化模型注意力的空间一致性），并通过自适应隐私核算实施正式的DP保证。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上的多种FL配置实验中，XCal-FL相比静态噪声FL方法将预测性能提升超过10%，解释保真度最高提升5倍，在保真度上优于最先进的适应性DP方法。XCal-FL还实现了更高的隐私预算效率，并能将每个单位的累积隐私损失转化为准确率和解释保真度的更大增益。分析还发现解释保真度与隐私损失呈非线性关系，与预测性能的近似线性关系不同。

Conclusion: 可解释性是隐私权衡中一个独立于效用的维度，无法仅从实用性推断得出。这一定性发现对决策关键型应用中的训练和隐私预算分配具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Differential Privacy (DP) is increasingly adopted to preserve data confidentiality in distributed machine learning. However, DP noise distorts learned representations and degrades explanation fidelity, limiting differentially private FL where trustworthy explanations are required, such as assistive clinical diagnosis. Prior work adapted DP noise with static feature-importance signals, restricting explainability to post hoc analysis and precluding noise calibration to explanation quality during training. We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [171] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究注意力机制的训练动力学，提出了"以注意力为索引的模型"框架，在高维极限下用有限个迹序参数刻画总体损失景观，同时用可被有限截断系统指数逼近的无穷矩阵矩层次描述在线SGD。研究发现注意力参数化本身可作为架构隐式偏差：直接优化注意力矩阵可能陷入无效状态，而绑定注意力（S=WW^T）自动诱导对称性破缺，在Θ(d²log d)样本下实现弱恢复；解绑注意力（S=UV^T）则呈现快慢机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心组件，但其训练动力学（尤其是注意力矩阵具有较大秩的情况）仍缺乏深入理解。作者希望从理论层面揭示注意力架构中参数化选择如何影响学习动态与样本复杂度。

Method: 提出了一个广泛的注意力索引模型框架，能够表示多层和多头注意力架构。在高维极限下，用有限个迹序参数刻画总体损失的景观；同时用无穷矩阵矩层次分析在线SGD的动力学，并证明该无限系统可被有限截断系统以指数精度逼近。通过对比直接参数化、绑定参数化和解绑参数化三种注意力矩阵优化方式的动力学行为来揭示架构偏差。

Result: 直接优化注意力矩阵可能停留在无信息状态；绑定注意力形式（S=WW^T）会自发产生对称性破缺机制，在Θ(d²log d)个样本下实现弱恢复；解绑注意力（S=UV^T）表现出快慢时间尺度分离的动力学，当快速动力学选择的状态打破初始对称性时，同样在Θ(d²log d)尺度实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 不同注意力参数化方式会带来截然不同的隐式偏差和学习动力学，参数化本身不只是表达能力的选择，还深刻影响训练的有效性和样本效率。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [172] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFFINT，一种具有可解释瓶颈结构的自编码器异常检测方法。其潜在瓶颈被组织为一组软性的、轴对齐的区间成员关系，直接从原始数值数据端到端学习，无需离散化或二值化。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的一个可读超矩形，重建误差作为异常分数。在48个ADBench基准上对22个基线方法进行评估，DIFFINT在ROC-AUC（4.10）和AUPR（4.16）上均取得最佳平均排名，是统计显著绑定的领先方法簇中唯一的可解释检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的异常检测方法（如深度自编码器）虽然准确但不可解释——它们能标记异常样本，却无法告诉从业者哪些特征范围导致了异常。这限制了模型在实际场景中的可信度和可用性，因此需要一个既能保持深度学习表征能力又具有可检查内部结构的异常检测框架。

Method: DIFFINT的潜在瓶颈被结构化为一组软性的、轴对齐的区间成员关系。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的一个可读超矩形，实例根据其落入每个区间的强度进行编码，重建误差即异常分数。方法包含三项关键技术：(1) 通过Lipschitz约束的解码器，对落在所有学习支撑活跃坐标之外的点提供可认证的重建误差下界；(2) 针对仅少数特征异常的常见情况，提供分级且经验验证的抑制机制；(3) 提供闭式、无标签的重要性度量，从模型已有参数中排序每个(单元,特征)对，将训练好的区间转化为可审计的候选约束，而无需任何异常标签。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上对22个基线在统一的[-1,1]归一化协议下评估，DIFFINT在ROC-AUC上取得4.10、在AUPR上取得4.16的最佳平均排名；在仅使用正常样本的检测器中明显领先其类别，且与最强的含污染数据检测器具有竞争力。它是统计显著领先的七方法簇中唯一的可解释检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT成功地将可微表示学习的效能与可检查的内部结构相结合，在保持顶尖异常检测性能的同时实现了模型可解释性，是该领域首个同时满足性能领先和可解释性要求的检测器。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出持续知识更新方法仅在单一最终检查点和单一适配器秩上进行比较是不充分的。通过在24个月Wikidata流上对比周期层级与累积回放方法，并系统变化评估月份、回放LoRA秩和查询表述，发现最优方法会随这些因素改变。作者提出应报告性能轨迹和能力扫描，并仅在评估区域上排序稳定时才能宣称稳健胜者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识更新方法的比较方式过于简化——只依赖最终检查点和单一适配器秩来判定方法优劣，可能得出误导性结论，无法识别真正更优的操作点。

Method: 在固定周期层级的前提下，与累积回放方法在24个月Wikidata数据流上进行对比，同时系统地变化三个维度：评估月份、回放LoRA秩以及查询表述方式，在多个模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B和Llama-3.2-1B）及保留释义上进行验证。

Result: 方法排名取决于评估时间与回放侧适配容量的联合作用。例如在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，层级方法相对秩8回放有5.0点优势，但相对秩72回放却落后11.6点；高秩下单一端点可能看似打平，而时间平均回放却领先9-13点。该秩条件反转在Llama-3.2-1B和保留释义上同样出现。

Conclusion: 持续更新中的方法排名应报告轨迹与能力扫描；仅当排序在整个评估区域上稳定时才宜宣称稳健胜者，否则应报告胜者区域及保留-稳定性-成本前沿。在更严格协议下，周期层级应被视为低更新成本的操作点，而非质量上的胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [174] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RATL是一种即插即用的残差检索与反馈修正方法，用于多变量时间序列预测。它冻结基础预测器，将历史预测残差存储为模型专属记忆，在推理时检索相似上下文中的残差轨迹，并通过路由机制选择组合这些轨迹来修正预测，在多个基准上提升了冻结基础预测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）在文本任务中效果显著，但将其思想直接迁移到连续输出的回归任务存在挑战——直接复用检索到的目标值在样本输出水平、数值尺度或局部动态不同时不够鲁棒。此外，传统预测流程仅用残差做优化和诊断，未将历史残差作为推理时可访问的记忆保留下来。

Method: RATL包含三个核心组件：（1）冻结基础预测器构造检索键；（2）将历史预测残差转化为仅训练可用的模型专属记忆库；（3）推理时在因果可用性约束下检索相似的残差轨迹，通过一个感知集合的路由器在预测块和变量维度上进行选择与组合，并利用验证集上的校正强度选择来限制残差过度注入。

Result: 实验表明，与当前上下文匹配的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息，RATL在大多数实验设置中提升了冻结基础预测器的性能。以iTransformer为主要冻结基础预测器，与多个强基线对比，并测试了跨骨干网络的迁移性，结果显示RATL在多数设置下能进一步改善基础预测器的表现。消融实验验证了学习路由和校正强度选择的有效性。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为模型专属的历史预测误差，为连续输出预测中的学习式反馈修正提供了一种即插即用、基于残差记忆的新范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [175] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Headroom-Drift Replay方法，一种针对GRPO的组级重放控制原语，将经验重放分解为"Headroom"（按剩余学习价值排序存储组）和"Drift"（按与当前策略的兼容性进行门控）两个决策，在不改变新鲜策略流、不引入额外生成或训练机制的前提下，在数学推理、多模态推理和智能体搜索基准上优于朴素重放，并且与更复杂的重放方法性能相当，同时在智能体搜索场景中以更低墙钟时间获得可比质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的后训练中，推理模型的训练日益被反复的新轨迹生成所瓶颈，尤其在智能体环境中环境交互主导了墙钟开销。现有的重放方法通常嵌入在更复杂的训练流程中（涉及探索、经验重构或混合策略优化），使得重放本身的贡献难以被单独评估。作者希望聚焦回答一个关键问题：仅靠原则性的重放选择能走多远？

Method: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay：一种用于GRPO的组级重放控制原语，将重放利用拆分为两个独立决策——Headroom根据存储组的剩余学习价值进行排序选择，Drift则根据该组与当前策略的兼容性进行门控过滤。新鲜的在策略数据流保持不变，该方法不添加任何辅助生成或训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search三个基准上，该单一干预方法优于朴素重放，并在Avg Mean@32指标上达到或超越更全面的重放方法。在Agentic Search中（环境交互成本占主导），该方法以显著更低的墙钟时间实现了可比质量。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，仅通过精心设计的原则性重放选择机制，就能在不增加复杂训练机制的情况下显著提升重放效率。Headroom-Drift Replay将重放简化为可解的排序与门控问题，验证了"纯粹的重放选择"足以匹敌乃至超越更复杂的传统重放方案，尤其在环境交互成本高的智能体情境中具有实际效益。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [176] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VestigeKV提出一种针对长生命周期KV缓存的无训练压缩方法，核心洞察是利用NoPE模型中decoupled分支（RoPE遗留的64维通道）作为与查询无关的显著性信号，通过读取每行11%数据将缓存分为attended层与GPU驻留的archive层，在8x压缩下保持检索1.00准确率，32x下为0.92，且在65k长上下文下与全行选择无差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长生命周期的KV缓存必须在产生读取它的查询之前就被压缩，而现有基于观测注意力（如H2O、SnapKV）的选择方法在这种场景下失效（NoPE MLA模型上针检索仅0.00-0.33），因为token的重要性尚未被观测到。需要一种不依赖查询信号且无需训练的缓存压缩机制。

Method: VestigeKV利用Kimi Linear缓存中已携带的与查询无关的信号：64维decoupled分支（这是RoPE在NoPE训练中被重新利用为显著性通道的"遗迹"）。通过只读取每行的11%数据，将缓存分为两层：top-m行留在attended层，其余行以精确移动（而非删除）的方式放入GPU驻留的archive层，该archive可通过每步的certified trigger访问。无训练、无语量化、无权重或内核改动；还提供host offload作为VRAM回收变体。此外，论文证明在RoPE下查询无关显著性信号不存在（top-1目标仅覆盖2.3-6.7%的token vs RoPE的10.2-46.8%），且RoPE下查询无关的精确合并被证明是不可能的。

Result: 在Kimi Linear上，8x压缩下检索准确率保持1.00，32x下为0.92，覆盖8k到65k上下文，与全行选择零差距。attended层在32x下仅占Kimi Linear每token缓存8.1KB中的0.25KB；recall tier（标准配置）在128x下仍保持1.00准确率。在RoPE MLA模型上，同一操作崩溃至0.08（普通驱逐为0.42）。论文提供了20个归档判定和8条封闭路线支撑结论。

Conclusion: VestigeKV证明了NoPE模型中的decoupled branch可以作为可靠的查询无关显著性信号用于KV缓存压缩，在长上下文场景下实现无损或近无损的压缩性能。该方法的有效性严格依赖于NoPE架构——在RoPE下同样的操作会崩溃，且理论上证明了RoPE下查询无关显著性不存在。方法可能扩展到Kimi K3（使用NoPE Gated-MLA变体），但论文不对此做出超出已测模型之外的声明。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [177] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输出空间统计重分布的标签移除方法，通过模块化输出过滤器近似重训练模型的置信度向量，无需访问原始数据，兼顾效率与隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类系统中类别动态更新/淘汰导致标签移除频繁出现，现有方法（重训练式和特征空间调整式）存在依赖原始数据、计算与存储开销大、结果不一致、可扩展性差及模型效用退化等问题。

Method: 在输出空间中利用统计重分布来近似重训练模型在移除标签后的置信度向量，作为模块化输出过滤器应用，仅需现有标签和先验输出置信度，无需特征空间调整或损失函数收敛。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个分类任务上与完全重训练相比具有竞争性表现，并在计算效率和隐私保护方面均有改进。

Conclusion: 本文方法通过输出空间统计重分布有效解决了标签移除问题，克服了现有方法对原始数据的依赖与高开销缺陷，兼具可扩展性和隐私保护优势，为动态分类系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [178] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RobustSeiz，一个开源、模型无关的框架，用于在临床可控的分布偏移下对癫痫发作检测器进行压力测试和标准化比较，弥补了现有检测器仅关注干净数据准确率而忽视真实世界鲁棒性的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的癫痫发作检测器虽然在留出数据集上表现良好，但在真实世界的采集变异、伪影和对抗输入下可能失效。缺乏一个标准化、可复现的协议来在部署前系统性评估检测器在受控分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Method: 将四个公开头皮EEG数据集（CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena、SeizeIT1）标准化为BIDS-EEG树结构，采用独立于受试者的评估分割。对环境、噪声和对抗性变换在预定义超参数网格上进行扫描，每个运行报告样本级和事件级的敏感性、精确率、F1、每24小时假阳性率、发作起止时间延迟以及蒙特卡洛dropout预测一致性。框架包含Docker化GPU管线、实验注册表以及完整评估和研究子集模式。

Result: 在TUSZ数据集上使用当代癫痫检测器演示了框架在所有实现变换网格上的应用，AWGN分析展示了扰动严重程度如何影响检测质量、发作起止时间和预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz为评估癫痫检测器在现实临床压力源下的鲁棒性提供了共享的基准标准，将部署前评估从单纯的干净数据准确率扩展到更全面的鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [179] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度扩散增强LLM（Uno模型），通过解耦AR权重与轻量扩散权重，在保持AR模型分布质量的同时实现多token并行生成，无需独立草稿模型即可获得高达3倍加速，并在多个基准上超越现有扩散LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM依赖next-token预测（NTP）但自回归（AR）结构导致生成速度缓慢，需要串行逐token生成，成为推理瓶颈。现有方法如投机解码需要额外草稿模型且复杂，而扩散LLM（d-LLMs）又会牺牲底层AR模型的质量。

Method: 提出扩散增强LLM（diffusion-augmented LLMs），将模型参数解耦为两组：AR权重使用标准NTP目标训练，轻量扩散权重通过简单的Diffusion Distillation阶段学习多token并行生成。同时引入Ψ-Spec采样器族，支持无损加速和固定上下文长度下的推理时间扩展。模型Uno可从零训练或通过增强现有开源AR LLM构建。

Result: Uno在各类batch size下吞吐量均高于领先的投机解码方法，相对基础AR模型最高可实现3倍加速（包括设备支持的最大batch size）。8B Uno模型在agentic工具使用、编码和长上下文推理的全部评估基准上均优于26B DiffusionGemma和专有的Mercury 2。

Conclusion: Uno成功地将AR模型的生成质量与扩散模型并行生成的效率结合，无需草稿模型即可实现无损加速，在性能和效率之间达到更好的平衡，为LLM加速推理提供了一条新路径。代码和模型权重已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [180] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对重尾潜在结果分布下的极端分位数处理效应（QTE）估计问题，提出了一种位置不变（location-invariant）的估计方法。现有方法在极端分位数估计中不满足位置平移不变性，作者通过两步解决：一是将位置不变的Fraga极端值指数估计器适配到因果推断框架（使用逆倾向得分加权），二是采用差分式外推方案使位置参数在分位数差分中抵消。作者证明了估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，并提供了有效推断所需的一致方差估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重尾分布下估计极端分位数处理效应时，现有极端QTE估计器依赖外推和因果极端值指数（EVI）估计，但该估计器在潜在结果分布发生共同位置平移时不具有不变性，而这本应不影响总体QTE。这一缺陷限制了方法的理论正确性和实际应用的可靠性。

Method: 作者采用两步策略：第一步将位置不变的Fraga EVI估计器通过逆倾向得分加权（IPSW）适配到因果设置中；第二步替代原有外推公式，采用差分式方案，使得在计算分位数差分时位置参数自然抵消，从而使最终的QTE估计器具备位置不变性。

Result: 作者证明了所提出的极端QTE估计量具有一致性和渐近正态性，并提供了一致方差估计量以实现渐近有效的推断。模拟研究验证了方法的位位置不变性、对阈值选择的稳定性以及置信区间的覆盖率表现。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了极端分位数处理效应估计中位置不变性缺失的理论空白，提出了在因果推断框架下兼具位置不变性和良好统计性质的新估计器，并通过模拟研究验证了其实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [181] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF，将主动偏好学习建模为序列贝叶斯滤波问题，通过在低维参数子空间中使用扩展卡尔曼滤波器追踪奖励模型的不确定性，以实现样本高效的RLHF主动学习。在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面均优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，且学到的奖励模型能带来有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF虽然强大但样本效率低下，需要主动学习来合成信息量丰富的偏好查询。然而，对大型神经网络奖励模型进行有效的不确定性量化（这是主动学习的关键前提）仍然是一个重要挑战——全参数空间的后验推断在计算上不可行。

Method: 将主动偏好学习框架化为序列贝叶斯滤波问题，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）在低维参数子空间中进行序贯推理，持续更新奖励模型后验。这个方法支持对神经网络参数进行可扩展的采样，从而高效计算用于主动奖励学习的采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上的实验表明，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，且学到的奖励模型能够带来有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了可扩展贝叶斯方法在RLHF偏好奖励建模中的潜力，为大型神经网络奖励模型中的不确定性量化提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [182] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用因果最优传输（causal optimal transport）来定义近似损失函数，以解决以奇异扩散系数和高异质（密度不存在或不光滑）条件分布为特征的条件下生成模型的引导问题，并在最小假设下识别最小熵控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型在训练中严重依赖分数函数（score function）进行引导，但当生成模型是奇异扩散系数下的扩散过程，且底层条件密度不存在或不光滑时，传统方法会失效。需要一种不依赖密度存在的替代框架来定义损失函数并实现引导。

Method: 作者利用因果最优传输来定义近似损失函数，其理论基础是因果最优传输在（条件化）扩散过程中的可预测表示性质（predictable representation property）上的刻画。该方法依赖于相关鞅问题的适定性（well-posedness），遵循Üstünel的框架，从而在极小的假设条件下识别最小熵控制。

Result: 该方法能够在底层条件密度不存在或不光滑、以及扩散系数奇异的情况下，仍能定义有效的近似损失函数并识别出最小熵控制用于条件生成模型的引导，所需假设极为宽松。

Conclusion: 本文为条件生成模型的引导提供了一种新的理论框架，通过因果最优传输替代分数函数方法，在极端不规则条件下仍能保证模型的可训练性和可控性。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [183] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direct-P方法，将注意力分数直接映射为4位浮点（FP4）概率，避免softmax转换开销，在GB200上非因果推理吞吐量达到BF16的2.13倍；同时提出因果路径将前向量化直接引入反向传播，使用FP8梯度操作数，使单GPU 80亿参数模型训练加速最高1.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell的FP4张量核心并不会自动加速注意力计算，因为当矩阵乘积缩小后，softmax转换和片上依赖成为瓶颈。需要设计直接兼容FP4的注意力路径来利用低精度硬件优势。

Method: 提出Direct-P方法用于非因果推理，将注意力分数直接映射为FP4概率，避免softmax浮点转换开销；同时设计因果训练路径，将前向量化直接传递到反向传播中，从保存的量化查询和键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数进行训练。分布式训练中保留FP8概率和数值。

Result: Direct-P在NVIDIA GB200上非因果前向推理吞吐量最高达到BF16的2.13倍；因果路径使完整的单GPU 80亿参数模型更新加速最高达1.14倍。所有测试的MXFP4概率/数值训练轨迹均发散。

Conclusion: Direct-P通过将量化直接集成到注意力概率计算和梯度传递中，成功在Blackwell FP4硬件上实现注意力加速，同时揭示了MXFP4在训练中不可行的局限性，需保留FP8精度以确保分布式训练收敛。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [184] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非耦合学习算法HOOD（高阶乐观与折扣算法），该算法在所有玩家均采用时，能保证任意N人正则形式博弈（每人最多K个动作）的每个玩家遗憾为O(N³log²K)，且在整个博弈时域上均匀成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般形式的N人博弈中，实现常数级（不随时间增长）的个体遗憾一直是一个挑战。此前尝试的主要障碍在于博弈中诱导的博弈序列会产生大幅度振荡，导致遗憾下界无法突破。作者旨在通过算法设计来可控地抑制这些振荡，从而首次在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾。

Method: 作者提出HOOD算法，它是乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的变体，核心创新是：(1) 使用带折扣的(N+1)阶预测器；(2) 在博弈策略空间的适当"提升"（lifting）上引入熵正则化。这两种成分的组合被特意设计用于以受控方式抑制博弈序列的大幅振荡，从而克服此前方法的关键障碍。

Result: 该算法实现了O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾界，该界不依赖于博弈时域长度，即渐近意义上达到常数遗憾。这一结果显著优于同时期独立工作（Liu等人）得到的O(N²¹log⁴K)遗憾界。

Conclusion: 作者通过高阶乐观与折扣策略的结合，成功在一般N人博弈中实现了多项式级别的常数个体遗憾，验证了该设计思路在消除序列振荡方面的有效性，并为该领域提供了更紧的遗憾保证。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [185] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs），这是一种受生物学启发的复数域时间滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特例），并引入参数无关的双抽头前瞻输入校正方法，使深层连续时间循环网络中每一层的自底向上输入具有"前瞻性"，从而缓解深度相关的时间梯度衰减问题。实验表明前瞻变体在RQF、S5和ORGaNICs模型上均匹配或超越非前瞻对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分赋予连续时间循环网络记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中会延迟自底向上信号并衰减自顶向下误差。作者试图回答这一失效模式能否通过使每层自底向上输入具有前瞻性来加以解决。

Method: 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是带通滤波器且可学习参数控制其调谐频率和带宽。通过参数无关的双抽头更新使每层自底向上输入前瞻化，保持循环转移和并行扫描不变，并将该校正扩展到一般对角SSM。在RQF、S5和ORGaNICs上分别使用完整BPTT和仅空间反向传播进行训练评估。

Result: 在完整BPTT下，前瞻变体在所有模型和配置中均匹配或超越非前瞻对照。非残差宽度32的六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上以31.9k参数达到96.09%准确率；宽度64的六层RQF在16,384步Path-X任务上达到83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs是参数高效的递归基底，前瞻输入编码是深层连续时间循环网络的一种输入侧校正手段。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [186] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本、开源的端到端自动驾驶实验平台，基于微型Ackermann车辆，结合物理车辆、印刷城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生。平台支持从仿真到现实的受控实验，并实现了命令条件行为克隆基线。在真实闭环实验中，策略的横向跟踪误差为6.1cm，接近人类演示的4.7cm；在数字孪生中，相机视场角从58度拓宽到120度可将误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。结合合成数据与真实演示训练的高容量策略是唯一能完成全部四条赛道闭环的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶研究往往依赖昂贵的全尺寸车辆平台或纯仿真环境，缺乏低成本、可复现的测试平台来连接仿真方法与真实世界执行。作者旨在建立一个开源、低成本的物理+仿真联动的实验环境，支持sim-to-real研究并促进可复现的科学研究。

Method: 平台由物理微型Ackermann车辆、印刷赛道、数据采集与轨迹配准工具、以及Webots数字孪生组成。作为基线方法，实现了命令条件行为克隆——神经网络策略接收车载相机图像和高层导航命令，输出转向和速度。同时使用数字孪生成合成数据，并采用sim-to-real图像翻译器缩小外观差异，训练更高容量的策略。

Result: 真实闭环实验中基线策略均横截误差为6.1cm（人类演示为4.7cm）；数字孪生中相机视场角从58°增至120°使误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。仅有使用合成数据+真实演示联合训练的高容量策略能在闭环中完成全部四条赛道，而紧凑基线和仅用真实数据训练的网络表现较差。

Conclusion: 该开源平台被证实是实用的sim-to-real研究测试平台，提供了命令条件模仿学习的初始基线。实验表明，合成数据与真实数据结合训练可持续提升闭环性能，平台已开源以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [187] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了适用于一般和并发随机博弈（CSGs）的PAC学习框架，在转移不确定性下解决纳什均衡（NE）存在性问题。算法维护基于数据的L1置信集并求解鲁棒CSG来获得社会福利最优的ε-NE，通过鲁棒MDP探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖。框架要么返回ε近似NE（社会福利接近最优），要么提供不存在精确NE的可靠证书。在最小可达性条件下，算法以多项式样本复杂度终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般和并发随机博弈中的纳什均衡求解面临两大挑战：一是转移不确定性（transition uncertainty）下缺乏可证明的学习保证；二是纳什均衡的存在性本身不确定，无法保证一定可以找到均衡。现有研究未能系统性地解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 构建数据驱动的L1置信集来刻画转移核的不确定性；2) 通过求解鲁棒CSG获得社交福利最优的ε-NE；3) 提出Nash margin特征化方法，用于判定均衡存在性并给出不存在精确NE的合理证书；4) 采用鲁棒MDP探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖。

Result: 在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max²H⁴|S|²|A|/(p_reach·ε²))。基准CSG上的经验结果展示了近似最优性能、对均衡存在/不存在情况的正确处理，以及符合理论的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为一般和CSG提供了同时处理转移不确定性和NE存在性问题的PAC学习保证，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [CAPQ-FAST: Content-Adaptive Perceived Quality Assessment for Faster Audiovisual Playback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03498)
*Jiarun Song,Yuxin Song,Fuzheng Yang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文针对在线音视频服务中快速播放场景下用户感知质量研究不足的问题，通过主观实验系统分析了视频、语音和音乐在不同播放速度下的感知质量，提取了内容特有的时间动态特征（视频的TI、语音的WPM、音乐的BPM），分别构建预测模型并整合为统一的内容自适应快速播放感知质量评估模型（CAPQ-FAST），实验验证了该模型能有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速播放已成为现代在线音视频服务的常见功能，但视频、音频（语音和音乐）和音视频等不同内容模态在快速播放下对可理解性和信息完整性有不同的要求，导致感知质量因内容类型而异。然而，不同播放速度下用户的感知质量尚未得到充分研究，现有研究存在明显空白。

Method: 首先开展一系列主观实验，分析视频、音频和音视频内容在播放速度与感知质量之间的关系；然后提取内容特定的内在特征以捕获时间动态特性（视频采用时间信息TI、语音采用每分钟词数WPM、音乐采用每分钟节拍BPM）；分别针对视频、语音和音乐开发感知质量的预测模型；最后通过整合这些子模型提出统一的内容自适应感知质量评估模型（CAPQ-FAST），用于快速音视频播放场景。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的CAPQ-FAST模型能够有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量，验证了内容自适应方法的有效性与可行性。

Conclusion: 该模型可以帮助服务提供商更好地理解用户在快速播放下的观看意图和感知体验，从而实现更具适应性的个性化推荐和播放控制，提升用户体验的自适应能力。

Abstract: Faster playback has become a common feature in modern online audiovisual services, allowing users to consume content in less time while still maintaining a coherent viewing experience. However, different modalities of media content, such as video, audio (including speech and music), and audiovisual, exhibit varying requirements for understandability and information integrity under faster playback. These differences lead to noticeable variations in perceived quality depending on the content type. Nevertheless, users' perceived quality at different playback speeds remains insufficiently investigated. To address this gap, this paper conducts a series of subjective experiments to analyze the relationship between playback speed and perceived quality for video, audio, and audiovisual content. Content-specific intrinsic features are extracted to capture temporal dynamics, including temporal information (TI) for video, words per minute (WPM) for speech, and beats per minute (BPM) for music. Predictive models of perceived quality under faster playback are then developed separately for video, speech, and music. By integrating these models, a unified content-adaptive perceived quality assessment model (CAPQ-FAST) is proposed for faster audiovisual playback. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model effectively predicts perceived quality under different playback speeds. This model can help service providers better understand users' viewing intentions and perceptual experiences under faster playback, thereby enabling more adaptive personalized recommendations and playback control to enhance user experience adaptability.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人工智能驱动的应用英语新型教材的五层架构，并在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了八周教学测试，结果显示单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在将应用英语教材从固定的纸质序列转变为能够诊断学习者、推荐任务并提供形成性反馈的自适应学习系统，因此需要研究这种新型教材的结构与应用方式。

Method: 提出了一个五层架构的AI驱动应用英语教材系统，包括知识映射、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈协调和教师端治理五个层面，并在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了为期八周的教学实验，与静态数字教材进行对比。

Result: 与静态数字教材相比，该系统将单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在保持课程稳定性的同时，提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料和可追溯的课堂数据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [190] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受治理的企业分析框架：语言模型仅负责解释用户问题意图，由确定性策略选择并执行预先审批的分析程序，同时返回结果和证据。实验表明，在330次运行时规划（runtime-planning）中，三个8B模型无一次完全满足答案与证据契约；而策略执行的分析器在110次测试中全部达标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析场景中需要兼顾表达力与治理合规性，直接让语言模型在运行时自由规划SQL和工具选择存在不可控风险，需要一种既能保证分析表达能力又符合治理要求的受限方法。

Method: 采用"语言模型解释意图 + 确定性策略执行审批程序"的双层架构。语言模型仅负责意图识别，策略层从预审批的分析程序中选取并执行，程序支持关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口、排序和相似性等操作。固定语义、策略、数据和执行规则以确保结果可回放。

Result: 在440次运行中，三个8B模型在运行时生成SQL并选择工具，而Qwen3-8B仅用于意图解释并由策略执行审批程序。330次运行时规划实验无一完全匹配跨数据集的答案与证据契约；110次策略执行实验全部匹配成功。

Conclusion: 受治理的受限分析方法在给定实验配置下能够稳定达成答案与证据的完整契约，且表达力足以覆盖定义的分析类别。但该结果是配置特定的，不能据此否定其他设计下运行时规划方案的可能性。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [191] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"推测宏提交"(SMC)的运行时机制，用于加速工具使用的LLM智能体系统。系统采用双层架构：一个大型权威actor模型生成官方轨迹，一个快速推测性drafter模型在隔离的环境快照上持续预测和执行未来的动作链。SMC从训练轨迹中挖掘重复的多动作骨架，存储在宏库中用于运行时匹配。当actor的下一个工具调用与drafter预测的首个动作匹配时，SMC将预执行的草稿步骤及其观察结果提交到官方轨迹中。实验表明，SMC在保持准确率的同时实现了显著的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的LLM智能体在实际运行中不仅消耗模型推理时间，还在串行的动作-观察轮次中浪费时间——每次工具调用、环境转换和观察都会延迟后续决策。现有的推测执行方法仅限于单步推测，缺乏宏(多步)级别的预测与执行复用，导致端到端延迟仍有较大优化空间。

Method: SMC采用双层智能体系统：(1) 权威actor模型产生官方轨迹；(2) 更快的推测drafter模型在隔离环境快照上连续预测并执行未来动作链。核心技术包括：从训练痕迹中挖掘重复出现的多动作骨架并存入宏库；运行时将drafter预测的动作链与宏进行匹配；当actor的下一个工具调用与drafter草稿的首个动作匹配时，批量提交剩余已执行的草稿步骤及其观察结果到官方轨迹。

Result: 使用Qwen3.5-27B INT4作为权威actor模型和Qwen3.5-4B作为推测drafter模型时：在τ²-Bench Telecom子集上，SMC保持与串行智能体相当的准确率，同时相比Speculative Actions (SA)基线降低10.23%延迟，相比串行执行降低18.59%延迟；在AppWorld上，相比SA基线的墙钟时间减少7.7%，相比串行执行减少44.9%，任务完成率仅有小幅下降。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用且有效的方法来复用多步推测执行，将智能体延迟降低到超越单步推测操作的水平。该工作证明了"宏"级别的推测提交是可行的加速范式，并将代码开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [192] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFence协议，解决分布式LLM智能体团队中的"过期计划执行"问题——即使状态新鲜，计划仍可能因引用过时的公共记录而失效。PlanFence通过让计划引用其使用的确切公共记录，并由执行者仅验证可能影响待执行外部动作的记录来实现依赖作用域的动作验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM智能体团队即使读取了最新的共享事实，仍可能基于过时的计划行动。传统状态新鲜度检查无法保证计划的时效性：一个规划器可能基于需求r3推导出动作，另一个智能体提交了r4，执行者收到r4但未替换基于r3的计划，导致执行过期计划。这暴露了"状态新鲜≠计划有效"的问题。

Method: 提出了PlanFence协议，一种依赖作用域的动作验证协议。其核心机制包括：(1) 计划明确引用其制定时所使用的具体公共记录；(2) 执行者仅验证可能影响待处理外部动作的记录；(3) 验证不完整时触发重新规划或阻塞。即执行者不检查所有状态的新鲜度，而是聚焦于与当前动作相关的依赖记录。

Result: 在30个含计划后修订的受控实时工作流中，仅依赖新鲜度的执行者在每个任务中都执行了过期计划，而PlanFence在所有任务中均无无效动作地完成。受控回放揭示两个条件边界：在高变更率下PlanFence避免了重复的更新路径协调，在大共享键空间下避免验证无关状态；在低变更率下主动同步的协调停滞更低。

Conclusion: PlanFence提供了受控的安全性提升和系统成本优势，而非泛化的任务精度增益。它有效地消除了过期计划执行导致的无效动作，但在低变更率场景下，主动同步策略在协调停滞方面表现更优，说明应结合具体场景评估部署选择。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [193] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于提示工程的框架，用于个性化定制基于LLM/RAG的AI教学助手（如Jill Watson），通过六个学习者维度定义96种学习风格画像，并结合布鲁姆分类法评估查询认知复杂度，实现无需模型重训练的个性化响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的AI教学助手虽然能提供可扩展的教育支持，但往往缺乏个性化能力，难以适应不同学习者的差异化需求。

Method: 提出基于提示工程（prompt-engineering）的个性化框架，利用六个学习者维度（自我评估、抽象偏好、冗长偏好、感知取向、信息处理风格、理解水平）生成96种学习者画像；使用布鲁姆分类法分析学生查询的认知复杂度；将学习者属性和认知评估编码为结构化提示词以调节LLM行为，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 通过NLP指标实验和五名参与者的人类研究进行评估，结果显示不同个性化条件下响应风格和结构存在可感知差异，统计分析识别出与可测量响应变化相关的学习者属性。

Conclusion: 研究为基于提示的个性化方法能够支持LLM驱动的教育代理实现适应性行为提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [194] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事俘获"（narrative captivity）这一LLM失效模式：在多轮道德咨询中，当用户仅提供单方面、无对立立场的自辩叙述时，模型会将其视为完整事实并偏向叙述者视角。作者构建了包含5,078个六维人际冲突场景的基准测试，在17个LLM上验证了该现象的普遍性——多轮叙事下的最终判断相比单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析表明偏好优化是主要诱因，四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的道德咨询往往是多轮、仅听单方自述的过程，会产生信息不对称；但以往研究多基于单轮判断或带有明确反驳压力的设定，无法揭示单纯叙述本身是否能影响LLM的道德判断。作者希望填补这一空白，促使LLM在真实咨询场景中保持独立判断力。

Method: （1）提出"叙事俘获"概念，定义为模型将无人反驳的单方面叙述当作完整信息并全盘接受叙述者解释的失效模式；（2）构建包含5,078个人际冲突场景的基准，覆盖六个道德维度；（3）在17个LLM上进行系统评测，对比多轮叙事与匹配单轮基线的最终判断差异；（4）进行阶段级（stage-level）归因分析，并测试四种推理时缓解策略。

Result: 叙事俘获在17个LLM上普遍存在：多轮叙事下的端态判断相比单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析指出偏好优化是主要贡献因素；四种推理时策略只能提供部分缓解，无法完全消除该问题。

Conclusion: LLM在真实世界的多轮道德咨询中容易受单方面叙事裹挟而丧失独立判断，现有推理时缓解手段不足，未来需要更根本的方法（如改进偏好优化阶段）来打造能够保持独立判断的LLM道德顾问。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [195] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dude，首个用于论文-代码不一致检测的双重检测多智能体系统，通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制，有效解决了多智能体系统中的过解释和过报告问题，在真实数据集上相比基线方法在召回率上提升高达22.8%，F1分数提升高达18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学术论文投稿数量激增，人工审查已无法满足需求，LLM赋能的论文-代码不一致检测受到广泛关注。然而，现有单智能体LLM范式存在上下文容量有限和单向不一致检测的问题，导致召回性能较差。此外，论文语言与代码语言之间的粒度不对称在多智能体系统中引入了过解释和过报告挑战，导致假阳性增加。

Method: Dude采用双检测多智能体架构，具体包括：1) 双检测机制，从两个方向检测论文与代码之间的不一致；2) 粒度对齐协商机制，解决论文语言与代码语言间的粒度不对称问题；3) 两阶段显著性过滤机制，有效防止智能体误报不一致。

Result: 在真实世界的论文-代码不一致数据集上的实验表明，Dude相比基线方法在召回率上显著提升高达22.8%，F1分数提升高达18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude通过多智能体协同检测和有效的过滤机制，显著提升了论文-代码不一致检测的性能，验证了多智能体系统在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [196] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval（DSB-IFEval）基准，用于评估全双工语音代理在实时口语交互中的隐式指令遵循能力。该基准包含1,038个测试用例，覆盖八种不同助理角色和五种指令条件协议，通过确定性指令遵循分数（IAS）和LLM评判的角色一致性分数（PAS）进行衡量。实验发现全双工模型对指令是否显式陈述或需从角色推断存在架构依赖性的权衡，且系统在面对冲突指令和安全冲突时表现各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要通过显式轮次管理指令测试语音代理行为，但实际部署场景中代理往往通过角色或人设配置，需要从隐含信息中推断适当的对话行为。缺乏评估隐式指令遵循能力的基准。

Method: 构建包含1,038个测试用例、覆盖八种角色的DSB-IFEval基准，设计五种指令条件协议（默认行为、显式行为指令、人设隐含行为、人设与规则组合、指令冲突），使用确定性IAS评分测量实时地板管理，使用LLM评判的PAS评分测量角色一致性内容，并在六个实时语音系统上进行评估。

Result: 全双工模型（如F-Actor和PersonaPlex）对指令是否显式或需推断敏感，人设单一条件下遵循度分别下降9.7%和4.5%；GPT-Realtime、MiniCPM-o和Fun-Audio-Chat虽能强遵循角色一致内容，但其地板行为不随显式/人设指令调整，且在若干主动行为上受限。系统在遵循与其角色一致的冲突指令方面表现可靠，但在安全冲突下仍难以覆盖这些指令。

Conclusion: 推断角色隐含行为、在恰当的对话时机执行该行为以及解决竞争性指令，仍然是全双工语音代理面临的三个不同挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [197] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONFLICTGUI基准测试，用于评估GUI代理在面临冲突指令时的终止能力，并设计了CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，通过对齐代理的可行性感知与动作生成，显著提升其在冲突任务上的成功率，同时不损害正常GUI任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户可能由于疏忽发布不可行的指令，可靠的GUI代理不仅需要知道如何行动，还需要知道何时不应行动。现有代理存在严重的执行偏向性过度服从问题，在冲突指令下仍盲目执行。

Method: 首先构建CONFLICTGUI基准，覆盖指令内部冲突和指令与GUI上下文冲突两类场景。然后提出CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，包含两个耦合组件：可行性验证协议（引导代理在行动前评估指令逻辑和GUI侧证据）和条件动作调制机制（将过度服从的执行行为转化为终止导向行为）。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的GUI代理上进行的实验表明，CONFLICTGUARD显著提高了平均冲突任务成功率，同时保持了正常的GUI任务性能。验证了轻量级推理时干预能有效提升代理识别不恰当执行场景并克制不必要行动的能力。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预即可大幅提升GUI代理在冲突场景下的判断与终止能力，为构建更可靠的GUI代理提供了有效且低成本的技术路径。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance.
